### [1] [Bringing closure to theory combination properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30762)
*Guilherme V. Toledo,Benjamin Przybocki,Yoni Zohar*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究了稳定无穷性、温和性（gentleness）和闪亮性（shininess，对可判定理论等价于强礼貌性）这三种经典组合性质在交集和可组合性下的闭包。通过计算所有可能的交集，再计算每个交集所能组合的最大理论集，迭代该过程直到不再产生新集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索三种经典组合性质（稳定无穷性、温和性、闪亮性）在交集与可组合性操作下的闭包结构，理解这些性质之间的逻辑关系和组合界限。

Method: 从一个初始的性质集合出发，反复计算所有可能的交集，以及每个交集所能组合的最大理论集，迭代直到收敛（不再产生新的集合）。

Result: 通过迭代过程，最终会收敛到有限个不同的性质集合。

Conclusion: 最终得到的性质集合数量取决于三种性质在交集和可组合性下的代数结构，具体数量需要通过完整的迭代计算得出（从摘要本身无法直接得出最终数字）。

Abstract: We consider the closure of three classical combination properties, namely, stable infiniteness, gentleness and shininess (or, equivalently for decidable theories, strong politeness), under intersection and combinability. We compute every possible intersection, and then compute the maximal set of theories that can be combined with each resulting intersection. We iterate this process until no new sets are identified. How many properties will we end up with?

</details>


### [2] [On first-order definable operations on relational structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31260)
*Bruno Courcelle*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了关系结构上的一阶可定义单元操作（FO- transduction）和基于不相交并集与笛卡尔积的二元操作，重点探讨了反向翻译定理和分裂定理，这些定理允许将输出结构的FO性质表示为有限个输入结构FO性质。在量化自由（QF）公式定义的操作中，所得语句的量词高度不会超过输入语句，因此FO语句的有限模型类在QF操作下是可识别的。可识别性对于有界树宽或团宽的结构具有基于有限树自动机的有趣算法性质。研究还扩展到了带模数计数存在量词的FO语句。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统化研究关系结构上FO可定义操作（包括单元操作和二元操作）的定义和主要性质，特别关注反向翻译定理和分裂定理，为理解这些操作如何保持FO可定义性提供理论基础。

Method: 通过形式化定义FO-transduction（单元操作）和基于不相交并集与笛卡尔积的二元操作，分析其反向翻译定理和分裂定理。特别考察量化自由（QF）公式定义的操作，并扩展到模数计数存在量词的情形。

Result: QF操作下，FO语句的量词高度不会增加；有限模型类在QF操作下是可识别的；对于有界树宽或团宽的结构，可识别性可通过有限树自动机实现算法化；这些结果可扩展到带模数计数存在量词的FO语句。

Conclusion: FO可定义操作（尤其是QF操作）具有良好的性质，使得FO语句的模型类在这些操作下可识别，进而为有界树宽或团宽的结构提供基于自动机的有效算法框架，并可推广到模数计数逻辑。

Abstract: We survey the definitions and main properties of first-order (FO) definable unary operations on relational structures, called FO-transductions, and of FO-definable binary operations based on disjoint union and Cartesian product. We focus our study on Backwards Translation Theorems and Splitting Theorems that permit to express FO properties of output structures in terms of finitely many FO properties of the corresponding input ones.
  In the particular cases where the operations are defined by quantifier-free (QF) formulas, the quantifier-heights of the obtained sentences are no larger than those of the input ones. It follows that the class of finite models of a FO sentence is recognizable with respect to the considered QF operations. Recognizability has interesting algorithmic properties based on finite automata on terms, for structures having bounded tree-width or clique-width.
  We extend our results to FO sentences constructed with modulo counting existential quantifiers.

</details>


### [3] [Aspects of Coherence in Dependence Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31269)
*Timon Barlag,Nicolas Fröhlich,Miika Hannula,Phokion G. Kolaitis,Juha Kontinen,Arne Meier,Jouko Väänänen*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究了依赖逻辑中k-相干性的结构与算法方面。主要发现：(1) 对于无量词依赖逻辑公式(DQF)，一阶可改写性等价于k-相干性；(2) 在全称量化依赖逻辑公式中，更强的相干性概念下类似结论成立；(3) 判定一般依赖逻辑公式的k-相干性是高度不可判定的，而DQF公式的相干性问题是共递归可枚举的；(4) 命题依赖逻辑公式的相干性问题是指数层级第二层完全的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 依赖逻辑的模型检验问题即使是对于无量词公式(DQF)也是NP完全的，因此研究者一直寻求使该问题易于处理的条件。Kontinen在2010年引入了k-相干性概念，已被证明k-相干的DQF公式具有良性的模型检验问题（存在一阶改写）。本文旨在深入探究相干性的结构与算法性质，特别是考察相干性与一阶可改写性之间的等价关系，以及判定相干性问题的计算复杂度。

Method: 本文采用形式化逻辑分析方法。首先，证明DQF公式的一阶可改写性蕴含存在某个正整数k使得该公式是k-相干的，从而建立等价关系。其次，将结果推广到全称量化依赖逻辑公式（使用更强的相干性定义）。最后，通过归约方法研究判定问题复杂度：证明一般依赖逻辑公式的相干性问题是高度不可判定的，确定DQF公式相干性问题的精确复杂度层级（共递归可枚举），并定位命题依赖逻辑公式相干性问题的复杂度（指数层级第二层完备）。

Result: (1) 对于DQF公式，一阶可改写性等价于k-相干性（对某个正整数k成立）。(2) 在全称量化依赖逻辑公式中，更强的相干性概念下类似等价关系成立。(3) 一般依赖逻辑公式的k-相干性判定问题是高度不可判定的。(4) DQF公式的k-相干性判定问题是共递归可枚举的。(5) 命题依赖逻辑公式的相干性判定问题是指数层级第二层完备的。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了依赖逻辑中相干性与一阶可改写性之间的关系，证明二者在DQF公式下等价，并将此结果扩展到全称量化情形。同时，本文系统性地刻画了不同片段依赖逻辑公式相干性判定问题的计算复杂度，从高度不可判定到共递归可枚举再到指数层级完备，揭示了相干性概念在不同表达力片段下的计算本质。

Abstract: Dependence logic extends first-order logic with dependence atoms asserting that the value of a variable is determined by the values of certain other variables. The semantics of dependence logic has a second-order character and involves sets of assignments, called teams, instead of individual assignments as in the classical Tarski semantics. Since the model-checking problem is known to be NP-complete even for quantifier-free dependence logic (DQF) formulas, researchers have pursued conditions on formulas that make this problem tractable. In 2010, Jarmo Kontinen introduced the notion of k-coherence for dependence logic formulas, where k is a positive integer. This notion asserts that if the formula is satisfied in a structure by all k-element subteams of a given team, then the given team itself satisfies the formula. It has been proved that k-coherent DQF-formulas have a tame model-checking problem, because such formulas admit a first-order rewriting. In this paper, we investigate the structural and algorithmic aspects of coherence. We show that if a DQF-formula is first-order ewritable, then it is k-coherent for some positive integer k. Thus, for DQF-formulas, coherence is equivalent to first-order rewritability. Furthermore, we show that an analogous result holds for universally quantified dependence logic formulas under a stronger notion of coherence. After this, we focus on the complexity of deciding if a given dependence logic formula is k-coherent. We establish that this decision problem is highly undecidable for arbitrary dependence logic formulas, while for DQF-formulas this problem is co-recursively enumerable. Furthermore, we pinpoint the computational complexity of the coherence problem for propositional dependence logic formulas by showing that this problem is complete for the second level of the exponential hierarchy.

</details>

### [4] [Kernel Foundry: A Diagnosis-driven Evolutionary Kernel Optimizer with Multi-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30359)
*Zixuan Huang,Da Chen,Kecheng Huang,Lihao Yin,Xing Li,Huiling Zhen,Mingxuan Yuan,Zili Shao*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出了Kernel Foundry，一个基于诊断驱动的进化框架，用于自动GPU内核优化。该方法结合专家引导的检索增强初始化与多岛屿进化搜索，通过结构化诊断反馈迭代优化候选内核，并利用中心化经验库积累可复用的优化知识。实验表明在KernelBench上正确性和性能均优于强基线，Level 2上达到100%正确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成高性能GPU内核既需要正确性又需要硬件感知优化，而现有LLM生成的代码往往无法同时满足这两个要求。

Method: 提出Kernel Foundry框架：(1) 专家引导的检索增强初始化；(2) 多岛屿进化搜索；(3) 结构化诊断反馈迭代优化；(4) 中心化经验库积累可复用优化知识；(5) 防止绕过内核计算的作弊行为机制。

Result: 在KernelBench上相较于强基线在正确性和性能上均有一致提升，Level 2任务上实现100%正确率。

Conclusion: 诊断驱动的进化框架能有效生成既正确又高效的GPU内核，结构化反馈和经验积累是关键。

Abstract: Generating high-performance GPU kernels remains challenging due to the need for both correctness and hardware-aware optimization. While large language models (LLMs) show promise in code generation, they often fail to produce kernels that are both correct and efficient.
  We propose Kernel Foundry, a diagnosis-driven evolutionary framework for automatic GPU kernel optimization. Our method combines expert-guided, retrieval-augmented initialization with a multi-island evolutionary search, where candidate kernels are iteratively refined using structured diagnostic feedback. A centralized experience library accumulates reusable optimization knowledge to guide subsequent evolution, while explicit mechanisms prevent cheating behaviors that bypass kernel-level computation.
  Experiments on KernelBench show that our method consistently improves both correctness and performance over strong baselines, achieving up to 100% correctness on Level~2.

</details>


### [5] [Gradient-Free Training of Spiking Neural Networks via Low-Rank Evolution Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30361)
*Dhruv Patankar,Sachit Ramesha Gowda*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出EGGROLL方法，通过低秩分解ES扰动，将SNN训练的内存消耗从O(mn)降至O(r(m+n))，在N-MNIST上实现79.21%测试精度，同时每代耗时减少2.23倍，实现了无梯度、兼容神经形态硬件的SNN训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN因离散脉冲阈值不可微导致训练困难，替代梯度方法依赖反向传播无法适应芯片上学习，而进化策略虽无需梯度但计算成本随参数量线性增长，难以用于大规模权重矩阵。

Method: 提出EGGROLL方法，利用低秩分解对ES扰动进行因子化，将每代内存开销从O(mn)降低到O(r(m+n))；结合Leaky Integrate-and-Fire SNN在N-MNIST数据集上进行无梯度训练。

Result: 在N-MNIST上达到79.21%测试准确率，相比全秩ES，每代计算时间减少2.23倍。

Conclusion: EGGROLL在SNN训练中是可行的，提供明确的精度-速度权衡，且无需替代梯度即可在神经形态硬件上进行训练。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) offer compelling energy efficiency on neuromorphic hardware, yet their training remains challenging because the discrete spike threshold is non-differentiable. Surrogate-gradient methods sidestep this by approximating the derivative, but they impose backpropagation infrastructure that is incompatible with on-chip learning. Evolution Strategies (\es) are a natural gradient-free alternative, yet their computational cost scales with the number of parameters, making them impractical for large weight matrices.
  We present a method for training SNNs using EGGROLL, a low-rank factorisation of ES perturbations that reduces per-generation memory from $\mathcal{O}(mn)$ to $\mathcal{O}(r(m{+}n))$. Combining EGGROLL with a Leaky Integrate-and-Fire SNN on N-MNIST, we demonstrate that gradient-free training achieves 79.21% test accuracy while reducing per-generation wall-clock time by 2.23$\times$ relative to full-rank ES. Our results demonstrate EGGROLL is viable for SNN training, with a clear accuracy-speed tradeoff, compatible with training on neuromorphic hardware without surrogate gradients.

</details>


### [6] [From Mean-Field Limits to Semiclassical Concentration: Global Convergence of the Canonical Evolutionary Strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30371)
*Matías Neto,Nicolás Garay,Luis Martí,Nayat Sanchez-Pi*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了规范进化策略（CES）作为受控数学框架，通过薛定谔型复制子-突变子方程的半经典极限分析进化算法的全局收敛性，建立了从离散个体动力学到确定性平均场极限的严格层次，证明了全局收敛由底层算子的主特征函数控制（即几何选择），自然偏向鲁棒平坦最优点，并在高维基准测试中展示了优于共识驱动和梯度方法的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决随机连续优化中全局收敛的理论问题，尤其是当全局最小点位于初始支撑集之外时，现有共识驱动方法容易出现方差过早崩溃的问题。

Method: 将规范进化策略（CES）建模为受控数学框架，通过薛定谔型复制子-突变子方程的半经典极限分析全局收敛，建立从离散个体动力学到确定性平均场极限的严格层次化推导，定义几何选择（Geometric Selection）性质，利用算子的主特征函数控制收敛行为。

Result: 证明全局收敛由底层算子的主特征函数控制，几何选择机制自然优先选择鲁棒平坦的最优点（"最平坦者生存"）；在d=30的高维移位初始化场景下，CES相比标准共识驱动和梯度方法实现了更低的残差误差，并具备内在的质量传输能力。

Conclusion: 从点态共识转向谱集中（spectral concentration）的视角，为进化策略提供了无需额外数值启发式的稳健全局收敛理论基础。

Abstract: We address the issue of global convergence in stochastic continuous optimization. For that purpose, we formulate the Canonical Evolutionary Strategy (CES) as a controlled mathematical framework to analyze global convergence in evolutionary algorithms via the semiclassical limit of a Schr{ö}dinger-type replicator-mutator equation. We provide a rigorous hierarchy from a discrete individual-based dynamics to a deterministic mean-field limit, demonstrating that global convergence is governed by the principal eigenfunction of the underlying operator. This property, defined as Geometric Selection, naturally prioritizes robust, flat optima over narrow local traps, offering a mathematical justification for the ''survival of the flattest'' phenomenon. Moreover, unlike consensus-driven methods that are prone to premature variance collapse when the global minimizer resides outside the initial support, the replicator-mutator dynamics of CES facilitate intrinsic mass transport. High-dimensional benchmarks (d = 30) confirm this advantage, showing that CES achieves lower residual errors in shifted initialization scenarios where standard consensus-driven and gradient-based methods fail to migrate effectively. By shifting the focus from point-wise consensus to spectral concentration, our framework provides a robust theoretical foundation for global convergence in Evolution Strategies (ES) without the need for additional numerical heuristics.

</details>


### [7] [GP-GOMEA with GPU-Based Fitness Evaluations: Design and Performance Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30954)
*Jasper Post,Johannes Koch,Anton Bouter,Tanja Alderliesten,Peter A. N. Bosman*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文首次提出基于GPU加速的GP-GOMEA进化算法适应度评估方案，通过设计GPU友好的个体表示和并行评估策略，大幅提升评估吞吐量，使得在相同时间预算内实现数量级更多的评估，从而提升符号回归性能，并首次实现问题无关的进化算法在四小时内可靠回归最大费曼方程之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GP-GOMEA作为符号回归的最先进进化算法，虽能发现小型可解释模型，但计算成本高昂，限制了其在更大数据集和更复杂目标表达式上的应用。受深度学习借助GPU大规模并行取得成功的启发，本文旨在通过GPU加速解决GP-GOMEA的计算瓶颈。

Method: 设计了一种GPU友好的模板化个体表示方法，并开发了相应的并行评估策略，利用群体搜索的内在并行性，实现了适应度评估的大规模GPU并行化。

Result: 在四个标准符号回归基准上，评估吞吐量提升数个数量级；在更大数据集和更大种群规模下性能显著提升；能够分析此前不可行的更大数据集和更复杂模板；首次实现问题无关的进化算法在四小时内可靠回归最大的费曼方程之一。

Conclusion: GPU加速有效突破了GP-GOMEA的计算瓶颈，大幅扩展了其适用范围，并提供了对表达式结构如何影响搜索难度的新见解。

Abstract: GP-GOMEA is a state-of-the-art evolutionary algorithm for symbolic regression, known for discovering small and interpretable models. However, its computational cost remains substantial, limiting its applicability to larger datasets and more complex target expressions. In contrast, the rise of modern subsymbolic approaches, particularly deep learning, has been driven largely by the massive parallelism offered by GPUs. In this work, we take the first major step toward a fully GPU-accelerated GP-GOMEA by introducing a GPU-based fitness evaluation scheme. We design a GPU-friendly representation of GP-GOMEA's template-based individuals and a corresponding evaluation strategy that exploits the inherent parallelism of population-based search. This substantially increases evaluation throughput, enabling orders of magnitude more evaluations within the same time budget. Across four standard symbolic regression benchmarks, this increased evaluation capacity yields performance improvements, particularly for larger datasets and larger population sizes. Moreover, the ability to efficiently evaluate much larger datasets and more complex templates enables analyses that were previously infeasible, allowing us to systematically analyze what makes expressions increasingly difficult for GP-GOMEA, providing new insights into how expression structure affects search difficulty. Finally, for the first time, this expanded capability allows a problem-agnostic evolutionary algorithm to reliably regress one of the largest Feynman equations within four hours.

</details>


### [8] [Linear Ordering Problem: Time for a Change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31051)
*Fabrizio Fagiolo,Marco Baioletti,Valentino Santucci*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文针对线性排序问题（LOP），指出现有基准测试基于过时经济数据且忽略多最优解问题，提出了基于最新真实经济数据的新基准套件，以及利用先进元启发式算法生成多样化高质量解集的算法方案，并引入评估质量和多样性的指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LOP算法基于过时宏观经济数据基准进行评估，无法反映当代经济结构；同时LOP实例存在多个差异显著的全局最优解，依赖单一解的应用面临挑战。

Method: 提出基于最新真实经济数据的新基准套件；设计利用先进LOP元启发式算法生成多样化高质量解集的算法方案；引入同时评估解质量和多样性的指标。

Result: 在新基准套件上进行了实验，分别在传统单解场景和新引入的多解场景下报告了结果。

Conclusion: 新基准和多样性解生成方案可更有效反映当代经济结构并满足多解应用需求。

Abstract: The Linear Ordering Problem (LOP) is a fundamental combinatorial optimization problem with important applications in areas such as economics, social choice, and machine learning. Its most prominent use is the triangulation of economic input-output tables, which helps identify critical industries in an economy. Most existing algorithms have been evaluated on benchmarks derived from outdated macroeconomic data, which no longer reflect the structure of contemporary economies. Furthermore, LOP instances often exhibit many distinct global optima that can differ substantially from one another, creating challenges for applications that rely on a single solution. To address these limitations, we introduce a novel benchmark suite derived from up-to-date real-world economic data and an algorithmic scheme that leverages state-of-the-art LOP metaheuristics to generate diverse sets of high-quality solutions, together with metrics for assessing both quality and diversity. Experiments were conducted to report results on the proposed benchmark suite under both the traditional single-solution setting and the newly introduced multi-solution scenario

</details>


### [9] [Developing a novel Comorbidities Index for predicting 10-year mortality in Prostate Cancer patients: A computational data-driven approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31213)
*Davide Farinati,Francesco Barletta,Paolo Zaurito,Simone Scuderi,Nicholas Raison,Alejandro Granados,Prokar Dasgupta,Giorgio Gandaglia,Alberto Briganti*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据驱动的框架，利用群体生物启发算法（PBBIAs）重新校准查尔森合并症指数（CCI）的权重，并演化出新的符号公式，以更准确地预测前列腺癌（PCa）患者接受根治性前列腺切除术（RP）后的十年生存率。结果表明，GA、FST-PSO和SLIM等方法在区分度上优于原始CCI和PCCI，其中GPLearn在保持模型可解释性的同时表现出竞争力，整体上改善了患者选择，减少过度治疗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始CCI权重可能无法反映当代预后，而前列腺癌根治性治疗仅推荐给预期寿命至少十年的患者，因此需要更准确的十年其他原因死亡率估计，以平衡肿瘤获益与竞争风险、避免过度治疗。

Method: 利用回顾性单中心队列数据，采用基于群体生物启发算法（PBBIAs）重新校准合并症权重，并通过遗传编程（GP）、种群元启发式等方法演化替代符号公式，优化十年生存区分度。比较了六种优化策略，包括符号回归、临床验证基线及生存预测模型。

Result: GA、FST-PSO和SLIM在包含PCa特异性变量时优于原始CCI和PCCI，一致性指数提高达0.1；GPLearn产生紧凑、可解释且性能有竞争力的模型。

Conclusion: 所提出的数据驱动框架提供了一种更新且可解释的工具，改进了前列腺癌患者接受根治性前列腺切除术的选择，有助于减少过度治疗。

Abstract: The Charlson Comorbidities Index (CCI) is a weighted additive index widely used to estimate ten-year mortality risk, but its original weights may not reflect contemporary prognoses. This limitation is critical in Prostate Cancer (PCa), where radical treatment is recommended only for patients with a life expectancy of at least ten years. For candidates eligible for Radical Prostatectomy (RP), accurate estimation of ten-year other-cause mortality is essential to balance oncological benefit against competing risks and avoid overtreatment. We propose a data-driven framework to derive a comorbidity index tailored to PCa patients considered for RP. Using a retrospective single-institution cohort, we apply Population-Based Bio-Inspired Algorithms (PBBIAs) to recalibrate comorbidity weights and evolve alternative symbolic formulations optimized for ten-year survival discrimination. We compared six optimization strategies, including symbolic regression approaches based on Genetic Programming (GP), population-based metaheuristics, clinically validated baselines, and survival prediction models. Results show that GA, FST-PSO, and SLIM outperform both the original CCI and the PCCI, particularly when PCa-specific variables are included, improving the Concordance Index by up to 0.1. GPLearn yields compact and interpretable models with competitive performance. Overall, the proposed approach provides an updated and interpretable tool to improve patient selection for RP.

</details>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [10] [HetCCL: Enabling Collective Communication For Mixed-Vendor Heterogeneous Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31000)
*Yuejie Wang,Tao Chang,Yuanyuan Zhao,Yulong Ao,Zeyu Gu,Zhiyu Li,Yanmin Jia,Yan Zhang,Mingjun Zhang,He Liu,Yongzhe He,Yonghua Lin,Guyue Liu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: HetCCL是一个面向异构集群的集体通信框架，通过高效的P2P传输（消除主机-设备内存拷贝开销）和边界通信器机制（实现供应商无关的规约操作），结合层次化拓扑抽象，实现了异构设备间的高效集体通信。在4种异构设置下，HetCCL比Gloo带宽提升17-19倍，端到端训练速度提升16.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有集体通信框架（如NCCL、RCCL）为同构环境设计，无法应对混合硬件场景；而支持异构的通信库（如Gloo、OpenMPI）在数据路径上存在严重开销问题。需要一种能高效支持异构集群中多供应商硬件（如不同品牌GPU）的集体通信方案。

Method: 1) 高效异构P2P传输：在异构设备间直接传输，消除主机-设备内存拷贝开销，将控制平面卸载到CPU；2) 边界通信器机制：利用供应商集体通信库中的内在规约操作实现规约集体通信（如AllReduce），达到供应商无关性；3) 层次化拓扑抽象：将集体通信分解为集群级原语，保证跨集群数据传输量最优和带宽利用率最优。

Result: 在4种异构设置下，HetCCL在异构通信中带宽比Gloo高17-19倍，端到端训练每步时间加速高达16.9%。

Conclusion: HetCCL通过高效的异构P2P传输和供应商无关的规约机制，显著提升了异构集群中大规模语言模型训练的通信效率，验证了在混合硬件环境下实现高性能集体通信的可行性。

Abstract: Training Large Language Models (LLMs) on heterogeneous clusters presents significant challenges for collective communication, as hardware from multiple vendors introduces diverse network and computational characteristics. Existing collective communication frameworks (e.g., NCCL, RCCL) designed for homogeneous environments fail to address mixed-hardware setups, while communication libraries with heterogeneous support (e.g., Gloo, OpenMPI) incur heavy overhead in the data path.
  This paper presents HetCCL, a framework that enables heterogeneous collective communication by efficient P2P transport across heterogeneous devices (e.g., GPUs), eliminating the host-device memory copy overhead while offloading the control to the CPUs. For combining collectives (e.g., AllReduce, ReduceScatter), HetCCL introduces a border-communicator mechanism that achieves vendor independence by using the intrinsic reduction in the combining collectives in vendor collective communication libraries. With efficient heterogeneous P2P transport and portable reduction mechanism, HetCCL proposes a hierarchical topology abstraction for heterogeneous clusters, dissecting collective communication into cluster-level primitives that guarantee optimal cross-cluster data transfer volume and optimal bandwidth utilization.
  We implement HetCCL with 4 different vendor support and evaluate it in 4 heterogeneous settings with benchmarks and end-to-end LLM tasks. Our evaluation shows that HetCCL achieves 17-19x higher bandwidth than Gloo in heterogeneous communications, and speeds up end-to-end training by up to 16.9% in the per-step-time.

</details>

### [11] [CodeGolf Bench: A Multi-Language Benchmark for Evaluating Concise Code Generation Capabilities of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30394)
*Vedant Padwal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CodeGolf Bench基准，通过代码高尔夫竞赛形式评估LLM在60种编程语言上的简洁代码生成能力。实验表明推理模型显著优于非推理模型，尤其在C++中差距更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代码生成基准存在固定问题集和语言覆盖有限的问题，缺乏对代码简洁性和效率的评估能力，亟需一个动态的、能反映人类实时表现的基准来全面衡量LLM的代码生成能力。

Method: 基于code.golf平台构建CodeGolf Bench基准，支持60种编程语言，以代码高尔夫（最短字符/字节数）为评估指标。使用该基准对9个LLM在Python和C++任务上进行评估，对比推理模型与非推理模型的性能。

Result: 推理模型在Python和C++任务上均显著优于非推理模型，最佳平均百分位达70.97%。性能差距在C++上尤为明显，非推理模型在两种语言上的效率优化能力均显著弱于推理模型。

Conclusion: CodeGolf Bench为评估LLM代码生成能力提供了动态框架，验证了推理能力对生成高效简洁代码的重要性，尤其对语法要求严格的语言作用更突出。

Abstract: This paper introduces Code Bench, a benchmark capable of evaluating Large Language Models (LLMs) concise code generation abilities in 60 programming languages. Based on code golf, a recreational programming competition focused on minimal character or byte solutions, the benchmark provides a distinctive measure of LLMs ability to produce efficient, concise code. Unlike existing benchmarks limited by fixed problem sets and language coverage, CodeGolf Bench leverages the code.golf platform to provide new problems and live human performance baselines. Evaluation of nine LLMs on Python and C++ tasks demonstrates that reasoning models significantly outperform non-reasoning models, achieving best average percentile of 70.97%. This performance gap is particularly pronounced in C++, highlighting reasoning's importance for languages with strict syntax requirements. Non-reasoning models struggle more with efficiency optimization across both languages, with best percentiles significantly lower than reasoning counterparts. CodeGolf Bench offers a dynamic framework for evaluating LLM code generation capabilities against evolving human performance on code golf.

</details>


### [12] [Improving Small Language Models for Code Generation with Reinforcement Learning from Verification Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30478)
*Egor Skopin,Evgeny Kotelnikov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）在Python代码生成任务上进行了实证研究，发现在MBPP基准测试中，结合单元测试和静态分析（Ruff linter）的混合奖励设计可使pass@1提升最多13个百分点，但纯静态分析奖励会导致模型偏向生成更短但功能正确性不一定改善的代码，而混合奖励能更好地平衡正确性与代码风格约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究RLVR在代码生成任务中的有效性，特别是不同奖励设计（单元测试奖励、静态分析奖励、混合奖励）和优化粒度对模型功能正确性和行为特征的影响，以及揭示除pass@1之外更全面的诊断指标的重要性。

Method: 使用Qwen3-0.6B和Llama3.2-1B两个小模型，在MBPP基准上采用LoRA微调，对比GRPO和GSPO两种基于组的策略优化变体，设计三种奖励方案（纯单元测试、纯静态分析Ruff linter、混合奖励），评估pass@1以及生成长度、Ruff严重度分布、执行错误类型等行为诊断指标。

Result: 在提出的混合奖励配置下，RLVR在MBPP测试集上将pass@1提升最多13个百分点；纯静态分析奖励会诱导模型偏向生成更短代码以减少lint错误，但并未可靠提升功能正确性；混合奖励能缓解此退化，在正确性与风格约束之间实现更稳定的权衡。

Conclusion: RLVR在代码生成中的效果对奖励设计和优化粒度高度敏感，仅依赖pass@1评估不足，需要结合生成长度、Ruff严重度分布、执行错误类型等多维度诊断指标来识别失败模式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) trains language models using programmatically checkable signals such as unit-test outcomes, enabling direct optimization for functional correctness in code generation. We conduct an empirical study of RLVR for Python code generation on the MBPP benchmark using two small models (Qwen3-0.6B and Llama3.2-1B) with LoRA fine-tuning. Across multiple reward formulations such as: unit-test-only rewards, static-analysis-only shaping via the Ruff linter, and a combined reward, we compare group-based policy optimization variants (GRPO and GSPO) and evaluate both functional correctness and behavioral diagnostics. In our experimental setting, RLVR improves pass@1 on MBPP test by up to 13 percentage points under proposed combined reward configuration. However, we find that reward shaping can induce systematic behavioral shifts: using only static-analysis penalties may bias the policy toward shorter completions that reduce lint errors without reliably improving functional correctness. In contrast, combined rewards mitigate this degeneration and yield more stable trade-offs between correctness and style constraints. Overall, our results highlight that RLVR effectiveness for code generation is highly sensitive to reward design and optimization granularity, and that diagnostics beyond pass@1, including generation length, Ruff severity profiles, and execution error types are useful for identifying failure modes.

</details>


### [13] [BlueFin: Benchmarking LLM Agents on Financial Spreadsheets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30907)
*Srivatsa Kundurthy,Clara Na,Colton Moraine,Anoushka Mohta,Case Winter,George Fang,John Ling,Emma Strubell,Zach Kirshner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: BlueFin是一个面向专业金融领域电子表格任务的大语言模型智能体基准测试，包含131个复杂任务和3,225个细粒度评分标准。前沿LLM在该基准上平均得分低于50%，在动态正确性方面表现尤其薄弱。评估结果表明，即使是最强模型也无法胜任专业的电子表格操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管电子表格软件拥有数亿付费用户（远超专业开发者数量），但针对LLM在电子表格领域能力的研究投入相对不足，尤其是缺乏贴合专业金融岗位实际工作场景的评估基准。

Method: 构建了包含131个真实金融场景复杂任务的基准数据集（3,225项细粒度评分标准），采用专家人工标注验证评分标准和LM裁判评估的一致性（专家共识α=0.826，macro-F1=0.839），并开发了开源评估框架。

Result: 前沿LLM平均得分低于50%，在动态正确性方面表现尤为薄弱。

Conclusion: 现有LLM在专业金融电子表格任务上能力不足，特别是在需要动态正确性的任务中表现较差；BlueFin可作为评估和改进LLM该领域能力的高质量基准。

Abstract: We present BlueFin, a benchmark that tasks large language model (LLM) agents with synthesis, manipulation, and comprehension tasks over spreadsheet workbooks in the professional finance domain. Though estimates of the global population of paying users of spreadsheet software range in the hundreds of millions -- an order of magnitude more than the estimated global population of professional developers -- comparatively fewer resources have been devoted to exploring and expanding LLM capabilities in the spreadsheet domain, with fewer still dedicated to mirroring real occupational tasks encountered by those in professional finance roles. In response, we curate a set of 131 challenging, complex tasks with real-world relevance in the domain, containing 3,225 granular rubric criteria; notably, our rubric criteria and LM judge evaluations are validated by a team of expert human annotators, resulting in high-quality, granular evaluations of complex tasks that are difficult to verify programmatically but can be reliably evaluated by an LM judge agent. Our judge achieves parity with expert consensus ($α=0.826$) with a macro-F1 score of 0.839. Frontier LLMs demonstrate poor performance on the challenging benchmark, with the strongest LLMs achieving less than 50\% average scores across tasks -- models exhibit particular weaknesses in dynamic correctness. Our contributions include a dataset of examples across three categories of spreadsheet tasks, an open source harness and agentic evaluation framework, and a characterization of existing frontier models' performance on our benchmark.

</details>


### [14] [Ladder Logic Translation using Large Language Models in Industrial Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31458)
*Oluwatosin Ogundare,Promise Ekpo,Nathanial Wiggins*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM和TIA Portal Openness API的自动化流水线，用于将Rockwell梯形图程序翻译为Siemens S7梯形图程序，解决了PLC供应商切换中的代码移植问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业自动化中切换PLC供应商时，由于编程环境不兼容、梯形图构造不匹配、语义表达差异以及黑盒专有工程工具的限制，导致梯形图翻译困难，亟需一种自动化翻译方法。

Method: 提出了一种数学形式化表述问题，构建了包含XML提取、结构归一化、基于LLM的约束生成函数以及通过TIA Portal Openness API进行系统集成的自动化翻译流水线架构。

Result: 翻译结果在各类指令类别上保持了高语义一致性。

Conclusion: 该流水线能够有效实现Rockwell到Siemens梯形图的自动翻译，且语义保真度高。

Abstract: Ladder logic translation is an important problem in industrial automation because without it, it is difficult to switch Programmable Logic Controller (PLC) vendors. The prevailing translation problem highlights mismatched programming environments, incompatible ladder logic constructs, limitations in terms of differences in the semantic expressiveness of the vendor formalisms and integrated black-box proprietary engineering tools which are exemplified in our example case; Rockwell to Siemens PLC code translation. This work presents a mathematical formulation of the problem, the detailed architecture of a solution which supports XML extraction, structural normalization, constrained generative function (LLM), and system integration via the TIA Portal Openness API as rigorously engineered pipeline for automated translation of Rockwell Ladder Programs to Siemens S7 ladder programs. Finally, we present results that show that the translations retain high semantic consistency across instruction categories.

</details>


### [15] [Governance-Aware Software Architecture for Multi-Stakeholder Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31316)
*Michael Nwankwo,Eric Umuhoza*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个治理-架构对应框架，将多利益相关方数字平台中隐性的治理决策显式化，使工程师能在部署前就数据可见性、服务分解和算法设计等架构决策对利益冲突的解决方式进行辩论。该框架将五项MSP治理原则映射到架构决策空间，并以卢旺达养猪知识平台为例说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件架构文献提供了数据隔离和多租户访问控制模式，但未解决架构决策如何化解结构性利益冲突；MSP治理文献识别了治理原则，但将技术视为中立基础设施。两者均未建立治理原则与架构决策空间之间的翻译机制，导致工程师在无意识中做出具有治理后果的架构决策。

Method: 提出治理-架构对应框架，将五项MSP治理原则映射到三个架构决策空间（数据可见性、服务分解、算法设计），为每项原则指定对应的治理感知设计选择及其覆盖的技术便利默认方案。计划通过前/后判断研究，在卢旺达养猪知识平台中对五类利益相关者进行实证验证。

Result: 框架目前为理论提案，尚未经验证。已识别出治理原则与架构决策空间之间的对应关系，以及治理感知设计选择与技术便利默认方案之间的张力。计划通过预/后判断研究测试关于治理结果的可证伪预测。

Conclusion: 该框架填补了治理原则与软件架构之间的鸿沟，使隐性治理决策在部署前显式化、可辩论，从而避免工程师在无意识中通过技术决策偏袒特定利益相关者。这为构建公平的多利益相关方数字平台提供了可操作的工程方法论。

Abstract: Multi-stakeholder platforms (MSPs) coordinate diverse stakeholder groups, often with competing or conflicting requirements. As these platforms increasingly take digital form, engineers building them make architectural decisions about data visibility, service decomposition, and algorithm design that directly determine which stakeholder requirements are prioritized when conflicts arise. Software architecture literature provides patterns for data isolation and access control among tenants but does not address how architectural decisions resolve conflicts among stakeholders with structurally divergent interests. MSP governance literature identifies the principles at stake but treats technology as neutral infrastructure. Neither addresses the translation between governance principles and architectural decision spaces. This paper proposes a governance-architecture correspondence framework that surfaces implicit governance decisions, making them explicit and debatable before deployment. The framework maps five MSP governance principles to the architectural decision spaces where they must be addressed, identifying for each the governance-aware design choice and the technically convenient default it overrides. We illustrate the framework in a constructed knowledge platform for pig farming in Rwanda, where five stakeholder types present structurally conflicting requirements. As work in progress, the framework is proposed but not yet empirically validated; a planned pre/post judgment study with platform users across all stakeholder types will test falsifiable predictions about governance outcomes.

</details>


### [16] [Knowledge Boundary Probing and Demand-Guided Intervention for LLM-Based Power System Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31478)
*Hui Wu,Xiaoyang Wang,Zhong Fan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出PowerCodeBench基准测试和边界感知干预方法，通过结构化API知识边界错误分析和主动文档注入，将开源权重LLM在电网代码生成任务上的准确率提升32-56个百分点，实现了无需微调或云端推理的可靠本地部署方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多电力公司和能源研究实验室出于保密性、合规性、可复现性和成本考虑，要求LLM在本地部署，但开源权重模型的可靠性不足。现有工作忽视了代码生成失败的主要原因是结构化API知识边界错误（幻觉函数名、参数误用、结果表处理错误），而非单纯的推理能力不足。

Method: 1) PowerCodeBench：将自然语言操作员查询与pandapower代码及数值真值配对的执行验证基准生成器；2) L0-L3文档驱动的探测程序，测量每模型的API知识概况；3) 边界感知干预：结合查询端API需求估计、目标性主动文档注入和路由式反应修正。

Result: 在2000个任务的冻结版本上评估了10个开源权重LLM（1.5B-480B参数）和4个商业中端API。干预使所有≥7B参数的开源模型和所有商业API提升32-56准确率百分点。70B-120B范围的开源模型达到商业中端API的准确率水平，Llama-3.1-405B和Qwen3-Coder-480B领先。目标提示在保持全上下文准确率天花板的同时，仅使用41%的提示token成本。

Conclusion: 该方法为电网分析工作流提供了无需微调或云端推理的、面向准确率和部署时成本的可靠本地LLM辅助路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to automate power-system analysis, but many utilities and energy-research labs require on-premise serving for confidentiality, regulatory, reproducibility, and cost reasons. This makes the reliability of open-weight models a deployment issue. We show that first-pass failures in power-system code generation are dominated not by reasoning alone, but by structured API-knowledge boundary errors: hallucinated function names, misused parameters, and mishandled result tables in versioned simulation libraries.
  We introduce PowerCodeBench, an execution-validated benchmark generator that pairs natural-language operator queries with pandapower code and numerical ground truth; an L0-L3 documentation-driven probing procedure that measures per-model API knowledge profiles; and a boundary-aware intervention that combines query-side API demand estimation with targeted proactive documentation injection and routed reactive correction.
  On a 2,000-task frozen release, we evaluate ten open-weight LLMs (1.5B-480B parameters) and four commercial mid-tier APIs. The intervention improves every evaluated open-weight model of at least 7B parameters and every commercial API by 32 to 56 accuracy points. Open-weight models in the 70B-120B range match the commercial mid-tier accuracy range, while Llama-3.1-405B and Qwen3-Coder-480B lead the panel. The targeted prompts preserve the full-context accuracy ceiling while using 41% of the prompt-token cost. The result is an accuracy-side, deployment-time path toward reliable on-premise LLM assistance for grid-analysis workflows without fine-tuning or cloud inference.

</details>


### [17] [Separating Secrets from Placeholders: A Hybrid CNN-CodeBERT Framework for Three-Class Credential Leakage Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31520)
*Maksuda Bilkis Baby,Khushika Shah,Naiyue Liang,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 针对公开代码仓库中的凭证泄露问题，提出基于CodeBERT的三分类框架（真实凭证、占位符/弱凭证、非凭证），在9,426样本数据集上实现MCC 0.86、宏F1 0.90，将占位符/弱凭证检测F1从54%提升至81%，高严重性告警减少33%且不牺牲安全覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有凭证检测工具因刚性模式匹配和二元分类方案无法区分真实凭证与占位符/弱凭证，导致高误报率，亟需一种能够显式建模占位符/弱凭证作为独立类别的检测方法。

Method: 提出三分类框架，将占位符/弱凭证作为独立类别建模；基于CodeBERT的语义理解结合字符级模式识别进行特征提取；构建涵盖10种编程语言的9,426样本数据集；采用留一语言法进行跨语言泛化评估。

Result: MCC 0.86，宏F1 0.90；真实凭证漏报召回率93%、精确率89%；占位符/弱凭证检测F1从54%提升至81%；高严重性告警减少33.0%（从373降至250）；10种语言中9种F1高于0.80。

Conclusion: 三分类框架有效降低了凭证检测中的误报率，显著提升占位符/弱凭证识别能力，同时保持强大的跨语言泛化能力和安全覆盖率的完整。

Abstract: Credential leakage in public source code repositories poses a critical security threat, with over 23.8 million secrets exposed in 2024 alone. Existing detection tools suffer from high false-positive rates because rigid pattern matching and binary classification schemes fail to distinguish genuine credentials from placeholder or weak credentials. We propose a three-class classification framework that explicitly models placeholder or weak credentials as a distinct class, leveraging CodeBERT-based semantic understanding combined with character-level pattern recognition. We evaluate our approach on a newly constructed dataset of 9,426 samples spanning 10 programming languages. Our model achieves a Matthews Correlation Coefficient of 0.86 and a macro F1-score of 0.90, achieving 93% recall and 89% precision for genuine credential leaks while reducing high severity alerts by 33.0% (from 373 to 250) without sacrificing security coverage. Compared to prior character-level approaches, our method improves placeholder or weak credential detection from 54% to 81% F1-score while maintaining strong cross language generalization, with 9 of 10 languages achieving F1 above 0.80 under leave-one-language-out evaluation.

</details>

### [18] [Listing Even Cycles Faster than the Submodular-Width Barrier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30564)
*Vasileios Nakos,Hung Q. Ngo,Andreas Panayi*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的算法，能够在$\tilde O(m^{(2k^2-k+1)/(k^2+1)}+t)$时间内列出所有$2k$-环，改进了30年来Alon-Yuster-Zwick（AYZ）的经典结果。关键在于提出了非对称超饱和（asymmetric supersaturation）引理，算法仅使用连接和投影算子，天然适合数据库系统实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AYZ结果（1997）是子模宽度（submodular width）和PANDA框架的基础，核心开放问题是组合算法能否突破子模宽度界限。Bringmann和Gorbachev（STOC 2025）给出了一般合取查询下子模宽度可能最优的下界证据，但含自连接和对称EDB的偶数环查询可能例外。明确$C_{2k}$检测与列出的复杂度是克服子模宽度屏障的关键一步。

Method: 核心创新是提出非对称超饱和（asymmetric supersaturation）结果用于偶数环。算法利用多个树分解计划（tree-decomposition plans）上的连接（join）和投影（project）算子，与以往基于BFS的图算法不同，天然适合数据库系统实现。

Result: 对所有$k\geq 3$，将$2k$-环列出的时间从AYZ的$\tilde O(m^{2-1/k})$改进到$\tilde O(m^{(2k^2-k+1)/(k^2+1)}+t)$。例如4-环（$k=2$）已有$\tilde O(m^{4/3})$结果，6-环（$k=3$）从$\tilde O(m^{5/3})$改进到$\tilde O(m^{8/5})$。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了30年来偶数环列出的开放问题，给出了优于子模宽度界限的组合算法。算法基于连接-投影算子而非BFS，易于在数据库系统中实现，为更广泛地突破子模宽度屏障提供了新思路。

Abstract: A classic result of Alon, Yuster, and Zwick (AYZ, Algorithmica 1997) shows that all $2k$-cycles in an $m$-edge graph can be listed in $\tilde O(m^{2-1/k}+t)$ time, where $t$ is the output size. This bound underlies the {\em submodular width} of Marx (JACM 2013) and the PANDA framework of Abo Khamis, Ngo, and Suciu (PODS 2017), which extend AYZ to arbitrary conjunctive queries with degree constraints. A central open question is whether combinatorial algorithms can beat the submodular-width barrier.
  Bringmann and Gorbachev (STOC 2025) gave lower-bound evidence that submodular width may be optimal for general conjunctive queries under combinatorial algorithms. The picture changes for $2k$-cycles on undirected graphs, whose queries have self-joins and symmetric EDBs: recent works improve on AYZ for even-cycle detection and listing. Pinning down the complexity of $C_{2k}$-detection and listing is thus a natural step toward overcoming the submodular-width barrier for such queries.
  For detection, Dahlgaard, Knudsen, and St{ö}ckel (STOC 2017) solved $C_{2k}$-detection in $\tilde O(m^{2k/(k+1)})$ time. Listing is harder. Jin and Xu (STOC 2023), and independently Abboud, Khoury, Leibowitz, and Safier (FSTTCS 2023), listed 4-cycles in $\tilde O(m^{4/3}+t)$ time; Vassilevska~Williams and Westover (ITCS 2025) listed 6-cycles in $\tilde O(m^{8/5}+t)$ time, improving the AYZ bounds of $\tilde O(m^{3/2})$ and $\tilde O(m^{5/3})$. The general case has remained open for 30 years.
  Building on these works, we list $2k$-cycles in $\tilde O(m^{(2k^2-k+1)/(k^2+1)}+t)$ time, improving AYZ for every $k\geq 3$. The key ingredient is an \emph{asymmetric supersaturation} result for even cycles. Our algorithms use only join and project operators over multiple tree-decomposition plans, making them naturally implementable in database systems, in contrast to prior BFS-based graph approaches.

</details>


### [19] [Sophrosyne: Agentic Exploration of Relational Data Systems Needs Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30862)
*Madhav Jivrajani,Ramnatthan Alagappan,Aishwarya Ganesan*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文研究了Text2SQL智能体在通过细粒度API探索数据系统时出现的过度探索问题，提出Sophrosyne系统——通过增强API响应中的指导指令来约束探索过程，实验结果显示过度探索降低4.6倍，准确率提升最高12.4%（约4个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据系统暴露的API在粗粒度和细粒度之间存在根本性权衡：细粒度API虽提供精确访问，但导致LLM驱动的Text2SQL智能体过度探索（over-exploration），引入无关schema元素，降低SQL生成准确性。作者旨在解决细粒度API环境下智能体的过度探索问题。

Method: 提出Sophrosyne数据系统环境，核心思路是在API响应中增强指导性指令（directives），用于引导智能体的探索过程，从而遏制过度探索行为。

Result: 过度探索减少4.6倍；SQL生成准确率提升最高12.4%（约4个绝对百分点）。

Conclusion: 指导性指令能有效缓解细粒度API下智能体的过度探索问题，在降低探索成本的同时提升SQL生成准确性，为数据系统API设计提供了新的优化方向。

Abstract: Text2SQL agents powered by LLMs translate natural language intent into SQL by exploring the data system through tool calls before formulating the query. However, to ensure secure and scoped access, data systems construct environments with explicit API surfaces. We study and categorize these APIs exposed today as either coarse-grained or fine-grained and posit that choosing between them presents a fundamental tradeoff between cost-efficient exploration and accurate SQL generation. Most data systems expose fine-grained APIs, but this inadvertently disadvantages agents: they over-explore, incorporating irrelevant schema elements into their query formulation and produce inaccurate results. We argue that curbing over-exploration is key to the effective use of these API surfaces, and propose Sophrosyne, a data system environment that augments API responses with directives that guide the agent's exploration process. Initial results show that directives reduce over-exploration by 4.6x and boost accuracy by up to 12.4% (approx. 4 percentage points).

</details>


### [20] [Modeling and Optimization for Massive Data Allocation in Database](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31002)
*Panpan Niu,Boxiang Ren,Hao Wu,Xin Yao*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文针对分布式数据库中数据放置问题，提出了一种基于谱聚类归一化割的模型，利用Bregman近端梯度法求解松弛后的0-1优化问题，在保证数据均衡和低通信开销方面优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式数据库环境下，数据分散在不同机器上，交易执行需要跨节点收集数据，产生额外通信成本。现有数据放置方案未能同时满足数据均衡和低通信开销的要求，亟需设计高效的算法。

Method: 受谱聚类中归一化割思想的启发，构建数据分配问题的数学模型，将问题形式化为0-1优化问题，并采用Bregman近端梯度法求解松弛后的优化问题，保证收敛性。

Result: 数值实验表明，收敛解可以平滑地舍入为离散解；该算法在最小化迁移成本的同时保持优越的数据均衡性，优于简单和元启发式分区方案。

Conclusion: 基于归一化割的模型能有效调和数据均衡与低通信开销之间的固有冲突，所提算法在分布式OLTP系统中具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: In the era of big data, e-commerce and Internet platforms face the challenge of processing massive amounts of data. However, due to data being scattered across different machines in distributed database, extra communication costs are incurred in gathering relevant data to complete transactions. Without a carefully designed data placement scheme, this cost can severely impact the performance of Online Transaction Processing systems. To meet industry requirements, algorithms that output a data placement scheme that achieves i) data balance and ii) low communication overhead within a fixed period of time are eagerly investigated. Although some existing methods have been studied, they do not adequately meet the aforementioned requirements. In this paper, inspired by the normalized cut of spectral clustering, we introduce a novel model for data allocation problem. The normalized cut reconciles the inherent conflict between the two objectives. Taking into account the variable characteristics of the model, we formulate the problem as a 0-1 optimization problem, and solve the relaxed problem using the Bregman proximal gradient method with guaranteed convergence. The numerical experiments reveal that the convergent solutions can be smoothly rounded to discrete solutions. Furthermore, our algorithm surpasses both simple and meta-heuristic partitioning schemes by minimizing migration costs while maintaining a superior balance.

</details>


### [21] [SpecDB: LLM-Generated Customized Databases via Feature-Oriented Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31097)
*Yunkai Lou,Longbin Lai,Shunyang Li,Zhengping Qian,Ying Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出 SpecDB，一个利用大语言模型按需生成定制化关系数据库的系统。通过分解 10 个功能模块及其变体，构建扩展的 FODA 依赖图 DBGraph，并采用多智能体流水线生成、验证和组装模块。在 TPC-C 基准测试中，生成的数据库（23,779 行 Rust）在 1 和 10 个仓库下零错误运行 60 分钟，10 仓库时 tpmC=130，与 PostgreSQL（128）和 MySQL（127）相当，延迟相近，代码量仅为其约 3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统关系数据库在各类部署中提供统一的功能集，而实际工作负载只使用其中一小部分子系统。这种一刀切的方式造成资源浪费和性能冗余。作者希望探究能否按需生成功能集与目标工作负载精确匹配的定制化数据库。

Method: 1）调研 9 个生产系统，分解为 10 个功能模块及其实现变体；2）采用 FODA 特征模型并扩展合作边（cooperate edge）构建依赖图 DBGraph；3）设计分层的模块构建流水线，每个模块由专用子智能体（含 Main、Tester、Architect 三个内部智能体）生成、验证和集成；4）由 Refining Agent 迭代修复和调优；5）配套选择组件将自然语言工作负载描述翻译为实现变体集合。

Result: 在 TPC-C/BenchmarkSQL 上，生成的 23,779 行 Rust 数据库在 1 和 10 个仓库下 60 分钟零错误运行。10 仓库时 tpmC=130，PostgreSQL 为 128，MySQL 为 127，延迟相近，代码量仅为其约 3%。

Conclusion: 智能体在模块规范层面而非产品源码层面操作，可跨系统边界组合技术。随着 LLM 成本下降，为目标工作负载生成定制数据库正变得简单可行。

Abstract: Mainstream relational databases ship a uniform feature set across deployments, although individual workloads exercise only a fraction of the available subsystems. We investigate whether a database can instead be generated on demand with a feature set matched to the target workload. We present SpecDB, a system that uses large language models (LLMs) to synthesize customized relational databases. We survey 9 production systems and decompose them into 10 functional modules, each further divided into implementation variants. To capture cross-module dependencies, including cases where implementations in disjoint subtrees must be co-designed, we adopt the FODA feature model and extend it with a cooperate edge, yielding a dependency graph DBGraph. SpecDB operationalizes DBGraph through a layered module-construction pipeline in which each module is generated, validated, and integrated by a dedicated subagent (driven by three inner agents: Main, Tester, Architect), and a Refining Agent that iteratively repairs and tunes the assembled database against a user-supplied refining harness with read-only access to existing database source code. A companion selection component translates a natural-language workload description into a set of implementation variants, providing an end-to-end pipeline from workload description to deployable database. We evaluate SpecDB on TPC-C with BenchmarkSQL. The generated database (23,779 lines of Rust) completes 60-minute TPC-C at 1 and 10 warehouses with zero errors. At 10 warehouses it reaches tpmC=130, compared to 128 for PostgreSQL and 127 for MySQL, with comparable latency at ~3% of their code size. Because the agent operates at module-specification level rather than product source, it can in principle combine techniques across system boundaries. Paired with falling LLM costs, generating a purpose-built database for a target workload is becoming straightforward.

</details>


### [22] [AIM: A practical approach to automated index management for SQL databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31406)
*Ritwik Yadav,Satyanarayana R. Valluri,Mohamed Zaït*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文介绍AIM（自动索引管理器），一个可配置的索引管理系统，能够为SQL数据库识别有影响力的二级索引以高效利用CPU、I/O和存储资源。该系统已在数千个生产数据库上验证，具备快速收敛、减少对查询优化器依赖、提供"零回归"保证等特性，是少数大规模部署于生产数据库的工业级索引推荐引擎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据库索引管理依赖人工经验或优化器，难以高效利用CPU、I/O和存储资源，且无法自适应工作负载变化。需要一种自动化的、工业级的索引管理方案来优化数据库物理设计。

Method: AIM采用端到端设计，具备快速收敛算法以推荐宽复合索引、减少对查询优化器的依赖、提供"零回归"保证（生产工作负载不降级），并为每个索引推荐附带基于指标的解释说明。

Result: 在数千个生产数据库上验证，AIM能快速识别最有效的索引，且最终物理设计接近最优。

Conclusion: AIM是少数大规模部署于生产数据库的工业级索引推荐引擎，能够自适应工作负载变化，使数据库物理设计自动调整，同时提供性能保证和可解释性。

Abstract: This paper describes AIM (Automatic Index Manager), a configurable index management system, which identifies impactful secondary indexes for SQL databases to efficiently use available resources such as CPU, I/O and storage. It has been validated on thousands of databases which support production systems. With AIM, the physical design of the database adapts itself to the changes in the workload.We lay out the end to end design of AIM while calling out the guarantees and tradeoffs associated with our design choices. Some of the salient features of AIM include fast convergence even while recommending wide composite indexes, reduced reliance on the query optimizer and a "no regression" guarantee for production workloads. Each index recommendation from AIM is accompanied with a metrics driven explanation, making it easier to verify machine driven changes.AIM is one of the few industrial strength index recommendation engines that is deployed on production databases at a large scale. The experimental results show that AIM is quick in identifying the most effective indexes and the resulting physical design is close to optimal.

</details>


### [23] [A Theoretical Study of DBLog: Certified Virtual Cuts for a Snapshot-Equivalent Replay of Live Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31475)
*Andreas Andreakis*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文是2019年DBLog论文的理论后续，形式化了DBLog机制的正确性对象：无需物理快照即可构建快照等价的重放证书（认证虚拟切割），并证明了在任意良构DBLog运行中每个键的重放等价性、证书见证性以及源端连续性结果。所有证明均在Isabelle/HOL中机器检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2019年DBLog论文从操作层面描述了CDC机制（主键范围扫描块与变更日志交织，无需表锁、写暂停或全局读事务），但未形式化其正确性对象。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，给出严格的正确性形式化定义和证明。

Method: 提出认证虚拟切割（certified virtual cut）作为核心形式化对象：一个有限证据束，其认证重放能在所选键范围和前沿达到与源相同的每键状态。虚拟切割是外延性的（重放等价而非物理快照），不要求所有块行对应同一源时间戳。利用伊莎贝尔/霍尔（Isabelle/HOL）进行机器检查证明。

Result: 证明了三个核心结果：（1）任意良构DBLog运行在其前沿和键范围上满足每键重放等价性；（2）接受的证书（基于忠实源观测评估）能见证这样的良构运行并产生虚拟切割；（3）源端连续性：在同一键范围上，已建立的切割可通过追加已提交的忠实CDC段向前推进到更后的前沿。所有证明均在Isabelle/HOL中完成机器检查。

Conclusion: 本文给出了DBLog机制的形式化正确性基础，但明确指出：全表正确性、恰好一次投递、sink状态收敛以及到特定部署的迁移并非仅凭证书接受就能保证。这为后续将正确性保证扩展到完整CDC管道提供了理论框架。

Abstract: This paper is a theoretical follow-up to the 2019 DBLog paper, which introduced a change-data-capture (CDC) mechanism for both backfilling a downstream system from a live source database and streaming its ongoing changes, while the source keeps accepting writes. DBLog reads the table in primary-key range scans (chunks) interleaved with the source change log: watermarks locate each chunk in log order, chunk rows become refresh events, and CDC events repair stale chunk observations. The mechanism requires no table lock, no pause in writes, and no global read transaction, and has since been adopted by Debezium and Apache Flink CDC.
  The 2019 paper described the mechanism operationally but did not formalize its correctness object. This paper formalizes that object: DBLog constructs a snapshot-equivalent replay certificate without a single physical snapshot read.
  The central formal object is a certified virtual cut: a finite evidence bundle whose certified replay reaches the same per-key state as the source at a chosen frontier on a chosen key scope. A virtual cut is extensional: replay equality at a frontier, not a physical snapshot read, and does not assert one source timestamp across chunk rows. The paper proves per-key replay equality for every wellformed DBLog run at its frontier and scope, and that an accepted certificate, evaluated against faithful source observation, witnesses such a run and yields a virtual cut. It also proves a source-side continuation result: on the same scope, an established cut advances to later frontiers by appending the scope-filtered faithful CDC segment committed in between. Each result is Conditional on premises stated where the result appears. Whole-table correctness, exactly-once delivery, sink-state convergence, and transfer to named deployments are not consequences of certificate acceptance alone. All proofs are machine-checked in Isabelle/HOL.

</details>

### [24] [Gait2Hip-60: A Unified Deep Learning Benchmark for Predicting Hip Muscle Forces and Joint Moments from Multi-Cadence Gait Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30374)
*Jiaqi Zhang,Ji Hou,Qing Sun,Xianzhi Gao,Bo Huo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究开发了基于深度学习的框架，直接从下肢步态运动学预测髋关节肌肉力和关节力矩，比较了LSTM、Transformer和Mamba三种序列模型。在60名健康成年人数据上，Transformer表现最佳（髋肌力R²=0.819，髋力矩R²=0.862），并在股骨头坏死患者的零样本外推测试中保持中等预测能力（R²≈0.55），验证了从步态运动学估计髋关节动力学的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于肌肉骨骼仿真估计髋关节肌肉力和关节力矩的方法虽然信息丰富，但耗时长、难以在临床环境中应用，亟需更高效、便捷的替代方案。

Method: 收集60名健康成人在三种节拍器引导步频下的步态数据，以10个双侧下肢关节角为输入，OpenSim导出的髋关节肌肉力和关节力矩为参考输出，在统一协议下训练LSTM、Transformer和Mamba三种深度学习模型，并在9名股骨头坏死患者上进行零样本外推测试。

Result: 在健康受试者中，Transformer在髋肌力预测（RMSE=1.33 N/kg, R²=0.819）和髋力矩预测（RMSE=0.11 Nm/kg, R²=0.862）上均最优；在ONFH患者零样本测试中保持中等预测能力（髋肌力R²=0.537，髋力矩R²=0.569）。

Conclusion: 从步态运动学估计髋关节动力学具有可行性，Transformer可作为强基线模型，但需在更广泛的病理数据上验证并提升泛化能力后才能用于临床。

Abstract: Estimating hip muscle forces and joint moments during gait typically relies on musculoskeletal simulation, which is informative but time-consuming and difficult to apply in clinical settings. This study developed a deep learning framework to predict these hip dynamics parameters directly from lower-limb gait kinematics and compared three representative sequence models under a unified protocol. Gait data were collected from 60 healthy adults under three metronome-guided cadence conditions. Ten bilateral lower-limb joint angles were used as inputs, and OpenSim-derived hip muscle forces and hip joint moments were used as reference outputs. Three deep learning models of LSTM, Transformer, and Mamba were trained and evaluated using the same subject-level split, preprocessing pipeline, and metrics. The best model was then directly tested on an external cohort of 9 patients with osteonecrosis of the femoral head (ONFH) without retraining. In the healthy-subject benchmark, Transformer achieved the best subject-level mean performance for both hip muscle force prediction (RMSE = 1.33 N/kg, MAE = 0.57 N/kg, R2 = 0.819) and hip joint moment prediction (RMSE = 0.11 Nm/kg, MAE = 0.07 Nm/kg, R2 = 0.862), with similar advantages across walking cadences. In zero-shot external validation, Transformer retained moderate predictive ability in ONFH for hip muscle force prediction (RMSE = 1.51 N/kg, MAE = 0.70 N/kg, R2 = 0.537) and hip joint moment prediction (RMSE = 0.17 Nm/kg, MAE = 0.12 Nm/kg, R2 = 0.569). These findings support the feasibility of estimating hip dynamics from gait kinematics, identify Transformer as a strong baseline, and highlight the need for broader pathological validation and improved generalization before clinical application.

</details>


### [25] [When LLMs Learn to Be Consistently Wrong: A Multi-Model Study of Linear Representations of Synthetic Deception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30381)
*Vahideh Zolfaghari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过在多模型（Pythia-1.4B、Gemma-2-2B/9B、Qwen2.5-7B、Llama-3.1-8B）上进行LoRA微调，研究合成性不诚实的表征基础。线性探针在早期层就能以近完美AUC检测不诚实，支持线性表征假说，且不诚实方向在深层逐渐巩固。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欺骗性对齐（模型保持准确内部表征却故意输出错误）是AI安全的核心挑战。合成性不诚实作为受控实验场景，为研究学习性欺骗的表征基础提供了测试平台。

Method: 对五个Transformer模型（Pythia-1.4B、Gemma-2-2B/9B、Qwen2.5-7B、Llama-3.1-8B）的诚实与不诚实变体使用LoRA微调，基于相同问题分布。训练线性探针（均值池化隐藏状态）检测不诚实，并对比逻辑回归探针与MLP探针。进行机制分析：Fisher判别比、有效秩、质心几何、方向稳定性、跨域对齐、校准误差（ECE）。

Result: 线性探针在四种架构的第1-3层即达到近完美AUC（≥0.99），Pythia-1.4B峰值为0.705。逻辑回归探针一致匹配或优于MLP探针。在TruthfulQA训练的探针泛化到MMLU（ΔAUC≈0）。深层表征对高斯噪声鲁棒，Gemma-2稳定性突出。发现两种机制：Pythia/Llama/Qwen的表征坍塌 vs Gemma-2的高维保持。不诚实方向在深层逐渐巩固，第1-4层可实现最优校准（ECE<0.01，Pythia除外）。

Conclusion: 鲁棒且域不变的不诚实表征可通过适度的监督微调快速固化，这对基于激活的监控方法具有重要启示。

Abstract: Deceptive alignment, in which models maintain accurate internal representations while deliberately producing false outputs, remains a central challenge in AI safety. While strategic deception is the primary long-term concern, synthetic dishonesty - induced via direct optimization on incorrect answers - provides a controlled testbed for studying the representational basis of learned deception. We introduce a multi-model paradigm in which honest and deceptive variants of five transformer models (Pythia-1.4B, Gemma-2-2B/9B, Qwen2.5-7B, Llama-3.1-8B) are fine-tuned using LoRA on the same question distribution. Linear probes trained on mean-pooled hidden states detect synthetic dishonesty with near-perfect AUC (greater than or equal to 0.99) as early as layers 1-3 in four architectures, while Pythia-1.4B reaches a peak of 0.705. Logistic regression probes consistently match or outperform MLP probes, supporting the Linear Representation Hypothesis. Probes trained on TruthfulQA generalize with near-zero loss (Delta AUC approx. 0) to held-out MMLU subjects. Late-layer representations show strong robustness to Gaussian noise, with Gemma-2 models exhibiting exceptional stability. Mechanistic analysis of Fisher Discriminant Ratio, effective rank, centroid geometry, directional stability, cross-domain alignment, and calibration (ECE) reveals two regimes: representational collapse in Pythia/Llama/Qwen versus high-dimensional preservation in Gemma-2. Across all models, the dishonesty direction consolidates progressively in deeper layers, with optimal calibration (ECE less than 0.01 except Pythia) achievable in layers 1-4. These results demonstrate that robust, domain-invariant dishonesty representations can be rapidly entrenched via modest supervised fine-tuning, with implications for activation-based monitoring.

</details>


### [26] [LongDS-Bench: On the Failure of Long-Horizon Agentic Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30434)
*Kewei Xu,Xiaoben Lu,Shuofei Qiao,Zihan Ding,Haoming Xu,Lei Liang,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LongDS是一个评估长程、多轮数据分析智能体的基准，包含68个从Kaggle笔记本构建的任务（共2,225轮），覆盖六个领域。该基准要求智能体在平均依赖跨度11.3轮的情况下维持、更新、恢复和组合演化中的分析状态。评估五个最先进模型，最佳模型平均准确率仅48.45%，从早期到后期轮次性能下降近47个百分点，长程误差占失败的52%-69%。分析表明，额外步骤未必提升性能，关键瓶颈在于维持正确的分析状态而非增加交互预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准大多评估孤立或短交互任务，无法测试智能体在长程数据分析中追踪演化上下文的能力，因此需要构建一个专注于长程、多轮、状态演化的评估基准。

Method: 从真实Kaggle笔记本构建68个任务，共2,225轮，覆盖地理科学、商业、教育等六个领域。任务围绕状态演化模式（反事实扰动、回滚、多状态组合）设计，平均依赖跨度11.3轮。评估五个SOTA模型并分析其长程性能退化与错误模式。

Result: 最佳模型平均准确率仅48.45%，从早期到后期轮次性能下降近47个百分点，长程误差占失败的52%-69%。额外智能体步骤未必提升性能。

Conclusion: 长程数据分析的关键瓶颈在于维持正确的分析状态而非增加交互预算，LongDS为研究可靠的长程智能体数据分析提供了基准。

Abstract: Real-world data analysis is inherently iterative, yet existing benchmarks mostly evaluate isolated or short interactive tasks, leaving agents' ability to track evolving analytical context over long horizons untested. We introduce LongDS, a benchmark for long-horizon, multi-turn data analysis where agents must maintain, update, restore, and compose evolving analytical states. LongDS comprises 68 tasks constructed from real-world Kaggle notebooks, spanning 2,225 turns across six domains including Geoscience, Business, and Education. Tasks are designed around state-evolution patterns (e.g., counterfactual perturbation, rollback, multi-state composition), with an average dependency span of 11.3 turns. Evaluating five state-of-the-art models, we find that the best model reaches only 48.45% average accuracy, performance drops nearly 47 points from early to late turns, and long-horizon errors account for 52%--69% of failures. Further analysis shows that additional agent steps do not necessarily improve performance, suggesting that the key bottleneck is maintaining a correct analytical state rather than increasing interaction budget. We release LongDS to support research on reliable long-horizon agentic data analysis. Code and data will be released at https://github.com/zjunlp/DataMind.

</details>


### [27] [Learning to Solve and Optimize by Evolving Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31049)
*Veronika Semmelrock,Benedetta Strizzolo,Francesco Zuccato,Gerhard Friedrich,Patrick Rodler,Konstantin Schekotihin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CHECKMATE工具，通过代码进化自动生成算法，仅需形式化规约（what）而无需人工设计求解过程（how），在配置与调度等工业问题上超越了最先进的求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大规模工业组合优化问题通常需要问题形式化、专用求解器和专家设计的启发式算法，专家不仅要指定解决方案是什么（what），还要指定如何推导（how）。本文旨在消除对"how"的需求，仅依赖"what"来自动生成算法。

Method: 提出CHECKMATE工具，利用代码进化（code evolution）自动生成算法。形式化规约确保解的正确性并支持系统性能评估，自然语言描述引导进化过程。

Result: 在配置（configuration）和调度（scheduling）两个工业领域的选定问题上，进化生成的算法在所有案例中均一致优于最先进的求解器。

Conclusion: 形式化方法在引导代码进化以自动解决复杂现实问题方面具有巨大潜力，代表了算法生成范式的转变。

Abstract: Combinatorial and optimization problems are fundamental to many industrial AI applications. Solving large-scale real-world instances of such problems typically requires careful problem formalization, specialized solvers, and expert-designed heuristics. Thus, experts need to specify not only what solutions are, but also how they are derived. By introducing the tool CHECKMATE, we show that algorithm generation via code evolution represents a paradigm shift by eliminating the need to formulate the how. CHECKMATE solely relies on the what. Specifically, a formal specification ensures solutions' correctness and enables systematic performance evaluation of the generated programs, while a natural language description guides the evolutionary process. The effectiveness of our method is demonstrated on selected problems from two industrial domains: configuration and scheduling. In all cases, the evolved algorithms consistently outperform state-of-the-art solvers. This underscores the potential of formal methods in guiding code evolution for automatically solving complex real-world problems.

</details>


### [28] [Generalized Intention Modeling in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31318)
*Mateusz Odrowaz-Sypniewski,Jasmine Bayrooti,Ajay Shankar,Amanda Prorok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有对手建模方法预先假定某种固定信息（如对手下一动作或未来状态）能普遍表征意图，但实际中意图因任务和环境而异。本文提出任务自适应的对手建模框架，学习基于性能驱动的多种意图表征混合，并引入一种与智能体未来回报互信息最大化的新意图表征，在多种任务中达到或超越现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对手建模方法将先验选定的固定信息（如对手下一动作、未来环境状态）作为意图表征，但实证表明意图具有任务和环境依赖性，固定信息无法普遍适用于不同场景。

Method: 提出任务自适应对手建模框架，学习性能驱动的多种意图表征混合；引入一种新的意图表征，通过与ego智能体未来回报的互信息最大化来捕捉与性能最相关的对手信息。

Result: 在多种任务中一致匹配或超越现有最优基线方法，并揭示了不同对手建模策略何时及为何成功。

Conclusion: 任务自适应的多意图表征混合方法能有效应对意图的任务与环境依赖性，基于互信息最大化的意图表征更能捕获与性能直接相关的对手信息。

Abstract: Modeling an opponent's intent is critical for effective decision-making in non-cooperative, competitive, and general-sum multi-agent reinforcement learning. Existing opponent modeling methods encode intent using an embedding derived from episode information chosen a priori, such as the opponent's next action or a future environment state, and use this to guide the ego-agent's behavior. These approaches assume that the chosen information is universally representative of intent; however, we show empirically that this is not the case as intentions are often task- and environment-dependent. To address this, we introduce a task-adaptive opponent modeling framework that learns a performance-driven mixture of multiple intent representations. We further introduce a new intention representation that maximizes mutual information with the ego-agent's future returns, thereby capturing opponent information that is most directly relevant to performance. Our approach consistently matches or exceeds the performance of state-of-the-art baselines across diverse tasks and yields insights into when and why different opponent modeling strategies succeed.

</details>


### [29] [Value Functions as Supermartingale Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31524)
*Alessandro Abate,Daniel Contro,Mirco Giacobbe,Agustín Martínez-Suñé,Diptarko Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了随机系统认证方法与强化学习之间的理论联系，证明在适当奖励函数下，几乎必然满足ω-正则性质的策略的价值函数可以编码为Streett超鞅证书，为通过强化学习合成形式化证书提供了原则性途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认证方法基于实值超鞅证书能为随机系统提供几乎必然满足ω-正则性质的充分证明规则，但强化学习方法虽然广泛研究ω-正则任务却缺乏形式化保证（除有限状态动作空间外）。本文旨在弥合这两条研究路线之间的差距。

Method: 建立理论连接：证明在适当的奖励函数下，几乎必然满足ω-正则性质的策略对应的价值函数可编码为Streett超鞅证书。该结论对有限、可数无限和连续状态空间均成立，并在有限马尔可夫决策过程上进行了实验验证。

Result: 理论上建立了价值函数与Streett超鞅证书之间的等价关系，实验上在有限MDP上验证了该理论连接的有效性。

Conclusion: 该理论连接为通过强化学习合成形式化证书提供了原则性途径，适用于包括可数无限和连续状态空间在内的一般状态空间。

Abstract: Certification methods for stochastic systems provide sufficient proof rules, based on real-valued supermartingale certificates, to determine the almost-sure satisfaction of $ω$-regular properties (and therefore of linear temporal logic) over general state spaces, encompassing both countably infinite and continuous state spaces. Conversely, reinforcement learning (RL) methods for $ω$-regular tasks have received considerable attention, but they typically lack formal guarantees that the learned policy satisfies the specification, except possibly for finite state and action spaces. We bridge these two lines of research by establishing a novel theoretical connection: under an appropriate reward, the value function associated to a policy that almost surely satisfies an $ω$-regular property encodes a Streett supermartingale certificate for that specification. Our results, validated experimentally on finite Markov decision processes, hold for finite, countably infinite, and continuous state spaces, suggesting a principled route to certificate synthesis via RL.

</details>


### [30] [A Novel Evaluation Metric for Unsupervised Learning in AIS-Based Maritime Anomaly Detection: MADQI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30388)
*Ismet Gocer,Zakirul Bhuiyan,Raza Hasan,Shakeel Ahmad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的系统性框架用于检测海事AIS数据中的异常（包括速度异常、位置跳跃、时间间隙和转向角度异常），并引入了一种无需标注数据的复合质量指标MADQI（海事异常检测质量指数），通过四个子指标（ARC、PPS、SDS、ECE）评估无监督异常检测性能，在AIS数据集上取得了80.37%的MADQI分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无监督异常检测算法（如Isolation Forest）虽被广泛用于检测异常船舶行为，但缺乏系统和有意义的评估指标，特别是缺少无需标注数据就能评估异常检测性能的方法。

Method: 提出MADQI复合指标，包含四个互联指标：异常率一致性（ARC）、物理合理性得分（PPS）、得分分布分离度（SDS）和极端案例证据（ECE）。使用Haversine距离计算分析AIS数据空间和行为特征，通过多分块评估和自适应缩放技术进行自动归一化融合。

Result: 整体MADQI得分达到80.37%；ECE得分为0.907，ARC得分为1.000，表明在检测极端异常和保持异常率一致性方面表现优异。

Conclusion: 该框架为无监督异常检测在海事AIS数据上的评估提供了可靠且有意义的方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces a new systematic framework for detecting anomalies in maritime Automatic Identification System (AIS) datasets. These anomalies include abnormal vessel behaviours related to speed, position jumps, time gaps, and turn angles. Although unsupervised learning algorithms such as Isolation Forest are widely used for detecting anomalous vessel movements, they often lack systematic and meaningful evaluation measures. To address this limitation, we propose a novel quality metric called Maritime Anomaly Detection Quality Index (MADQI). The prosed MADQI is a composite index designed to evaluate the anomaly detection performance of machine learning models without requiring labelled data. The proposed framework uses Haversine distance calculations to analyse AIS datasets and identify anomalies based on their spatial and behavioural characteristics. The proposed MADQI evaluation framework integrates four interconnected metrics: Anomaly Rate Consistency (ARC), Physical Plausibility Score (PPS), Score Distribution Separation (SDS), and Extreme Case Evidence (ECE). These metrics are combined through automatic normalisation using multi-chunk evaluation and adaptive scaling techniques. Experimental results on the AIS dataset show that the proposed framework achieved a MADQI score of 80.37%, demonstrating its effectiveness for unsupervised anomaly detection. In particular, the algorithm performed strongly in identifying abnormal vessel behaviour. Among the individual MADQI components, ECE and ARC achieved scores of 0.907 and 1.000, respectively, indicating excellent capability in detecting extreme anomalies and maintaining anomaly rate consistency. Overall, these results are encouraging and demonstrate that the proposed framework provides a reliable and meaningful approach for evaluating unsupervised anomaly detection in maritime AIS data.

</details>


### [31] [NumLeak: Public Numeric Benchmarks as Latent Labels in Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30393)
*Anany Kotawala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NumLeak框架，系统性地揭示了前沿大语言模型在公开数值基准测试中存在严重的记忆性回忆问题，特别是在金融、经济和气候数据上表现出近乎完美的相关性（r=0.97-0.99），通过白盒实验复现了剂量-反应关系，并证明闭源API的黑盒探测低估了记忆通道的强度；同时发现简单的系统提示防御即可有效阻断非自适应单轮后缀攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公开数值基准可能已被预训练数据污染，模型在日期条件任务上的表现可能反映的是记忆性回忆而非真正的样本外泛化能力，亟需一种能够区分记忆与泛化的测量框架。

Method: 提出NumLeak框架，结合两个层面：(1) 对生产级闭源模型进行API边界探测；(2) 在开源因果语言模型上进行白盒受控验证。使用三轮种子平均皮尔逊相关系数、解析率、对数概率排名等多种指标进行量化评估。

Result: 顶级前沿LLM在Fama-French市场超额收益上达到r=0.97-0.99，五个兄弟因子偏差在25bps以内；美国失业率、CPI通胀和NOAA温度数据上也有类似高保真度。在新近发布的数据集上解析率下降至21-57%，但已回答月份的相关性仍保持约0.99。白盒实验复现了剂量-反应关系。Sonnet模型"日期到市场情绪"回归与真实Mkt-RF的相关性从r=0.74下降到r=0.02（残差化后）。单行系统提示防御可阻断99.8%的非自适应单轮后缀攻击，且对概念性和历史叙事性查询的效用损失接近于零。

Conclusion: 前沿LLM在数值基准测试中存在严重的记忆性回忆问题，常规的黑盒探测方法低估了这一通道；简单的系统提示防御策略可以有效缓解此类攻击，为未来更安全的模型评估提供了实践方向。

Abstract: Public numeric benchmarks appear in pretraining, so an evaluation that conditions on a date may be measuring memorized recall rather than out-of-sample skill. We introduce NumLeak, a measurement framework that combines API-boundary probes on production models with a white-box controlled validation on an open causal LM. Top-tier frontier LLMs recall the Fama-French market excess return at 3-seed pooled Pearson r=0.97-0.99 while staying within 0.15 within-25bps on the five sibling factors; comparable fidelity appears on U.S. unemployment, CPI inflation, and NOAA temperature. On a recent-release holdout, parse rate collapses to 21-57% but r stays at approximately 0.99 on months answered, the refuse-or-recall asymmetry a memorized channel predicts. The white-box experiment reproduces the dose-response, and logprob ranking detects memorization that open-ended generation misses, implying closed-API black-box probes understate the channel. A Sonnet "date to market-sentiment" regression that correlates with true Mkt-RF at r=0.74 collapses to r=0.02 once the model's own recall is residualized out. A one-line system-prompt defense blocks 99.8% of a non-adaptive single-turn suffix attack set at near-zero utility cost on conceptual and historical-narrative queries

</details>


### [32] [Calibrated Preference Learning: The Case of Label Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30447)
*Santo M. A. R. Thies,Viktor Bengs,Timo Kaufmann,Sebastian J. Vollmer,Eyke Hüllermeier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次形式化了概率标签排序中的校准问题，建立了包含全排序、子排序和Top-K排序的校准层次体系，证明了全排序校准蕴含其他但反之不成立，且子排序与Top-K校准不可比。实验发现流行模型校准不佳，且校准与RLHF奖励模型的基准准确率强相关但不完美，揭示了超越Top-1准确率的质量维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 校准对于可靠决策至关重要，已在分类和回归中得到广泛研究，但概率标签排序（预测标签集排序上的分布）尚未有正式的校准定义。简单将排序视为类别会忽略其结构，无法捕捉成对和Top-K预测等重要模态。

Method: 形式化定义了标签排序的校准概念，构建了覆盖全排序、子排序和Top-K排序的层次化校准体系，并通过理论证明与实证分析验证了各层次之间的关系。

Result: 全排序校准蕴含子排序和Top-K校准但反之不成立；子排序与Top-K校准不可比；流行标签排序模型校准较差，子排序与Top-K指标存在显著差异；校准与RLHF奖励模型的基准准确率强相关但不完美。

Conclusion: 校准捕捉了超越Top-1准确率的有意义质量维度，未来工作应研究校准不良的下游影响并开发校准方法。

Abstract: Calibration, the alignment of predicted probabilities with true outcome frequencies, is essential for reliable decision-making. While extensively studied for classification and regression, calibration has not been formally addressed for probabilistic label ranking, where the goal is to predict a distribution over orderings of a label set. Naively treating rankings as classes ignores their structure and fails to capture important modalities such as pairwise and top-k predictions. We formalize calibration for label ranking and develop a hierarchy of notions covering full rankings, sub-rankings, and top-k rankings. We prove that full-rank calibration implies the others but not conversely, and sub-ranking and top-k calibration are incomparable. Empirically, we find popular label ranking models are often poorly calibrated, with substantial differences between sub-ranking and top-k metrics. Applying our framework to RLHF reward models, we find that calibration correlates strongly but not perfectly with benchmark accuracy, suggesting it captures a meaningful quality dimension beyond top-1 accuracy. These findings motivate future work on understanding the downstream effects of miscalibration and developing methods to correct it.

</details>


### [33] [Bounded Behavioral Indistinguishability for Black-Box LLM Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30448)
*Munawar Hasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了有界行为不可区分性概念，用于评估黑盒大模型蒸馏效果，并通过实验证明语义相似度不足以衡量蒸馏质量，需要引入对抗性和类别感知的评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒LLM蒸馏评估仅关注输出语义相似性或任务一致性，但输出相似并不代表学生模型在行为上与教师模型不可区分，缺乏对行为层面一致性的形式化评估标准。

Method: 形式化定义了(ε,q,t,𝔸)-有界行为不可区分性，在Qwen和Llama教师-学生对上构建5000条提示的行为探测套件，使用对抗性判别器、成对类别分析和教师识别攻击等方法进行综合评估。
