IBP achieves, on average, 74% faster GNN training, 180% faster DLRM embedding lookup, and 24% faster LLM inference.

</details>


### [70] [OrcaRouter: A Production-Oriented LLM Router with Hybrid Offline-Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30736)
*Zhenghua Bao,Fengya Tian,Chris Zhang,Zhenjun Chen,Xile Ma,Yi Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OrcaRouter是一种生产级LLM路由系统，结合LinUCB上下文赌博机与混合离线-在线学习，在RouterArena排行榜上以72.08分位列第二，准确率75.54%，成本每千次查询1美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在能力和推理成本上各不相同，实际部署时面临一个关键问题：对于每个传入请求，应由哪个模型来处理？需要一种高效且经济的路由策略。

Method: 采用LinUCB上下文赌博机，利用词汇和句子嵌入特征；提出混合离线-在线学习协议：离线阶段通过评估每个候选模型获得全信息反馈并拟合岭回归器，在线阶段从离线参数初始化并可选择性地继续通过赌博机反馈学习。

Result: 在RouterArena公开排行榜上，OrcaRouter-Adaptive以72.08的竞技场分数排名第二，准确率75.54%，成本为每千次查询1.00美元。

Conclusion: OrcaRouter展示了一种兼顾性能与成本的生产级LLM路由方案，通过混合学习范式有效实现了对请求的智能分配。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models, each with distinct capabilities and inference costs, raises a practical deployment question: given an incoming request, which model should handle it? We present OrcaRouter, a production-oriented LLM router that combines a LinUCB-based contextual bandit over lexical and sentence-embedding features with a hybrid offline-online learning protocol. Offline, OrcaRouter obtains full-information feedback by evaluating each candidate model on a curated set of routing prompts, yielding a reward matrix used to fit one ridge regressor per arm. At deployment time, it initializes from these parameters and can optionally continue learning from bandit feedback, updating only the selected model's arm after observing its reward. At the time of our RouterArena submission (May 20, 2026), OrcaRouter-Adaptive ranked second on the public RouterArena leaderboard with an arena score of 72.08, achieving 75.54% accuracy at a cost of USD 1.00 per 1,000 queries.

</details>


### [71] [Chain-of-Thought and Compressed Looped Transformers: A Memory-Budget Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30757)
*Haozhou Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了三种测试时记忆机制：压缩隐状态循环、完整序列状态循环和思维链暂存器。研究发现压缩循环的记忆容量受限于循环状态大小，即使增加计算步数也无法处理需要大工作记忆的P完全问题，而思维链凭借可增长的外部暂存器能解决此类问题。指针追踪和联想召回实验验证了这一记忆预算观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究不同测试时计算范式（思维链提示与循环Transformer）中记忆机制的区别，特别是持久可变记忆作为测试时推理核心资源的作用。

Method: 从理论（计算复杂性）和实验（指针追踪、联想召回）两方面比较三种记忆体制：压缩隐循环、完整序列状态循环和思维链暂存器。

Result: 压缩循环受限于固定大小的循环状态，即使增加步数也无法解决P完全问题；完整序列状态循环因携带每位置状态而拥有更强记忆能力；任务表现取决于记忆预算是否匹配工作记忆需求。

Conclusion: 持久可变记忆是测试时推理的核心资源，不同记忆体制导致不同的推理能力上限，压缩循环本质上是一个小空间推理器。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought prompting and looped Transformers both give a fixed model more test-time computation, but they differ in what they remember. Chain-of-thought stores intermediate state in generated tokens that remain in the context, whereas a looped Transformer carries state through recurrent hidden activations. We argue that this persistent mutable memory is a central resource for test-time reasoning.
  We compare three memory regimes, the compressed latent loop, the full sequence-state loop, and the chain-of-thought scratchpad. Our main result shows that a compressed loop is limited by the size of its recurrent state. Running the loop longer adds computation but does not by itself create a growing scratchpad, so a loop with a small recurrent state remains a small-space reasoner even when run for many steps. Under a standard complexity assumption, such loops cannot decide problems that are P-complete under logspace reductions, whereas polynomial-length chain-of-thought can.
  The separation is specific to compressed loops, as full sequence-state loops carry state at every input position and live in a memory-rich regime closer to explicit scratchpads. Controlled pointer-chasing and associative-recall sweeps illustrate this memory-budget view, with performance sensitive to whether the persistent-state budget matches the task's working-memory demand.

</details>


### [72] [Smaller Models are Natural Explorers for Policy-Level Diversity in GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30789)
*Yiming Ren,Yiran Xu,Zicheng Lin,Chufan Shi,Yukang Chen,Dingdong Wang,Tianhe Wu,Junjie Wang,Yujiu Yang,Yu Qiao,Ruihang Chu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出S2L-PO（小到大策略优化）框架，利用同模型家族中较小模型固有的更高策略级多样性来训练大模型，通过渐进退火策略从离线小模型rollout过渡到大模型自采样，在数学推理任务上显著提升准确率并降低rollout计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GRPO主要通过注入token级随机性来增加rollout多样性，但这种方式会引入逐步噪声，导致轨迹不连贯。作者发现同模型家族中小模型天然具有更高的策略级多样性（pass@k指标更高），且这种多样性具有时间相关性、保持逻辑一致性，能为梯度估计提供结构化探索信号。

Method: 提出S2L-PO框架：1）利用固定的小模型作为自然探索器为大模型生成多样化rollout；2）设计渐进退火策略，从离线小模型rollout逐步过渡到大模型自身采样，在探索与利用之间取得平衡，避免因小模型容量限制导致的训练中期性能下降。

Result: 在多个数学推理基准上取得显著提升，例如在AIME 24上使用1.7B探索器引导8B模型获得+8.8%的准确率提升，同时减少了rollout计算开销。

Conclusion: S2L-PO通过利用小模型的策略级多样性替代token级噪声，结合渐进退火策略，实现了更快的收敛速度和更高的性能上限，验证了策略级多样性相比token级随机性在GRPO中的优势。

Abstract: We identify a new dimension for enhancing rollout diversity in Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) for LLMs. While GRPO relies on diverse rollouts, prevailing strategies primarily increase diversity by injecting more token-level randomness, which may introduce step-wise noise and lead to incoherent trajectories. We uncover that smaller models within the same model family inherently exhibit higher policy-level diversity, indicated by their superior pass@k relative to larger counterparts as sample counts increase. Unlike token-level noise, this diversity is temporally correlated, preserves logical consistency, and provides structured exploration signals for gradient estimation. We thus propose S2L-PO (Small-to-Large Policy Optimization), a framework that leverages fixed small models as natural explorers to train larger models. To balance exploration and exploitation, we design a progressive annealing strategy that transitions from offline small-model rollouts to the large learner's own sampling. This shift elegantly avoids mid-training performance drops caused by the small model's capacity limits, achieving faster convergence and unlocking a higher performance ceiling. S2L-PO improves accuracy on diverse mathematical reasoning benchmarks (e.g., +8.8% on AIME 24 using a 1.7B explorer to guide the 8B model) while reducing rollout compute.

</details>


### [73] [Conformal Reliability: A New Evaluation Metric for Conditional Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30807)
*Yachen Gao,Xinwei Sun,Yikai Wang,Ye Shi,Jingya Wang,Jianfeng Feng,Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于共形预测的可靠性分数（reliability score）作为条件生成模型的新评估指标，并开发了CReL框架以高效计算该指标，实验证明其在图像-文本和文本-图像任务中的有效性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件生成模型评估指标通常只评估单次输出，未能捕捉模型的不确定性和生成结果的变异性或潜在风险，缺乏考虑固有不确定性的可靠性评估指标。

Method: 提出基于共形预测的可靠性分数作为评估指标，衡量预测集在预设置信水平下的最差性能；开发CReL框架，包含预测集构建（保证覆盖率）和可靠性分数优化两个步骤，并给出覆盖率理论保证。

Result: 理论证明了覆盖率保证；实验表明该方法生成比现有方法更具信息量的预测集；在合成数据、图像到文本和文本到图像任务上验证了新指标的可解释性与计算框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的可靠性分数和CReL框架能有效评估条件生成模型的不确定性和风险，提供更具信息量的预测集，为生成模型的可靠性评估提供了新范式。

Abstract: Conditional generative models have recently achieved remarkable success in various applications. However, a suitable metric for evaluating the reliability of these models, which takes into account their inherent uncertainty, is still lacking. Existing metrics, which typically assess a single output, may fail to capture the variability or potential risks in generation. In this paper, we propose a novel evaluation metric called reliability score based on conformal prediction, which measures the worst-case performance within the prediction set at a pre-specified confidence level. However, computing this score is challenging due to the high-dimensional nature of the output space and the nonconvexity of both the metric function and the prediction set. To efficiently compute this score, we introduce Conformal ReLiability (CReL), a framework that can (i) construct the prediction set with desired coverage; and (ii) accurately optimize the reliability score within the constructed prediction set. We provide theoretical results on coverage and demonstrate empirically that our method produces more informative prediction sets than existing approaches. Experiments on synthetic data and the image-to-text and text-to-image tasks further demonstrate the interpretability of our new metric, and the validity and effectiveness of our computational framework. Source code can be found at https://ggc29.github.io/CReL/.

</details>


### [74] [IRIS: time-structured manifold projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30810)
*Brian Ondov,Chia-Hsuan Chang,Weipeng Zhou,Xingjian Zhang,Xueqing Peng,Yutong Xie,Huan He,Qiaozhu Mei,Hua Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IRIS是一种新的流形学习算法，能够同时在时间顺序和流形拓扑结构上对高维生物医学数据进行可视化布局，适用于scRNA-seq、比较宏基因组学和文献等多种动态数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流形学习算法（如t-SNE和UMAP）无法在布局中融入时间顺序信息，从而模糊了细胞类型或其他类别的动态变化过程。

Method: IRIS是一种新的流形学习算法，通过同时考虑时间顺序和流形拓扑结构来构建数据布局。

Result: IRIS能够可视化多种动态生物医学数据，包括单细胞RNA测序数据、比较宏基因组学数据和文献数据。

Conclusion: IRIS有效地解决了高维时序数据可视化中时间信息缺失的问题，为动态生物医学数据的分析提供了新的工具。

Abstract: High-dimensional biomedical data, such as cell-by-gene matrices, are increasingly generated temporally. However, Manifold Learning algorithms, like t-SNE and UMAP, cannot incorporate time-ordering in their layouts, obfuscating the dynamics of cell types or other classes. As a solution, we present IRIS, a new Manifold Learning algorithm that structures layouts both chronologically and by manifold topology. IRIS can visualize a wide range of dynamic biomedical data, including scRNA-seq, comparative metagenomics, and literature.

</details>


### [75] [Non-destructive Identification of Oyster Species is possible from Hyperspectral Images with Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30811)
*Ethan Kane Waters,Max Wingfield,Aiden Mellor,Paul Stewart,Iman Tahmasbian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用高光谱成像（950-2515nm）结合PLS-DA和CNN模型，快速无损区分黑唇岩牡蛎和悉尼岩牡蛎的方法。PLS-DA模型在左右壳瓣的分类准确率均达100%，优于CNN（83%和96%）。元素分析表明右壳外层碳氧含量差异与几丁质和糖蛋白组成相关，为物种鉴别提供了机理支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牡蛎物种鉴别对商业养殖和供应链可追溯性至关重要，但传统DNA分析方法具有破坏性且耗时，亟需一种快速、无损的检测方法。

Method: 采集156只活体黑唇岩牡蛎和悉尼岩牡蛎的高光谱图像（950-2515nm），分别对左右壳瓣的光谱反射率建立PLS-DA和CNN分类模型，采用蒙特卡洛交叉验证评估性能。同时利用电子显微镜测量壳瓣表面和截面的元素及矿物组成。

Result: PLS-DA模型对左右壳瓣的分类准确率中位数均为100%，优于CNN（左壳83%，右壳96%）。右壳分析显示BL有4层结构而SR仅2层；BL右壳外层富碳，SR富氧。透射分析表明光可透过壳瓣及边缘，光谱可能受另一壳瓣或软体组织影响。

Conclusion: 高光谱成像结合PLS-DA是一种有效的快速、无损牡蛎物种鉴别方法，碳氧含量差异对应的分子振动特征为分类提供了光谱学依据。

Abstract: Differentiating between oyster species is important for developing new commercial oyster species suited to production systems and is critical for traceability in seafood supply chains. Common methods, such as DNA profiling, are destructive and time consuming. The possibility of using hyperspectral imaging (HSI) for discriminating between Black-Lip rock (BL) and Sydney rock (SR) oysters was investigated. Live BL and SR samples (N = 156) were scanned with a HSI camera (950-2515nm). Partial Least Square Discriminant Analysis and Convolutional Neural Networks were trained with Monte Carlo Cross Validation to distinguish BL and SR oysters from the spectral reflectance of their left and rights valves. The PLS-DA model successfully distinguished between the species from both the left and right valves with a median test set classification accuracy of 100%, out performing the CNN with 83% and 96% respectively. Elemental and mineralogical composition in the surface and cross-section of oyster valves were measured with electron microscopy. Analysis of the right valve revealed a greater number of layers in BL compared to SR (4 vs 2). The concentrations of carbon and oxygen varied in the outer layer of the right valves, with BL being rich in carbon and SR being rich in oxygen. The variation in carbon and oxygen concentrations observed between BL and SR right valves may reflect differences in the relative abundance or composition of chitin and glycoproteins. This is supported by model-derived wavelength importance corresponding to vibrational modes of functional groups characteristic of these compounds. Transmittance analysis revealed that light was transmitted through the valves, around the valve edges, indicating that the spectral signatures may have been influenced by the other valve or the meat. Ultimately, the findings highlight an effective rapid, non-destructive methodology for oyster species.

</details>


### [76] [Learning Permutation-invariant Macroscopic Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30812)
*Zhichao Han,Mengyi Chen,Qianxiao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种置换不变的自编码器框架，用于从无序微观系统（如粒子系统）中学习低维宏观动态，通过重建质量分布而非逐点重建，并在多种物理系统中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动的宏观动力学建模方法通常假设微观自由度在输入中具有固定顺序，但许多实际系统（如粒子系统）的微观状态本质上是无序的（置换不变的），需要一种能够学习置换不变潜在表示的自动编码器框架。

Method: 采用置换不变的编码器，解码器设计为重建观测点处的质量分布（而非逐样本重建），然后联合学习观测量的宏观动力学与潜在状态。

Result: 在相互作用粒子系统的能量动力学、Lennard-Jones流体的混合动力学、以及聚合物在拉伸力场中的运动视频数据上，验证了该方法在学习宏观动态方面的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该置换不变自编码器框架能够有效地从无序微观系统中学习宏观动力学，为粒子系统等置换不变场景下的动力学建模提供了可行的数据驱动方案。

Abstract: Accurately modeling the macroscopic dynamics of high-dimensional microscopic systems is of broad interest across the sciences. Many data-driven approaches learn a low-dimensional latent state through an autoencoder trained for pointwise input reconstruction. These methods typically assume a fixed ordering of microscopic degrees of freedom in the input. However, in many settings, such as particle systems, the microscopic state is inherently unordered. This motivates an autoencoder framework that learns permutation-invariant latent representations. To this end, we adopt a permutation-invariant encoder and design the decoder to reconstruct the mass distribution centered at the observed points rather than per-sample reconstruction. We then jointly learn the macroscopic dynamics of the observables together with the latent states. We demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed method across a range of microscopic settings, including learning the energy dynamics in interacting particle systems, predicting mixing dynamics in Lennard-Jones fluids, and modeling the stretching dynamics from video data of polymers moving in an elongational force field.

</details>


### [77] [Unlearning in Diffusion Models: A Unified Framework with KL Divergence and Likelihood Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30825)
*Shervin Khalafi,Alejandro Ribeiro,Dongsheng Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于约束优化的扩散模型遗忘学习框架，通过KL散度和似然约束显式平衡遗忘效果与模型保留能力，并证明了非凸KL约束的强对偶性，实验表明该方法在遗忘-保留权衡上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型中的遗忘学习面临两个根本冲突的目标：移除不良数据/概念与保留预训练模型的效用。现有方法缺乏对这两个目标之间权衡的显式建模，需要一个原则性的框架来形式化这一冲突。

Method: 提出约束优化框架，将遗忘学习建模为最小化与预训练模型的偏差（目标函数），同时施加与遗忘分布显式分离的约束（约束条件）。具体构造了基于反向KL散度、前向KL散度和似然约束的三类优化问题，并利用强对偶性理论推导出最优解（遗忘目标），设计原始-对偶算法求解。

Result: 实验表明：KL约束方法在概念遗忘和数据遗忘任务中，遗忘-保留权衡优于基于权重的基线方法；基于似然的约束方法在保持相同遗忘效果的同时，能更好地保留应保留的概念。

Conclusion: 通过约束优化框架，可以显式建模遗忘与保留之间的权衡，且非凸KL约束问题仍具有强对偶性，从而获得可计算的最优解。该方法在概念遗忘和数据遗忘任务上均优于现有基线。

Abstract: Unlearning in diffusion models aims to remove undesirable data or concepts while preserving the utility of pretrained models -- two fundamentally conflicting objectives. We propose a principled constrained optimization framework that formulates unlearning as minimizing the deviation from a pretrained model, subject to explicit separation constraints from the unlearning distributions. Specifically, we formulate three constrained optimization problems based on reverse and forward KL divergences, and likelihood constraints. The first two generalize existing approaches for concept and data unlearning, while the third offers a novel and natural formulation for unlearning. Despite the nonconvexity of the KL constraints, we establish strong duality for all three problems, enabling us to explicitly characterize their optimal solutions as unlearning targets and develop primal-dual algorithms for each formulation. Experimental results demonstrate that our KL-constrained approach achieves superior retention-unlearning tradeoffs compared to weight-based baselines for concept and data unlearning, and that our likelihood-based approach matches unlearning effectiveness while better preserving retained concepts compared to baselines.

</details>


### [78] [Cross-Layer Subspace Coupling for LLM Compression: A Unifying Framework and Its Empirical Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30836)
*Snigdha Chandan Khilar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一种统一SVD压缩方法的理论优化虽然在权重重建误差上提升高达46%，但在实际任务中导致困惑度和准确率严重下降。作者通过机制分析发现，尽管束方法在数学上耦合了相邻层，但Transformer残差流在前向传播中实际上解耦了各层，因此逐层优化比跨层联合优化更重要。结论指出权重空间重建对于跨层压缩是 flawed objective，未来方法应聚焦逐层激活重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于SVD的大语言模型压缩方法（如SVD LLM和Basis Sharing）可以统一为一个优化问题，但缺乏对其有效性的深入验证与机制理解。

Method: 将现有SVD压缩方法统一为单一优化框架，进行数学证明和Pythia模型测试，并通过机制分析解释为何理论优化在实际任务中失效。

Result: 统一方法在权重重建误差上提升高达46%，但在下游任务中困惑度和准确率严重低于逐层SVD LLM基线。

Conclusion: 权重空间重建是跨层压缩的错误目标，未来方法应聚焦于逐层激活重建而非跨层权重优化。

Abstract: Recent SVD based compression methods for large language models like SVD LLM and Basis Sharing can be unified under one optimization problem. While mathematical proofs and tests on Pythia models show this unified approach improves weight reconstruction error by up to 46% percent it fails in practical tasks. Downstream metrics like perplexity and accuracy severely degrade compared to standard per layer SVD LLM. The authors explain this failure mechanistically. Although the bundle method mathematically couples adjacent layers the transformer residual stream actually decouples them during forward passes. Thus per layer optimality matters more than joint cross layer optimization. The paper concludes that weight space reconstruction is a flawed objective for cross layer compression and future methods must focus on per layer activation reconstruction instead.

</details>


### [79] [A Lecture Note on Offline RL and IRL, Part II: Foundations of Inverse Reinforcement Learning and Dynamic Discrete Choice Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30843)
*Enoch Hyunwook Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了结构计量经济学中的动态离散选择（DDC）模型与机器学习中的熵正则化逆强化学习（IRL）模型在数学上等价，系统梳理了从经典DDC识别理论到现代ML/IRL方法的演进脉络，并展示了Kang等人的经验风险最小化框架如何统一两类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管DDC和熵正则化IRL研究的是完全相同的概率模型，但两个领域长期独立发展，缺乏沟通。本文旨在建立两者间的等价性，梳理各自的方法论脉络与局限，为跨领域的理论融合与计算实践提供统一视角。

Method: 首先通过数学推导证明DDC与熵正则化IRL的等价性；然后系统回顾经典DDC的四条计算路径（Rust嵌套不动点、Hotz-Miller条件选择概率、Adusumilli-Eckardt的两种时序差分方法），并指出各自的局限（维度灾难、转移核估计、致命三元组、投影不动点偏差）；接着梳理现代ML/IRL方法（对抗IRL、占优匹配、IQ-Learn、离线ML-IRL），阐明各方法的实际目标函数及其识别能力；最后引入Kang等人的经验风险最小化框架，提供一个可梯度优化的离线IRL/DDC估计器。

Result: 证明了DDC与熵正则化IRL模型的等价性；厘清了经典DDC四条计算路径的适用边界与理论局限；揭示了现代ML/IRL方法各自的实际优化目标和识别特性；给出了Kang等人统一的经验风险最小化估计框架。

Conclusion: DDC和熵正则化IRL本质上是同一模型的两个名称，两个领域的方法各有优劣。经典DDC方法受限于维度诅咒和转移核估计等，现代ML/IRL方法则面临致命三元组等挑战。Kang等人的框架为两类方法的统一与实用化提供了新方向。

Abstract: In the forward reinforcement-learning problem, the reward is fixed and known; the learner is asked to find a good policy or value function. Here we turn the question around. Given offline data generated by an expert, can we recover the reward the expert was optimizing? This is the inverse reinforcement learning problem, and remarkably, two communities, structural econometricians studying dynamic discrete choice (DDC) and machine learners studying entropy-regularized IRL, have been working on exactly the same probabilistic model under different names. We begin by proving their equivalence. We then develop the classical identification result of Magnac and Thesmar and the classical computational paradigms that grew out of it: Rust's nested fixed-point algorithm, the conditional-choice-probability approach of Hotz and Miller, and the two temporal-difference approaches of Adusumilli and Eckardt: linear semi-gradient TD and approximate value iteration. Each route has its limits: dimensionality, transition-kernel estimation, the deadly triad, or projected fixed-point bias. We then walk through the modern ML/IRL strand: adversarial IRL, occupancy matching, IQ-Learn, and offline ML-IRL, deriving each method's actual objective and stating precisely what it does and does not identify. We close with the empirical-risk-minimization framework of Kang et al., which yields a gradient-based estimator for offline IRL/DDC.

</details>


### [80] [ForecastCompass: Guiding Agentic Forecasting with Adaptive Factor Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30858)
*Yurui Chang,Yongkang Du,Yuanpu Cao,Jinghui Chen,Lu Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FoCo是一个面向智能体预测的自适应因子记忆框架，通过分层任务分类、因子记忆和推理记忆组件，以及基于回溯分析的迭代记忆修正机制，提升了预测的准确性和校准度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆方法不适用于预测任务，通常存储过去的交互、反思或事实关联，缺乏对可复用预测因子和校准知识的显式表达。

Method: 构建分层预测任务分类法组织经验；维护因子记忆（捕捉可复用预测维度）和推理记忆（编码概率更新、不确定性处理和校准原则）两个互补组件；通过回溯分析作为学习信号，利用言语化记忆修正程序迭代更新记忆。

Result: 在Prophet Arena和FutureX数据集上，使用GPT-5-mini和Gemini-2.5-Flash进行实验，FoCo在概率准确性和校准度上均优于基线。

Conclusion: FoCo通过可迁移的预测知识积累，能够有效提升智能体在动态环境中的预测性能。

Abstract: Agentic forecasting is important for decision-making in dynamic environments, but it remains challenging because agents must reason from incomplete, time-limited evidence and produce calibrated probabilities before outcomes are resolved. Memory provides a natural mechanism for transferring experience from resolved forecasts to future prediction tasks. However, existing agent-memory methods are not tailored to forecasting, as they typically store past interactions, reflections, or factual associations without explicitly representing reusable predictive factors or calibration knowledge. We propose ForecastCompass (FoCo), an adaptive factor-based memory framework for agentic forecasting. FoCo organizes forecasting experience with a hierarchical forecasting-task taxonomy, enabling retrieval task-relevant forecasting knowledge. It maintains two complementary memory components: factor memory, which captures reusable predictive dimensions, and reasoning memory, which encodes probability updating, uncertainty handling, and calibration principles. Using retrospective analyses as learning signals, FoCo iteratively revises memory through a verbalized memory-revision procedure, enabling the agent to accumulate transferable forecasting knowledge over time. Experiments on Prophet Arena and FutureX with GPT-5-mini and Gemini-2.5-Flash show that FoCo improves both probabilistic accuracy and calibration.

</details>


### [81] [DARTS: Distribution-Aware Active Rollout Trajectory Shaping for Accelerating LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30859)
*Yujie Wang,Siwei Chen,Longzan Luo,Xinyi Liu,Xupeng Miao,Fangcheng Fu,Bin Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对强化学习中长尾响应分布导致的部署效率瓶颈，本文提出主动分布塑形范式，通过细粒度识别提示内长尾（无效冗长内容），结合分布感知轨迹采样和自适应冗余分配，在不影响模型性能的情况下实现最高1.77倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习系统在rollout阶段存在效率瓶颈，根源在于生成长尾响应分布。已有工作仅通过提示级尾部调度缓解影响，未从根本解决分布本身的问题。

Method: 1) 细粒度识别intra-prompt长尾分布，发现其由无效冗长内容构成；2) 提出主动分布塑形范式，将rollout分布向简洁性和确定性方向塑形；3) 设计分布感知轨迹采样机制，从冗余探索空间中选择轨迹；4) 提出自适应冗余分配方案，平衡塑形效果与系统效率。

Result: 在实验中，相比最先进的系统，该方法取得最高1.77倍加速，且不损害模型性能。

Conclusion: 通过主动重塑rollout分布而非被动调度尾部，能够从根本上解决长尾分布带来的开销问题，实现更高效的强化学习系统。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has become pivotal for improving model capabilities yet suffers from rollout efficiency bottlenecks due to the long-tail response length distribution. While existing works mitigate the impact of long tails via prompt-level tail scheduling, we focus on the root source of inefficiency: the distribution itself. Specifically, we characterize the long-tail distribution at a finer granularity, identifying intra-prompt long tails, and revealing that they frequently consist of ineffective verbosity. To address this, we propose a novel paradigm of active distribution shaping to shape the rollout distribution towards conciseness and certainty, thereby fundamentally resolving tail-induced overheads. We achieve this through a distribution-aware trajectory sampling mechanism, which selects trajectories from a redundant exploration space for each prompt, and an adaptive redundancy allocation scheme to maximize both shaping effectiveness and system efficiency. Experiments demonstrate significant acceleration over state-of-the-art systems by up to 1.77x without compromising model performance.

</details>


### [82] [GlucoFM: A Dual-Stream Foundation Model for Continuous Glucose Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30865)
*Zechen Li,Keerthana Natarajan,Weizhi Zhang,Menglian Zhou,Simon A. Lee,Yuwei Zhang,Maxwell A. Xu,Zeinab Esmaeilpour,Flora D. Salim,Mark Malhotra,Lindsey Sunden,Shwetak Patel,Yuzhe Yang,Ahmed A. Metwally*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GlucoFM是一个轻量级CGM基础模型，通过将血糖数据分解为慢速生理状态流和瞬时事件流，并在477名受试者的109,066小时数据上预训练，在7个临床预测任务中平均PR-AUC比最佳CGM专用模型高出4.1个点，尤其在糖尿病风险和β细胞功能障碍任务上表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时间序列和CGM基础模型通常将血糖轨迹编码为纠缠的单流序列，未能有效建模血糖动态中不同时间尺度的结构（慢速生理状态与瞬时事件），限制了表征的迁移能力。

Method: GlucoFM将不规则CGM记录对齐到24小时时间网格并保留观测掩码，将血糖动态分解为慢速生理状态流和瞬时事件流；采用两个互补预训练目标：掩码上下文潜在预测（融合每日表示）和时间动态预测（状态/事件流），在477名受试者、109,066小时无标签数据上预训练。

Result: 在4个队列、7个临床预测任务中，GlucoFM线性探测平均PR-AUC比最佳CGM专用模型高4.1个点；在所有糖尿病风险和β细胞功能障碍任务以及3/4的胰岛素抵抗任务上取得最优；跨数据集迁移和少样本适应表现最佳；多天聚合预测持续提升。

Conclusion: 生理感知的分解（慢速状态流+瞬时事件流）是可迁移CGM表示学习的有效归纳偏置，能显著提升下游临床预测性能。

Abstract: Continuous glucose monitoring (CGM) provides a dense view of daily metabolic physiology, yet existing generic time-series and CGM-specific foundation models often encode glucose traces as entangled single-stream sequences, leaving the distinct temporal structure of glycemic dynamics only implicitly modeled. We present GlucoFM, a lightweight CGM foundation model that aligns irregular recordings to a 24-hour chronological grid, preserves observation masks, and decomposes glucose dynamics into slow physiological state and transient event streams, capturing low-frequency glycemic baselines and short-term deviations that may reflect acute physiological responses or sensor artifacts. GlucoFM is pretrained on 109,066 hours of unlabeled CGM recordings from 477 subjects with two complementary objectives: masked contextual latent prediction over fused daily representations and temporal dynamics prediction over state and event streams. Across four diverse cohorts and seven clinical prediction tasks, GlucoFM achieves the strongest subject-disjoint linear-probing performance among evaluated baselines, improving average PR-AUC by 4.1 points over the best CGM-specific foundation model. Its gains are most pronounced on core metabolic outcomes, leading PR-AUC on all diabetes-risk and $β$-cell dysfunction tasks and on 3 of 4 insulin-resistance tasks. GlucoFM also achieves the best overall cross-dataset transfer performance and strong few-shot adaptation among evaluated methods, and consistent gains when aggregating multiple days for subject-level prediction, highlighting physiology-aware decomposition as an effective inductive bias for transferable CGM representation learning.

</details>


### [83] [Federated Variational Preference Alignment with Gumbel-Softmax Prior for Personalized User Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30873)
*Jabin Koo,Hoyoung Kim,Minwoo Jang,Jungseul Ok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对联邦学习中全局奖励模型平均化用户偏好（如有用性与无害性冲突）的问题，提出FedVPA-GP框架，通过联邦混合先验和正交损失，在保护隐私的同时解耦多样化偏好，实验表明在HH-RLHF数据集上显著优于单峰基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦对齐框架使用单一奖励模型，不可避免地平均化用户间相互冲突的偏好（如有用性 vs. 无害性），缺乏个性化能力。变分偏好学习（VPL）虽可个性化，但在去中心化场景下因本地数据稀缺和异质性导致后验坍塌。

Method: 提出FedVPA-GP框架：(1) 联邦混合先验（Federated Mixture Prior），让客户端利用聚合的人口分布作为动态先验以稳定变分推断；(2) 正交损失（Orthogonal Loss），在潜在空间中显式分离偏好原型。

Result: 在HH-RLHF数据集上，FedVPA-GP显著优于单峰基线，成功解耦冲突的用户意图，并支持动态偏好切换。

Conclusion: FedVPA-GP通过联邦混合先验和正交损失有效解决了去中心化场景下变分偏好学习的后验坍塌问题，实现了隐私保护下的个性化偏好对齐。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving pathway for aligning Large Language Models (LLMs); however, existing frameworks typically enforce a monolithic reward model, inevitably averaging out inherently conflicting user preferences (e.g., helpfulness vs. harmlessness). While Variational Preference Learning (VPL) offers a pathway to personalization, adapting it to decentralized settings presents a fundamental challenge: posterior collapse driven by severe local data scarcity and heterogeneity. In this paper, we propose Federated Variational Preference Alignment with Gumbel-Softmax Prior (FedVPA-GP), a framework designed to disentangle diverse preferences without compromising privacy. To stabilize variational inference, we introduce a Federated Mixture Prior that enables clients to leverage the aggregate population distribution as a dynamic prior. Furthermore, we incorporate an Orthogonal Loss that explicitly enforces the separation of preference prototypes in the latent space. Experiments on the HH-RLHF dataset demonstrate that FedVPA-GP significantly outperforms monolithic baselines, successfully disentangling conflicting user intents and enabling dynamic preference switching.

</details>


### [84] [Inverse Reinforcement Learning without an Optimal Demonstrator: A Feasible Reward Set Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30903)
*Kihyun Kim,Shripad Deshmukh,Nikos Vlassis,Jiawei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多个次优演示者的逆强化学习问题，提出了一种可行奖励集框架，通过编码每个演示者的次优性水平作为线性约束并取交集，理论证明了联合可行集随数据增加单调收缩，给出了新演示者严格收紧边界的确切条件，并建立了两个恢复保证。实验在表格网格世界和大语言模型微调中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准IRL假设单一最优演示者，但实际应用中数据常来自多个次优程度不同的不完美演示者，现有方法无法有效处理这种异质性次优性。

Method: 提出可行奖励集框架：为每个演示者将其声明的次优性水平编码为线性约束，然后对所有演示者的可行集取交集。设计了处理奖励歧义性的策略，并给出了适用于高维环境的带函数近似的离线算法。

Result: 理论上证明了联合可行集随数据单调收缩，给出了新演示者严格收紧集的确切条件，建立了两个恢复保证（一个依赖与最优占用的接近度，另一个只需足够覆盖且无近最优演示者）。实验验证了理论预测及相对于基线的优越性。

Conclusion: 该框架有效处理了多个异质性次优演示者的IRL问题，具有理论保障和实际可行性，适用于包括LLM微调在内的高维场景。

Abstract: Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) typically assumes demonstrations from a single optimal demonstrator, but in many applications data come from multiple imperfect demonstrators with heterogeneous suboptimality levels. We study reward learning in this setting through a feasible-reward-set framework: for each demonstrator, we encode its declared suboptimality level as a linear constraint and intersect the resulting feasible sets across demonstrators. Our theoretical analysis shows that the joint feasible set shrinks monotonically as data are added, and we give an exact characterization of when a new demonstrator strictly tightens it. We further establish two recovery guarantees for the feasible reward set of the ground-truth optimal demonstrator: one bound depends on closeness to the optimal occupancy, while the other requires only sufficient coverage and no near-optimal demonstrator. On the practical side, we introduce strategies to address the inherent reward ambiguity in the obtained reward set and provide an offline algorithm with function approximation for high-dimensional environments. Experiments in tabular grid-world and large language model (LLM) fine-tuning settings are consistent with the theoretical predictions and demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework over baselines.

</details>


### [85] [Welfare, Improvability, and Variance: A Principal-Agent Approach to Optimal Benchmark Item Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30916)
*Andreas Haupt,Justin Hartenstein,Anka Reuel,Mykel Kochenderfer,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将基准测试建模为多任务主从博弈，揭示了基准测试的福利损失由三个项目级要素共同决定：与规范性福利优先级的对齐程度、边际可改进性和性能方差。通过审计框架对OLMES项目进行排名，发现了其中帕累托劣势的项目。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI基准测试研究多关注污染、饱和度和构造规范不足等问题，但聚合方法（通常简单平均）受到的关注很少。本文旨在从理论上建模基准测试的福利损失，并提出相应的审计框架。

Method: 将基准测试建模为多任务主从博弈，推导出福利损失由三个项目级要素决定：与规范性福利优先级的对齐、边际可改进性和性能方差。据此设计审计框架，对OLMES项目沿三个维度进行排名，使用WORKBank衡量福利、EvoLM 4B套件衡量可改进性、PolyPythias 410M面板衡量方差。

Result: 在OLMES中识别出帕累托劣势的项目，表明现有均匀聚合方式存在可改进空间。

Conclusion: 基准测试的聚合方式不应简单平均，而应考虑项目级的福利对齐、可改进性和方差三个要素，以降低福利损失。

Abstract: AI benchmarks have well-documented limitations, with prior work examining contamination, saturation, and construct underspecification. Aggregation has received far less attention: benchmarks are typically summarized by uniformly averaging item-level scores, implicitly treating every test item as equally valuable. We model benchmarking as a multitask principal-agent game and show that the welfare loss from a benchmark is determined jointly by three item-level primitives: alignment with normative welfare priorities, marginal improvability, and performance variance. We translate the theory into an audit framework that ranks items along each of these three axes, and apply it to OLMES items using WORKBank for welfare, the EvoLM 4B suite for improvability, and the PolyPythias 410M panel for variance. The framework surfaces items that are Pareto-inferior within OLMES subject to a pro-worker welfare operationalization. All code is available at https://github.com/stair-lab/principal-agent-benchmarks.

</details>


### [86] [De-attribute to Forget for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30919)
*Xinyang Lu,Jiabao Pan,Rachael Hwee Ling Sim,See-Kiong Ng,Anthony Kum Hoe Tung,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DareU框架，将LLM遗忘问题转化为数据归因归零任务，通过强化学习降低模型生成内容对遗忘数据的归因得分，在平衡遗忘效果与模型效用方面优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM遗忘方法多依赖优化预测损失（如最大化遗忘集损失），易导致过度遗忘和模型效用下降，亟需更平衡的遗忘框架。

Method: 将遗忘目标重新框架为归零数据归因，提出DareU框架，采用强化学习更新LLM，降低其生成响应对遗忘数据所有者的归因得分，使用LLM分类器作为归因的高效近似。

Result: DareU在实现有效遗忘的同时，较好地平衡了遗忘质量与模型效用，优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于数据归因奖励的强化学习遗忘框架是解决LLM遗忘问题的有效途径，能够在遗忘效果和模型可用性之间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models (LLMs) has raised concerns on the use of inappropriate data for training, which has led to a growing interest in LLM unlearning. Many existing LLM unlearning approaches rely on optimizing prediction loss(es), such as maximizing the loss on the forget set, but often face critical issues like over-forgetting and poor model utility. To address them, this paper novelly frames the optimization objective for LLM unlearning as one of zeroing out data attribution instead. In particular, we propose the first LLM unlearning framework based on data attribution rewards called DareU that performs reinforcement learning to update the LLM by reducing the attribution score of its generated responses (i.e., de-attributing) to the forget data owners. Empirical evaluation using an LLM classifier as an efficient approximation of attribution shows that DareU outperforms existing baselines by achieving effective unlearning while balancing forget quality and model utility well.

</details>


### [87] [Unsupervised Diffusion Solver for Combinatorial Optimization via Combinatorial Adjoint Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30920)
*Shengyu Feng,Tarun Suresh,Yiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Combinatorial Adjoint Matching (CAM)，一种用于组合优化的无监督离散扩散求解器训练框架，通过将扩散模型形式化为连续时间马尔可夫链上的随机控制问题，并引入离散伴随动力学传播优化信号，在多种组合优化问题上取得与监督方法和传统求解器竞争的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散的组合优化神经求解器通常依赖监督训练，需要大量近优解作为标注数据，这限制了其实际应用。作者希望消除对监督信号的需求，实现无监督训练。

Method: 将扩散模型用于组合优化问题形式化为连续时间马尔可夫链上的随机控制问题，提出离散伴随动力学（discrete adjoint dynamics）来在离散生成轨迹中传播优化信号，并基于此构建了Combinatorial Adjoint Matching (CAM)无监督训练框架，提供结构化、低方差的轨迹级优化信号。

Result: CAM在多种组合优化问题上一致优于现有的无监督扩散基线方法，性能与强监督扩散求解器及传统求解器竞争。

Conclusion: 通过将伴随法扩展到离散组合域，实现了无监督训练离散扩散求解器，达到了与监督方法相媲美的性能。

Abstract: Diffusion-based neural solvers have shown strong promise for combinatorial optimization (CO), but existing methods typically rely on supervised training with large collections of near-optimal solutions. In this work, we extend adjoint-based trajectory optimization methods to discrete combinatorial domains. We formulate diffusion-based CO as a stochastic control problem over Continuous-Time Markov Chains and introduce discrete adjoint dynamics for propagating optimization signals through discrete generative trajectories. Building on this formulation, we propose Combinatorial Adjoint Matching (CAM), an unsupervised training framework for discrete diffusion solvers with structured and low-variance trajectory-level optimization signals. Empirically, CAM consistently outperforms existing unsupervised diffusion baselines and achieves performance competitive with strong supervised diffusion solvers and even traditional solvers across diverse combinatorial optimization problems. Our code is available at https://github.com/Shengyu-Feng/CAM.

</details>


### [88] [Local linear convergence of gradient methods for overparameterized Gaussian mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30936)
*Jingxing Wang,Vasileios Charisopoulos,Maryam Fazel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了过参数化下高斯混合模型的学习问题，提出了一种交替使用短步长梯度下降和长步长Polyak步长的优化方法，实现了局部线性收敛速度，表明过参数化的慢收敛并非固有挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过参数化对于避免虚假局部最优和实现全局恢复至关重要，但会显著降低局部收敛速度。本文旨在解决这一矛盾，证明过参数化的慢收敛并非不可克服的内在困难。

Method: 提出一种交替方法：先执行若干"短"梯度下降步以逼近损失景观中一个缓慢增长的流形，然后执行"长"Polyak步长以缩小到最小化子的距离。该方法利用了Polyak步长在该流形上能几何级降低损失的特性。

Result: 对于满足一定混合权重假设的模型，该方法实现局部线性收敛；对于任意权重的混合模型，该方法收敛到接近最优的解（达到自然 misspecification 阈值）。

Conclusion: 过参数化导致的慢收敛并非固有挑战，通过利用损失景观的有利结构可以克服。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning Gaussian mixture models under overparameterization. Prior work has shown that while overparameterization is essential for avoiding spurious local optima and enables global recovery of the ground-truth model using the gradient-EM (expectation-maximization) algorithm, it can dramatically slow down the local rate of convergence. Under certain assumptions on the mixture weights, we show that a standard divergence measure minimized by statistical learning procedures possesses a manifold of slow growth on which the well-known Polyak stepsize reduces the loss geometrically, and design a gradient-based method that converges to minimizers at a locally linear rate. Additionally, we show that our method converges to nearly optimal solutions -- up to a natural misspecification threshold -- for mixtures with arbitrary weights. At a high level, the method alternates between several "short" gradient descent steps that approach the manifold and "long" Polyak steps that contract the distance to minimizers. Our results suggest that slow convergence is not an intrinsic challenge of overparameterization, but can be overcome by exploiting the favorable structure of the loss landscape.

</details>


### [89] [Spectral Anatomy of Quantum Gaussian Process Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30952)
*Jian Xu,Chao Li,Guang Lin,Yuning Qiu,Delu Zeng,John Paisley,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现量子高斯过程（QGP）中看似无关的两个现象——HHL回归指数加速被否定与量子核后验病态——均由同一量控制：核Gram矩阵的归一化谱熵 S(K)/log n。该诊断在多种量子/经典核族上表现一致，并在IBM Heron硬件上以低误差实现迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥合量子高斯过程中两个看似独立的最新发现（HHL回归的指数加速被否定、量子核后验病态破坏贝叶斯优化），揭示其底层统一机理。

Method: 理论上证明谱熵与Nyström近似误差的柯西-施瓦茨尾界、Bach自由度方差收缩恒等式、以及目标最优熵与目标在核本征基中的本征维度的关系。实验上在多种核（硬件高效、matchgate、IQP、RBF/Matérn/RFF/深度核）以及IBM Heron量子硬件上验证诊断的泛化性和迁移性。

Result: 所有核族在反量子化、ECE和方差收缩面板上坍缩到相同的S/log n曲线。诊断从模拟器迁移到IBM Heron硬件时中位绝对误差3.2%，均值5.2%（n_q=4）；在第二个Heron后端均值误差2.7%；扩展到n_q=6均值误差1.7%。未使用任何误差缓解技术。

Conclusion: 谱熵是量子高斯过程的统一诊断量，能同时解释去量子化趋势、校准性能和方差收缩行为，且在真实量子硬件上可可靠迁移。

Abstract: Two recent results have reshaped quantum Gaussian processes (QGPs). On the one hand, \citet{lowe2025assessing} rule out the exponential speedups claimed by HHL-based QGP regression in the typical, well-conditioned regime; on the other, an independent line of work shows that highly expressive quantum kernels suffer posterior pathologies that break Bayesian optimization. We show that these seemingly unrelated phenomena are governed by the same quantity: the normalized spectral entropy $S(K)/\log n$ of the kernel Gram matrix. We prove a Cauchy--Schwarz tail bound on Nyström approximation error, a finite-sample variance-contraction identity in terms of Bach's degrees of freedom $d_σ(K)$, and a characterization of the \emph{target-dependent} optimal entropy via the intrinsic dimension of the target in the kernel eigenbasis. Empirically, the diagnostic is kernel-agnostic: hardware-efficient, matchgate, IQP \emph{and} RBF/Matérn/RFF/deep-kernel families all collapse onto identical $S/\log n$ curves on dequantization, ECE, and variance-contraction panels. The NLL sweet spot lives at high entropy for smooth targets and at low entropy for band-limited quantum-data targets. The diagnostic transfers from simulator to IBM Heron hardware with median absolute error $3.2\%$ and mean $5.2\%$ in $S/\log n$ across $24$ configurations at $n_q = 4$, with matchgate and IQP within $5\%$ mean and a single HE configuration returning a $30\%$ outlier that drops to $0.5\%$ on rerun (attributed to calibration drift); the same diagnostic transfers to a second Heron backend (mean error $2.7\%$) and to a $n_q = 6$ scale-up on the original backend (mean error $1.7\%$). No error mitigation is applied throughout.

</details>


### [90] [Revisiting Zeroth-Order Hessian Approximation: A Single-Step Policy Optimization Lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30960)
*Junbin Qiu,Zhaowei Hong,Renzhe Xu,Yao Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ZoVH，一个零阶Hessian估计的统一框架，通过策略优化视角推导出低方差的Hessian、正则化逆及逆Hessian-梯度乘积估计器，并具有理论最优基线和查询复用策略，在理论上保证了无偏性、方差最优性和收敛性，实验验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维设置下，现有的零阶Hessian估计方法难以同时获得完整的、低方差的Hessian及其逆的估计器，这限制了其在双层优化、贝叶斯推断等任务中的应用效果。

Method: 将零阶Hessian近似重新解释为单步策略优化的Hessian，建立理论等价性；在此基础上提出ZoVH套件，包含：(1) 理论推导的最优基线以最小化方差，(2) 查询复用策略利用历史函数值提升样本效率。

Result: 严格的数学证明证实了Hessian估计器的无偏性、基线方差最优性，给出了整套估计器的误差界和曲率感知ZO算法的收敛保证；实验表明ZoVH在估计精度和收敛性能上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: ZoVH通过策略优化视角统一了零阶Hessian估计理论，提供了一套理论上最优、实际高效的方差缩减估计器，为导数自由优化中的曲率信息利用提供了新范式。

Abstract: Accurate Zeroth-Order (ZO) Hessian estimation is a cornerstone of derivative-free methods, essential for tasks such as bilevel optimization, Bayesian inference, and uncertainty quantification. However, obtaining a complete suite of low-variance estimators for the Hessian and its inverse in high-dimensional settings remains a significant challenge. To address this, we propose a unified framework that reinterprets ZO Hessian approximation through the lens of single-step Policy Optimization (PO). This perspective establishes a theoretical equivalence between general ZO Hessian estimators and the Hessian of a smoothed PO objective, unifying distinct classical randomized estimators as specific instances of baseline selection. Building on this foundation, we introduce ZoVH, a comprehensive suite of variance-reduced estimators for the full Hessian matrix, its regularized inverse, and the bias-corrected inverse Hessian-gradient product. ZoVH leverages two key techniques: (1) a unique optimal baseline derived to provably minimize variance, and (2) a query reuse strategy that incorporates historical function queries to enhance sample efficiency without inflating costs. Our rigorous theoretical analysis confirms the unbiasedness of the Hessian estimator, validates the variance optimality of our baseline, provides error bounds for the entire ZoVH suite, and establishes convergence guarantees for the resulting curvature-aware ZO algorithm. Extensive empirical results validate our theoretical findings, demonstrating that ZoVH achieves superior estimation accuracy and convergence performance in real-world applications. Code is available at https://github.com/Qjbtiger/ZoVH

</details>


### [91] [Learning Multi-Agent Coordination via Sheaf-ADMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31005)
*Jeffrey Seely,Bartłomiej Cupiał,Llion Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可微分的多智能体协调优化框架，通过将输入分解为重叠的局部视图，每个智能体求解由神经编码器参数化的凸子问题，并利用交替方向乘子法（ADMM）和细胞层约束实现智能体间的协调。通过展开优化过程进行反向传播，联合训练多智能体系统的所有组件。在迷宫路径规划、图像分类和数独任务上评估，局部视图分解在MNIST上相比标准CNN提升了分布偏移鲁棒性，在数独上相比参数匹配的MPNN基线显著提高了求解率。ADMM结构暴露了原始、共识和对偶状态变量，使得协调动态可直接分析和干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有消息传递架构在处理多智能体协调时存在黑箱特性，缺乏可解释性和可干预性，且难以处理异构智能体间的共识问题。作者希望通过引入可微分的优化框架来克服这些限制。

Method: 将输入分解为重叠的局部视图，每个视图由独立智能体处理；智能体求解由神经编码器参数化的凸子问题；通过细胞层（cellular sheaf）指定智能体间的一致性约束，支持异构共识；使用ADMM展开优化过程，并通过反向传播联合训练所有组件。

Result: 在迷宫路径规划、图像分类和数独任务上验证了有效性；在MNIST上，局部视图分解相比标准CNN增强了分布偏移鲁棒性；在数独上，优化驱动的结构相比参数匹配的MPNN基线获得更高的求解率。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将多智能体协调建模为可微分的优化过程，不仅提升了任务性能，还通过ADMM结构提供了可解释的协调动态（原始、共识、对偶状态变量），使得协调过程可直接分析和干预，这是传统消息传递架构不具备的特性。

Abstract: We present a differentiable optimization framework for multi-agent coordination. An input is decomposed into overlapping local views, each processed by an agent that solves a convex subproblem parameterized by a neural encoder. Agents coordinate through the Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) with inter-agent constraints specified by a cellular sheaf. The sheaf specifies which aspects of neighboring solutions must agree, allowing for heterogeneous notions of global consensus. Backpropagating through the unrolled optimization jointly trains all components of the multi-agent system. We evaluate on maze pathfinding, image classification, and Sudoku, where agents with individually insufficient local views learn to coordinate to produce correct global outputs. On MNIST, the local-view decomposition yields improved robustness to distribution shifts relative to a standard CNN. On Sudoku, the optimization-derived structure yields markedly higher solve rates than parameter-matched MPNN baselines. Finally, the ADMM structure exposes distinct primal, consensus, and dual state variables, opening the coordination dynamics to direct analysis and intervention -- a property unavailable in standard message-passing architectures.

</details>


### [92] [DEM: A Distilled Explanation Model for Interpretable Anomaly Detection in Physiological Sensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31007)
*Jyotirmoy Singh,Anushka Roy,Shreea Bose,Chittaranjan Hota*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种三阶段玻璃箱框架DEM（蒸馏解释模型），通过将梯度提升专家的非线性知识蒸馏到基于线性基线残差的可解释决策树中，在生理传感器异常检测任务上实现了高精度（AUC达0.9964）与可解释性的统一，同时推理速度比SHAP快1235倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生理传感器异常检测需要高预测精度和临床可解释性，但现有方法要么是黑盒模型（高精度但不可解释），要么依赖SHAP/LIME等事后解释方法（近似而非真正的可解释性），缺乏一个既能提供透明预测又能保证解释可信度的框架。

Method: 提出三阶段玻璃箱框架DEM：第一阶段构建线性基线模型；第二阶段训练XGBoost梯度提升专家模型学习残差；第三阶段将专家模型的非线性知识蒸馏到可解释的决策树中，该决策树在相对于线性基线的残差上操作。同时引入蒸馏保真度指标量化解释树捕获专家模型非线性贡献的忠实程度。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV、WESAD、eICU和SmartNet四个数据集上，临床上下文异常检测AUC达0.9964，可穿戴压力检测AUC达0.9047；推理速度0.17ms/1000样本，比SHAP快1235倍；消融实验证实XGBoost蒸馏步骤比朴素残差拟合有可测量的增益；深度敏感性分析展示了用户可控的准确率-可解释性权衡。

Conclusion: DEM实现了预测即解释的设计理念，其解释不是近似而是预测本身，且通过蒸馏保真度指标提供了解释可信度的量化度量，适合实时生理监测场景。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in physiological sensor data from Wireless Body Area Networks (WBANs) can be caused by sensor faults, network disruptions, or missing data, leading to false alarms. Hence, it demands both high predictive accuracy and clinically interpretable explanations. Existing approaches rely either on black-box models that achieve strong performance but offer no transparency, or on post-prediction explanation methods such as SHAP and LIME. In this paper, we propose the Distilled Explanation Model (DEM), a three-stage glass-box framework that distills the non-linear knowledge of a gradient boosting expert into an interpretable decision tree operating on residuals relative to a linear baseline, so that the explanation is not an approximation but the prediction itself. DEM introduces a novel distillation fidelity metric that quantifies how faithfully the explanation tree captures the expert model's non-linear contribution, providing a principled measure of explanation trustworthiness absent from prior interpretable models. Evaluated across four physiological datasets, including MIMIC-IV, WESAD, eICU, and an in-house SmartNet WBAN corpus, DEM achieves an AUC of 0.9964 on clinical contextual anomaly detection and 0.9047 on wearable stress detection while producing human-readable if-then rules at a controllable depth. Inference requires 0.17ms per 1000 samples, rendering DEM 1235x faster than SHAP-based post-hoc explanation and suitable for real-time physiological monitoring. Ablation studies confirm that the XGBoost distillation step provides measurable gains over naive residual fitting, and depth-sensitivity analysis demonstrates an explicit, user-controlled accuracy-interpretability trade-off unique to DEM among existing intrinsically interpretable models.

</details>


### [93] [An Efficient and Scalable Graph Condensation with Structure-Preserving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31016)
*Yulin Hu,Fuyan Ou,Ye Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种高效、可扩展且保持结构的图压缩方法SP-ESGC，通过解耦设计（节点压缩与结构生成分离），利用热核特征传播、混合聚类和预训练边预测器，在保证高计算效率和跨GNN架构泛化能力的同时实现精确图压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图压缩方法存在两个主要问题：1）耦合优化导致计算效率低下；2）跨GNN架构泛化能力差。因此需要一种高效、可扩展且结构保持的图压缩方法。

Method: SP-ESGC采用解耦设计，分为三步：1）利用热核特征传播（基于谱图理论的扩散）生成节点表示；2）设计新型混合聚类策略从节点表示中提取判别性的类内质心；3）使用预训练边预测器从原始图中推断可迁移的结构模式，生成准确的合成图。

Result: 在真实图数据集上的实验表明，SP-ESGC实现了精确的图压缩，同时计算效率显著提升，并且在不同GNN架构上具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: SP-ESGC通过解耦节点压缩与结构生成，有效解决了现有GC方法的计算效率低和泛化性差的问题，为资源受限场景下的GNN部署提供了一种高效、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Graph condensation (GC) is pivotal for enabling Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) deployment in resource-constrained scenarios by compressing large-scale graphs into compact synthetic counterparts. Existing GC methods commonly suffer from computational inefficiency due to coupled optimization as well as encountering poor generalization across GNN architectures. To address these challenges, this study proposes an Efficient and Scalable Graph Condensation with Structure-Preserving (SP-ESGC), which possesses a decoupled design that separates node condensation from graph structure generation. Specifically, it first employs heat kernel feature propagation to generate node representation via spectral graph theory-inspired diffusion. Further, a novel hybrid clustering strategy is designed to extracts discriminative intra-class centroids from the node representation. Finally, a pre-trained edge predictor infers transferable structural patterns from the original graph, ensuring accurate synthetic graph generation. Extensive experiments on real-world graph datasets demonstrate that the proposed SP-ESGC implementes a precise GC with significantly high computational efficiency. Moreover, SP-ESGC also generalizes well across diverse GNN architectures.

</details>


### [94] [Annealed Softmax Greedy in Many-Armed Bayesian Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31034)
*William Overman,Mohsen Bayati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在贝叶斯多臂伯努利赌臂问题中，若先验满足β-正则条件（β=1），退火softmax贪心策略可达到近最优的Õ(√T)贝叶斯遗憾，为RLVR/GRPO等不显式建模不确定性的策略优化方法提供了理论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR和GRPO等基于群体的策略优化方法通过采样多个完整结果并增加高奖励结果的概率来更新策略，但并未显式追踪认知不确定性。本文试图解释为什么这种忽视不确定性的更新方式仍然有效。

Method: 分析退火softmax（玻尔兹曼）策略在贝叶斯多臂伯努利赌臂问题中的表现。策略根据经验平均奖励的softmax选择动作。假设先验满足线性上尾条件（β-正则性的β=1情况），即存在大量接近最优的动作。

Result: 退火softmax贪心策略达到贝叶斯遗憾Õ(m + T/m)，当动作数m = Θ(√T)时达到Õ(√T)，这是近最优的遗憾率。但在动作数较少时可能遭受线性遗憾。

Conclusion: β-正则性下，大量动作的经验均值接近最优值，因此softmax采样非最优动作时往往也是接近最优的，而非明显较差的。这在结构上与RLVR类似：基础策略以不可忽略的概率生成正确完成，起到了β-正则性的作用。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) and group-based policy optimization methods such as GRPO update a stochastic policy by sampling multiple completions per prompt and increasing the policy's probability on those with higher reward, regularized by a KL penalty toward a reference policy. These updates do not include explicit mechanisms that track epistemic uncertainty. This paper studies a stylized explanation for why such uncertainty-agnostic updates can nevertheless be effective. We analyze an annealed softmax (Boltzmann) policy that selects actions according to a softmax of empirical mean rewards in a many-armed Bayesian Bernoulli bandit. Under a linear upper-tail condition on the prior (the $β=1$ case of $β$-regularity), which implies an abundance of near-optimal arms, we prove that annealed softmax greedy achieves Bayes regret $\tilde{O}(m + T/m)$, and in particular $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$ when the number of arms scales as $m = Θ(\sqrt{T})$. This is the near-optimal Bayes regret rate in this regime, attained also by empirical-mean greedy. Under $β$-regularity, many arms maintain empirical means close to the optimum throughout learning, so when softmax samples an arm other than the empirically best, that arm tends to be another near-optimal one rather than a clearly inferior one. By contrast, with a small number of arms, the same kind of softmax policy can suffer linear regret. The result also provides a structural analogy to RLVR, where a base policy with a non-negligible probability of producing a correct completion plays the role of $β$-regularity.

</details>


### [95] [UniRTL: Unifying Code and Graph for Robust RTL Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31040)
*Yi Liu,Hongji Zhang,Lei Chen,Mingxuan Yuan,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UniRTL是一个多模态预训练框架，通过联合利用RTL代码和控制数据流图(CDFG)学习统一的硬件设计表示，在性能预测和代码检索任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RTL表示学习方法通常依赖单一数据模态（仅RTL代码或仅图结构），限制了表达能力和泛化性。RTL的CDFG提供完整的结构信息，而代码显式编码语义和功能信息，整合这些互补模态对于全面理解RTL设计至关重要。

Method: 提出UniRTL多模态预训练框架：1) 通过互掩码建模实现代码与CDFG的细粒度对齐；2) 采用分层训练策略，包含预训练的图感知分词器，以及先对齐文本（功能摘要）与代码、再集成图结构的分阶段对齐方法。

Result: 在性能预测和代码检索两个下游任务的多项设置下，UniRTL始终优于先前方法，被验证为更鲁棒且更强大的硬件设计自动化基础模型。

Conclusion: 联合利用代码和CDFG多模态信息进行表示学习，能够显著提升对RTL设计的理解能力，为硬件设计自动化提供了更优的基础框架。

Abstract: Developing effective representations for register transfer level (RTL) designs is crucial for accelerating the hardware design workflow. Existing approaches, however, typically rely on a single data modality, either the RTL code or its associated graph-based representation, limiting the expressiveness and generalization ability of the learned representations. For RTL, the control data flow graph (CDFG) offers a comprehensive structural representation that preserves complete information, while the code modality explicitly encodes semantic and functional information. We argue that integrating these complementary modalities is essential for a thorough understanding of RTL designs. To this end, we propose UniRTL, a multimodal pretraining framework that learns unified RTL representations by jointly leveraging code and CDFG. UniRTL achieves fine-grained alignment between code and graph through mutual masked modeling and employs a hierarchical training strategy that incorporates a pretrained graph-aware tokenizer and staged alignment of text (i.e., functional summary) and code prior to graph integration. We evaluate UniRTL on two downstream tasks, performance prediction and code retrieval, under multiple settings. Experimental results show that UniRTL consistently outperforms prior methods, establishing it as a more robust and powerful foundation for advancing hardware design automation.

</details>


### [96] [Best-Arm Identification-Based Trust Region Selection for Bayesian Optimization on Multimodal Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31050)
*Nobuo Namura,Sho Takemori*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种轨迹感知的贝叶斯优化框架，结合最优臂识别与信任区域方法，通过预测多个局部优化器的最终性能并逐步淘汰次优候选，加速多模态问题的全局优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准高斯过程贝叶斯优化在复杂多模态或高维问题上性能下降，信任区域BO虽能缓解但区域选择效率有待提高。

Method: 将信任区域选择建模为多臂老虎机问题，利用轨迹感知框架外推多个局部优化器的优化轨迹以预测最终性能，并通过最优臂识别逐步消除次优候选。

Result: 理论上证明了在温和假设下，所提方法比传统BO更快收敛到全局最优；实验在合成和真实基准上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: BAI引导的信任区域BO能有效提升多模态优化问题的求解效率。

Abstract: Gaussian process-based Bayesian optimization (BO) is a popular approach for expensive black-box optimization, but its performance often degrades on complex multimodal or high-dimensional problems. Trust region-based BO mitigates this issue by focusing on local regions, and recent studies suggest that selecting an effective region can be formulated as a multi-armed bandit problem. We propose a trajectory-aware framework that integrates best-arm identification (BAI) with trust region-based BO to efficiently solve multimodal optimization problems. Our method extrapolates the optimization trajectories of multiple locally initialized optimizers to predict their final performance and progressively eliminates suboptimal candidates via BAI. We theoretically show that the proposed BAI-guided BO converges faster to the global optimum than conventional BO under mild assumptions, and demonstrate its effectiveness through extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks.

</details>


### [97] [Learning to Bid in FCR Markets: A Best-of-Both-Worlds Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31070)
*Marius Potfer,Cheng Wan,Pierre Gruet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究欧洲频率 containment 储备（FCR）市场的 bidding 问题，将多国 FCR 清算问题转化为重复多单位统一价格拍卖，并采用 Best-of-Both-Worlds 组合半强盗算法，在随机环境下实现对数伪遗憾，在对抗环境下达到 O(√T) 遗憾，历史数据回测显示该学习方法在稳定产品上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FCR 市场中柔性提供者面临竞标信息隐蔽、仅能观察到清算价格和成交数量的部分反馈，导致竞价决策困难，需要一种仅基于标准市场反馈的在线学习 bidding 策略。

Method: 将多国 FCR 清算问题重新表述为针对内生反对出价向量的重复多单位统一价格拍卖，进而转化为在线学习问题；采用 Best-of-Both-Worlds 组合半强盗算法，该算法仅需清算价格和成交数量作为输入，在随机和对抗环境下均具备理论保证。

Result: 在随机环境下实现对数伪遗憾，在对抗环境下实现 O(√T) 遗憾；合成实验验证了理论缩放关系；欧洲 FCR 历史数据回测显示该方法在稳定产品上表现优于基线，而 EXP3 类基线在强非平稳场景下更安全。

Conclusion: 基于学习的 FCR 市场 bidding 在理论上是有根据的，且当学习规则与产品层面的市场稳定性匹配时在实践中具有竞争力。

Abstract: Bidding in the European Frequency Containment Reserve (FCR) market is challenging for flexibility providers because competing offers are hidden and bidders observe only partial feedback form the market, such as, clearing price and awarded quantity. For a participant active in a single country, we show that the multi-country FCR clearing problem can be recast as a repeated multi-unit uniform-price auction against an endogenous vector of opposing bids. This reformulation yields an online learning problem and allows us to adapt a Best-of-Both-Worlds combinatorial semi-bandit algorithm implementable from this standard market feedback. The resulting bidder achieves logarithmic pseudo-regret in stochastic environments and $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T})$ regret in adversarial ones. Synthetic experiments confirm the expected scaling, and backtests on historical European FCR data show competitive performance in practice: the method performs especially well on stable products, while EXP3-type baselines can be safer under stronger non-stationarity. Overall, the results show that learning-based bidding in FCR markets is theoretically grounded and practically useful when the learning rule matches product-level market stability.

</details>


### [98] [Subspace-Decomposed JEPAs: Disentangling Progression and Content in Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31111)
*Lucas Thil,Jesse Read,Rim Kaddah,Guillaume Doquet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SD-JEPA，将JEPA潜变量分解为正交的进步子空间（低维、角度编码）和内容子空间（高维、SIGReg正则化），两者抗坍缩力作用于不同坐标从而加性组合。该方法在控制基准上优于LeWM基线，且1D角坐标可作为场景感知罗盘，比预测误差标量更好地分离惊喜与语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准JEPA学习紧凑潜世界模型但未指定任何潜变量坐标编码任务进展，缺乏显式的任务进度表征。

Method: 将JEPA潜变量分解为两个正交子空间：低维进步子空间（余弦间隔三元组损失塑造）和高维内容子空间（SIGReg正则化）；两个抗坍缩力作用于不相交的坐标从而加性组合。

Result: SD-JEPA在大多数控制基准上优于LeWM基线，在Push-T上优于最强非LeWM JEPA基线。1D角坐标可量化任务进度（前进/后退/异常事件定位），|Δθ_t|在40个held-out立方体情节中语义事件定位的pooled AUROC提升高达+0.18（97.5%每集胜率）。8维进步子空间（占潜变量4.2%）解释72-95%的任务进度方差。

Conclusion: 显式分解潜空间为进度与内容子空间可提升JEPA世界模型性能，同时获得可解释的、场景感知的进度罗盘，比传统预测误差更好地分离惊喜事件与其语义含义。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) learn compact latent world models by predicting future embeddings, but no single coordinate of the latent is designated to encode task progression. We carve the JEPA latent into two orthogonal subspaces with disjoint roles: a low-dimensional progression subspace shaped by a cosine-margin triplet loss, and a high-dimensional content subspace regularised by the existing SIGReg objective of LeWM. We prove that the two anti-collapse forces act on disjoint coordinates, so they compose additively rather than competing on the same dimensions. Our method, SD-JEPA improves over the LeWM baseline on the majority of its control benchmarks at matched compute, and outperforms the strongest non-LeWM JEPA baseline on Push-T; a subspace-ablation falsifier confirms the split is the load-bearing ingredient. Beyond planning, the resulting 1-D angular progression coordinate functions as a scene-aware compass on the latent. It advances with task progress, regresses when the agent backtracks, and under controlled perturbations both spikes and relocalises to a semantically appropriate new task-phase sector, separating the moment of surprise from its meaning in a way that prediction-error scalars cannot. Three quantitative tests back this up: $|Δθ_t|$ outperforms the standard latent-prediction-error surprise at localising semantic events on 40 held-out cube episodes by up to +0.18 pooled AUROC (97.5% per-episode win rate at $\pm 1$-step tolerance); a within-episode linear probe across all four environments (40 episodes per env) shows the 8-dimensional progression subspace (4.2% of the latent) explains 72-95% of task-progress variance..

</details>


### [99] [Scalable Bayesian Inference for Nonlinear Conservation Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31127)
*Tim Weiland,Philipp Hennig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于高斯过程先验的数值保守方法，用于非线性守恒律的不确定性感知模拟，通过稀疏近似技术扩展到大规模正反问题，在正向模拟中提供结构化不确定性量化，在逆问题中秒级恢复非参数源场后验，优于需要分钟级计算的神经基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性守恒律在科学和工程中至关重要，但实际应用中常受稀疏或噪声测量等不确定性影响，导致经典数值方法和深度学习方法难以恰当处理不适定问题。

Method: 将经典数值方法框架化为高斯过程先验下的贝叶斯推断，利用稀疏近似技术实现大规模正反问题的高效求解，同时保持数值守恒性。

Result: 正向模拟继承经典求解器精度并提供结构化不确定性量化；逆问题在秒级内恢复非参数源场后验，优于需要数分钟的神经基线方法（仅提供点估计且精度更低）。

Conclusion: 所提出的不确定性感知数值保守方法有效解决了非线性守恒律中的不适定问题，兼顾了经典方法的准确性与贝叶斯推断的不确定性量化能力。

Abstract: Nonlinear conservation laws are at the heart of many of the most important dynamical systems in science and engineering. In practical applications, such systems are often subject to various sources of uncertainty, e.g. due to sparse or noisy measurements. Inferring physical quantities and fields of interest then becomes an ill-posed problem which both classical numerical methods and modern deep learning-based methods struggle to treat appropriately. Recent work has framed classical numerical methods as Bayesian inference under Gaussian process priors, resulting in a physics-aware treatment of uncertainties. Following this line of work, we develop a novel numerically conservative method for uncertainty-aware simulations of nonlinear conservation laws. We use recent sparse approximation techniques to scale up to large-scale forward and inverse problems. For forward simulation, we inherit the accuracy of classical solvers while providing structured uncertainty quantification. On inverse problems, we recover posteriors over nonparametric source fields in seconds -- outperforming neural baselines that take minutes to produce a less accurate point estimate.

</details>


### [100] [Generalizing Multi-Scale Time-Series Modeling with a Single Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31129)
*Cheonwoo Lee,Dooho Lee,Doyun Choi,Jaemin Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SiGMA（单一通用多尺度架构），通过可学习离散高斯（LDG）核实现距离感知缩放，克服了现有多尺度时序预测方法依赖固定离散缩放的局限性，在长短期预测任务上均达到SOTA，训练速度提升最高5.3倍，内存消耗降低最高3.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列多尺度建模方法缺乏理论基础，且存在依赖固定和离散缩放的固有局限性，亟需一种更灵活、有理论依据的统一架构。

Method: 将现有缩放方法统一为缩放算子族，提出SiGMA架构，基于尺度空间理论引入可学习离散高斯（LDG）核实现距离感知的连续缩放。

Result: 在长短期预测基准上全面超越SOTA多尺度基线，长时预测16个设置中13个取得最佳；训练速度提升最高5.3倍，内存消耗降低最高3.8倍。

Conclusion: SiGMA通过理论驱动的可学习缩放机制，在精度和效率上均显著优于现有方法，为多尺度时序预测建立了新基础。

Abstract: Multi-scale modeling has emerged as an effective design principle for time-series forecasting by capturing temporal dynamics at multiple resolutions. As no principled foundation has been established in the literature, we unify existing scaling methods into a scaling operator family, revealing a fundamental limitation of existing approaches: reliance on fixed and discrete scaling. To address this limitation, we propose SiGMA (Single Generalized Multi-scale Architecture), which enables distance-aware scaling via the learnable discrete Gaussian (LDG) kernel grounded in scale-space theory. We evaluate SiGMA comprehensively on long- and short-term forecasting benchmarks against state-of-the-art multi-scale baselines. SiGMA outperforms all competitors on both tasks, especially achieving the best performance in 13 out of 16 long-term evaluation settings. Beyond accuracy, SiGMA significantly improves training speed by up to 5.3 times and reduces memory consumption by up to 3.8 times over the strongest competitors. Code is available at https://github.com/cheonwoolee/SiGMA.

</details>


### [101] [Learning Hyperspherical Time-Frequency Representations for Time-Series Out-of-Distribution Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31155)
*Willian T. Lunardi,Samridha Shrestha,Martin Andreoni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于超球面嵌入的时序列异常检测方法，通过von Mises-Fisher似然目标学习类条件结构，融合时域和频域特征，在UCR和UEA数据集上显著优于对比学习和事后基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序数据的分布外(OOD)检测相较于视觉和语言领域研究不足，缺乏对如何利用监督时序表示进行可靠分布偏移检测的原则性理解。

Method: 将时序OOD检测形式化为超球面嵌入上的表示学习，利用von Mises-Fisher(vMF)似然目标在单位球面上诱导类条件结构；通过域特定编码器融合输入信号的时域和频域视图，整合到联合嵌入空间；检测时使用基于距离的评分(k-NN和Mahalanobis)。

Result: 在完整的UCR和UEA时间序列档案上采用跨数据集协议进行评估，实验结果表明在k-NN和Mahalanobis评分下均一致优于强对比学习和事后基准方法。

Conclusion: 超球面嵌入结合时频域融合的表示学习方法能有效提升时序数据OOD检测性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection for time-series data remains comparatively underexplored compared to vision and language, with a limited principled understanding of how supervised time-series representations can be leveraged for reliable detection under distributional shifts. This work formulates time-series OOD detection as representation learning with hyperspherical embeddings, where class-conditional structure is induced by a von Mises-Fisher (vMF) likelihood-based objective on the unit sphere. The learned representation combines time- and frequency-domain views of the input signal via domain-specific encoders, integrating them into a joint embedding space for OOD detection. Detection uses distance-based scores over the learned embeddings, including k-nearest neighbors (k-NN) and Mahalanobis scores. We evaluate the approach at scale on the complete UCR and UEA time-series archives under a cross-dataset protocol. Empirical results show consistent improvements under both k-NN and Mahalanobis scoring over strong contrastive learning and post-hoc baselines in the same setting. Code is available at https://github.com/tiiuae/hypertf-time-series-ood.

</details>


### [102] [TabCausal: Pretraining Across Causal Environments for Tabular Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31156)
*Zi-Rong Li,Si-Yang Liu,Tian-Zuo Wang,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TabCausal，一种基于数据驱动的因果发现基础模型（CDFM），通过动态任务构建策略在多样化的因果环境（包括不同图先验、结构机制、噪声模型、维度、样本量和干预机制）上进行广泛因果预训练，从而将数据集直接映射为因果图。TabCausal在合成基准上优于多种基线方法，并引入LLM审计的语义因果环境基准，展现出尤其在干预数据下鲁棒的结构恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CDFM在因果发现任务中表现有限，往往无法稳定超越经典方法，其关键瓶颈在于因果预训练任务的构建方式不够有效。作者旨在通过改进预训练任务设计来提升CDFM的迁移能力与鲁棒性。

Method: 提出TabCausal模型，核心包括：(1) 在多样化的因果环境中进行广泛预训练，覆盖不同图先验、结构机制、噪声模型、维度、样本量和干预机制；(2) 动态任务构造策略，组合这些因果环境形成多样的发现任务；(3) 引入协议引导+LLM审计的语义因果环境基准，用于评估分布外泛化能力。

Result: 在大规模合成基准上，TabCausal在宏观平均性能上优于多种因果发现基线方法；在语义因果环境基准上，TabCausal展现出鲁棒的结构恢复能力，尤其在存在干预证据的情况下表现突出。

Conclusion: 广泛的因果预训练是提升可迁移的摊销式因果发现能力的关键要素，TabCausal验证了这一思路的有效性，为构建更通用的因果发现基础模型提供了新方向。

Abstract: Causal discovery aims to recover directed causal relations from observational and interventional data, providing a basis for mechanistic understanding and reliable decision-making. Causal discovery foundation models (CDFMs) seek to amortize this problem by mapping a dataset directly to a causal graph in a single forward pass, avoiding per-dataset testing, search, or optimization. However, existing CDFMs remain limited, often failing to consistently match strong classical methods, and we find that a key bottleneck is how causal pretraining tasks are constructed. Based on this observation, we propose TabCausal, a data-driven CDFM trained with broad causal pretraining over diverse graph priors, structural mechanisms, noise models, dimensions, sample sizes, and intervention regimes. A dynamic task construction strategy composes these causal environments into varied discovery tasks, enabling more transferable structural learning from observational and mixed-interventional data. On large-scale synthetic benchmarks, TabCausal achieves better macro-averaged performance than a diverse set of causal discovery baselines. To further bridge abstract synthetic generators and realistic causal reasoning scenarios, we introduce a protocol-guided and LLM-audited semantic causal environment benchmark, where domain-grounded SCMs generate interpretable observational and interventional datasets for out-of-distribution analysis. Across both synthetic and semantic environments, TabCausal demonstrates robust structure recovery, especially under interventional evidence, highlighting broad causal pretraining as a key ingredient for transferable amortized causal discovery.

</details>


### [103] [Trust-Region Behavior Blending for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31159)
*Daniil Plyusov,Alexey Gorbatovski,Alexey Malakhov,Nikita Balagansky,Boris Shaposhnikov,Daria Korotyshova,Daniil Gavrilov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出信任域行为混合（TRB）预热方法，通过学生中心KL信任域内用最接近教师的行为策略替换早期rollout策略，解决在线策略蒸馏中早期学生rollout质量差的问题，在数学推理任务上取得最佳平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）虽然解决了离线蒸馏中的前缀不匹配问题，但早期学生rollout质量仍然很差，导致教师监督作用在弱或低质量前缀上，需要一种预热方法来改善早期训练阶段的学生策略质量。

Method: 提出Trust-Region behavior Blending (TRB)，在学生中心KL信任域内，用最接近教师的行为策略替换早期rollout策略，保持每个前缀上的reverse-KL OPD损失不变，并将KL预算逐渐退火至零，使训练在预热后回归纯学生rollout。

Result: 在两种数学推理蒸馏设置下，TRB在所有对比方法中取得了最强的平均性能。

Conclusion: TRB作为一种预热方法，有效缓解了在线策略蒸馏中早期学生rollout质量差的问题，通过信任域内行为策略混合实现了更稳定的训练和更好的最终性能。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on prefixes sampled from its own policy while matching a stronger teacher. This addresses the prefix mismatch of offline distillation, but early student rollouts can still be poor, placing teacher supervision on weak or low-quality prefixes. We propose Trust-Region behavior Blending (TRB), a warmup method that replaces the early rollout policy with the closest-to-teacher behavior policy inside a student-centered KL trust region, while keeping the per-prefix reverse-KL OPD loss unchanged. The KL budget is annealed to zero, so training returns to pure student rollouts after warmup. Across two math-reasoning distillation settings, TRB attains the strongest average among the compared methods.

</details>


### [104] [Convergence of Two-Timescale Markovian Stochastic Approximations with Applications in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31172)
*Vagul Mahadevan,Claire Chen,Shuze Daniel Liu,Shangtong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究建立了双时间尺度随机逼近在马尔可夫噪声下的稳定性和收敛性，并首次证明了TDC算法在离策略学习中的几乎必然收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前双时间尺度SA的稳定性与收敛性仅在同分布独立噪声下被证明，而强化学习中更常见的是马尔可夫噪声。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 关键技术创新是用快时间尺度参数的运行最大值（running max）来控制慢时间尺度参数，而非像大多数先前工作那样使用当前慢时间尺度参数。无需使用投影算子，也无需噪声位于紧集空间。

Result: 在马尔可夫噪声下建立了双时间尺度SA的稳定性和收敛性；作为关键应用，首次证明了带资格迹的TDC在离策略学习与线性函数逼近下的几乎必然收敛。

Conclusion: 该工作将双时间尺度SA的理论保证从i.i.d.噪声推广到更实际的马尔可夫噪声设置，为相关RL算法的理论分析提供了更坚实的基础。

Abstract: This work studies the convergence of two-timescale stochastic approximations (SA), a class of iterative algorithms that update two sets of parameters in fast and slow timescales respectively. Notable examples of two-timescale SA in reinforcement learning (RL) include temporal difference learning with gradient correction (TDC) and actor-critic methods. Previously, the stability (i.e., boundedness) and convergence of two-timescale SA were only established under i.i.d. noise. This work instead establishes the stability and convergence of two-timescale SA under Markovian noise, a setup that is more realistic in RL. Notably, we do not need to use any projection operator and the noise does not need to live in a compact space. Our key technical novelty is to control the fast timescale parameter with the running max of the slow timescale parameter, instead of with the current slow timescale parameter, as most prior works do. As a key application, we establish the first almost sure convergence of TDC with eligibility traces under off-policy learning with linear function approximation.

</details>


### [105] [Retriever Portfolios: A Principled Approach to Adaptive RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31176)
*Miltiadis Stouras,Vincent Cohen-Addad,Silvio Lattanzi,Ola Svensson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种自动选择多样化检索器组合（portfolio）的方法，用于覆盖异构查询分布，并通过期望最优目标实现高效且接近最优的检索器组合构建，在多个QA基准上优于单检索器和朴素多检索器基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG系统使用单一检索器和固定超参数，无法有效处理从简单事实性问题到复杂多跳推理的高度异构查询，需要一种能自动适配不同查询类型的检索策略。

Method: 从大量候选检索器中自动选择一个小而多样的子集（portfolio），通过期望best-of-k目标形式化问题，并设计具有近优保证的高效组合构建算法；同时配合路由器（router）管线进行查询分配。

Result: 在多个QA基准上，学习的组合和路由器管线在检索指标和答案质量上均优于单检索器和朴素多检索器基线；相比推理时超参数调优方法，固定组合在保持可比甚至更好准确率的同时，显著降低了延迟和token成本。

Conclusion: 多样化的固定检索器组合配合路由器可以高效覆盖异构查询分布，兼顾准确率与推理效率，是替代推理时调优的有效方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems typically rely on a single retriever and a single set of hyperparameters, despite facing highly heterogeneous queries that range from simple factoid questions to complex multi-hop reasoning. We propose a method that automatically selects a small, diverse subset of retrievers (a portfolio) from a large pool of candidates, to cover different regions of the target query distribution. We formalize this setting via an expected best-of-$k$ objective over the query distribution and show that it admits an efficient portfolio construction algorithm with near-optimal guarantees. Across multiple QA benchmarks, our learned portfolios and router pipeline consistently outperform single-retriever and naive multi-retriever baselines on both retrieval metrics and answer quality. In addition, compared to inference-time hyperparameter tuning approaches, fixed portfolios enable parallel retrieval and LLM calls, achieving comparable (and sometimes better) accuracy with substantially lower latency and token cost.

</details>


### [106] [How well does Classification Accuracy capture Concept Drift Detection Quality? An overview of Concept Drift Detection evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31186)
*Joanna Komorniczak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了合成非平稳数据流中概念漂移检测质量指标与分类性能之间的关系，分析了8种漂移检测质量指标在7种合成数据流生成工具上的表现，并考虑了漂移动态因素，旨在找出最具信息量的漂移检测质量指标集并加深对方法评估的理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前学术界尚未建立统一的评估概念漂移检测任务的框架，现有研究常依赖分类质量指标来评估漂移检测，但这些指标受多种因素影响，无法可靠反映漂移检测的真实质量。

Method: 研究采用合成非平稳数据流，分析了8种漂移检测质量指标与分类器性能之间的关系，利用7种合成数据流生成工具进行实验，并将漂移动态作为影响因素纳入考量。

Result: 识别出最具信息量的漂移检测质量指标集，并揭示了漂移检测质量指标与分类性能之间的深层关系。

Conclusion: 研究有助于建立更可靠的概念漂移检测评估框架，推动该领域评估方法的规范化和标准化。

Abstract: Data streams are nowadays among the most frequently analyzed data structures, with the concept drift posing a major challenge encountered by processing systems. Despite the proposition of numerous solutions to counteract the accuracy degeneration due to concept drift, the scientific community has not yet established a unified framework for evaluating the concept drift detection task. Existing research often relies on classification quality metrics, but these can be affected by multiple factors and may not reliably reflect drift detection quality. In this work, we present an in-depth overview of the relationship between metrics for quantifying drift detection quality and classification performance in synthetic nonstationary data streams. The proposed research studies eight drift detection quality metrics in relation to the classifier's performance across seven synthetic data stream generation tools, additionally considering drift dynamics as a factor. The studies aim to identify the most informative set of drift detection quality metrics and provide a deep understanding of the method's evaluation.

</details>


### [107] [FlagGAM: Rule-Based Generalized Additive Modeling for Explainable Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31189)
*Zijie Zhao,Roy E. Welsch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FlagGAM框架，通过规则定义的基函数将特征级规则构建与预测分离，在保持可解释性的同时提升表格数据预测的鲁棒性和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险领域的表格预测需要同时满足准确性、透明性和对不完美输入（缺失、噪声）的鲁棒性，现有方法难以兼顾这三者。

Method: 设计Flag Core Module将数值和类别变量转换为稀疏、可读的单变量基函数（阈值标志、类别标志、尾部偏差基、类别阶梯函数），保留稀疏规则基矩阵而非压缩为计数摘要；默认加性头以GAM风格组合基函数，同时支持灵活的非线性预测头。

Result: 默认FlagGAM在透明加性模式下接近EBM，在混合类型回归上显著优于岭回归；在缺失和噪声扰动下AUROC下降幅度小于常见基线；灵活头进一步提升精度，接近强树模型基线。

Conclusion: FlagGAM为需要竞争性精度、可沟通规则和对不完美输入鲁棒性的表格预测场景提供了一个实用的中间方案。

Abstract: Tabular prediction in high-stakes domains requires models that are accurate, transparent, and robust to imperfect inputs. We propose FlagGAM, a rule-defined basis framework that separates feature-level rule construction from prediction. A Flag Core Module converts numerical and categorical variables into sparse, human-readable univariate bases, including threshold flags, category-level flags, tail-deviation bases, and categorical step functions; a default additive head then combines these bases as a restricted GAM-style predictor. Rather than reducing triggered rules to compact count summaries, FlagGAM retains a sparse rule-basis matrix that supports mixed-type classification and regression, feature-specific weighting, and optional flexible prediction heads. Across tabular benchmarks, default FlagGAM remains close to EBM in transparent additive mode, improves substantially over ridge regression on mixed-type regression, and shows smaller AUROC degradation than common baselines under missing and noisy perturbations. Flexible heads further improve accuracy and approach strong tree-based baselines, with the caveat that the resulting model should be interpreted as a rule-basis representation followed by a nonlinear predictor rather than as a fully additive GAM. Overall, FlagGAM provides a practical middle ground for tabular settings that require competitive accuracy, communicable rules, and robustness to imperfect inputs.

</details>


### [108] [Student Capacity Moderates Knowledge Distillation Effectiveness: A Systematic Study Across ResNet Teacher-Student Pairs on CIFAR-10](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31191)
*Umut Onur Yasar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了ResNet架构下师生容量关系对知识蒸馏效果的影响，发现学生容量是调节蒸馏增益的关键因素，修正梯度裁剪错误和输入分辨率适配架构分别对特征蒸馏效果和整体基线有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究师生模型容量关系如何调节知识蒸馏的有效性，澄清先前研究中因实现缺陷（如梯度裁剪错误、架构不匹配）导致的混淆结论。

Method: 在CIFAR-10上使用ResNet系列（R50、R34、R18）构成三组师生对，比较Logit-KD和Feature-KD两种蒸馏方法，在3个随机种子下报告均值±标准差，严格控制实验可复现性。

Result: 1) R34学生从KD中获益显著优于R18学生，R50->R34的Feature-KD取得最大增益+0.30pp；2) 修正梯度裁剪bug后，Feature-KD在两组对中匹配或超越Logit-KD；3) 修正输入分辨率适配架构使教师准确率提升超5pp，远大于任何KD增益。

Conclusion: 学生容量是蒸馏增益的关键调节因素；实现正确性（梯度裁剪）和架构适配（输入分辨率）对蒸馏效果有重大影响，误导性结论需通过可控实验纠正。

Abstract: We investigate how teacher-student capacity relationships modulate knowledge distillation (KD) effectiveness in ResNet-based image classification on CIFAR-10. Across three teacher-student pairs -- R50->R18, R34->R18, and R50->R34 -- we compare Logit-KD and Feature-KD under controlled, reproducible conditions (3 seeds, mean+/-std reported throughout). We report three main findings. First, student capacity is a key moderating factor in distillation gain: R34 students benefit substantially more from KD than R18 students even when teacher-student accuracy gaps are comparable, with the strongest gain of +0.30pp observed for R50->R34 Feature-KD versus +0.18pp for R34->R18 Feature-KD and +0.00pp for R34->R18 Logit-KD. Second, implementation correctness critically affects Feature-KD: a gradient clipping bug that excluded projection layers suppressed Feature-KD performance and produced misleading comparisons with Logit-KD. After correction, Feature-KD matches or outperforms Logit-KD in two of three pairs, reaching 95.55% on R50->R34 against a baseline of 95.25%. Third, input-resolution-aware architecture is a prerequisite for effective distillation: correcting the ResNet stem for 32x32 inputs raises teacher accuracy by over 5pp -- an order of magnitude larger than any KD gain. All code and results are available at github.com/umutonuryasar/kd-capacity-gap.

</details>


### [109] [Geometry-based Schrödinger Bridges for Trustworthy Multimodal Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31193)
*Jiayu Xiong,Jing Wang,Qi Zhang,Wanlong Wang,Jun Xue*

Main category: cs.LG
