TL;DR: 提出基于几何度量的多模态融合方法GMF，利用扩散薛定谔桥传输的校正得分作为独立于模型预测的可靠性信号，有效提升对低质量多模态数据的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可信融合方法依赖模型自身预测置信度来判断数据质量，存在循环依赖问题：当模型自信但错误时，这些方法无法检测到错误。

Method: 提出几何多模态融合（GMF），不依赖预测结果，而是通过测量输入在潜空间所需的传输校正量来评估可靠性。采用带Rectified Flow的扩散薛定谔桥传输，将初始速度的平方作为高效的学习校正得分。

Result: 实验表明，与基于置信度的基线方法相比，GMF在应对严重传感器噪声和语义冲突时显著提升了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于几何度量的可靠性信号可作为独立于分类器的评判标准，有效标记不可靠输入，打破原有循环依赖问题。

Abstract: Real-world multimodal systems must be robust against low-quality data, such as sensor noise, incomplete multimodal data and conflicting inputs. However, existing trustworthy fusion methods rely on the model's own prediction confidence to judge data quality. This creates a circular dependency: when a model is confident but wrong, these methods fail to detect the error. To break this loop, we propose Geometry-based Multimodal Fusion (GMF). Instead of relying on predictions, we evaluate reliability by measuring how much transport correction the input needs in latent space. We implement Diffusion Schrödinger Bridge transport with Rectified Flow, where the squared initial velocity gives an efficient learned correction score. Valid data has low squared velocity magnitude, while noisy, incomplete data or conflicting data requires stronger transport correction. This geometry-based reliability signal acts as an independent judge, effectively flagging unreliable inputs even when the classifier is fooled. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GMF significantly improves robustness against severe sensor noise and semantic conflicts compared to confidence-based baselines.

</details>


### [110] [Beyond Additive Decompositions: Interpretability Through Separability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31200)
*Jinyang Liu,Munir Eberhardt Hiabu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Tensor Separation Learning (TSL)回归模型，通过阶段贪婪过程与正交重构学习单变量特征函数的秩-1乘积之和，避免了传统可加模型在强交互下的信号抵消和外推问题，在保持可解释性的同时达到与黑盒模型相当的回归性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释机器学习方法（如GAM、SHAP、functional ANOVA）依赖可加表示，在存在强交互作用时会出现信号抵消和支撑域外推的信息损失问题，亟需一种既能保留交互信息又保持可解释性的建模方法。

Method: 提出Tensor Separation Learning (TSL)，通过阶段贪婪过程（stagewise greedy procedure）结合正交重构（orthogonal refitting），学习一组秩-1乘积之和形式的单变量特征函数。模型可从一阶偏依赖函数完全重构（仅差常数因子），实现可视化与拟合组分的保真对齐。

Result: 建立了对具有有界混合p阶偏导数的函数的逼近率保证，并在回归基准测试中证明TSL性能与黑盒模型相竞争。

Conclusion: TSL通过可分离性学习避免了可加模型因边际化高阶交互而导致的信息损失，同时在可解释性和预测精度之间取得了良好平衡，为可解释机器学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Interpretable machine learning requires models that are accurate and structurally faithful to the data.Existing explainability methods rely heavily on additive representations (e.g., Generalized Additive Models (GAMs), SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP), functional ANOVA), which can suffer from signal cancellation and off-support extrapolation in the presence of strong interactions. We propose Tensor Separation Learning (TSL), a regression model that learns a sum of rank-1 products of univariate per-feature functions via a stagewise greedy procedure with orthogonal refitting. By enforcing separability, TSL avoids the information loss inherent in additive projections caused by marginalizing higher-order interactions. The learned TSL model can be fully reconstructed from first-order partial dependence functions, up to constant factors. This stage-wise correspondence ensures that the resulting visualizations are faithful to the fitted components. We establish approximation-rate guarantees for functions with bounded mixed $p$-th order partial derivatives and demonstrate that TSL competes with black-box models on regression benchmarks.

</details>


### [111] [Fixed-Point Masked Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31215)
*Andrea Miele,Yiming Qin,Alba Carballo-Castro,Justin Deschenaux,Pascal Frossard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoFRe框架，通过固定点求解器（固定点求解器）替代部分去噪器，结合交叉步一致性损失和三状态重用（3SR），在减少参数量、训练时间和显存占用的同时，提升了掩码生成模型在低采样预算下的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有掩码生成模型（MGMs）每一步都需要全序列双向Transformer，训练成本高且低采样预算下质量差；而现有改进方法（更好的采样器或固定深度去噪器）仍为每个精炼步分配固定计算量，缺乏自适应深度机制。

Method: 1) 将部分去噪器替换为共享注意力层上的固定点求解器，实现自适应深度；2) 引入交叉步一致性损失，对齐相邻去噪步的隐藏表示；3) 提出三状态重用（3SR），根据未改变、仍掩码和新揭示三种token状态对前一步解进行热启动；4) 支持预训练MGM通过短微调转换为FP-MGM。

Result: 在OpenWebText上，参数量减少38.8%，训练时间减少11.5%，显存减少16.9%，在96次Transformer块前向传播预算下生成困惑度从830.8降至101.8；在ImageNette上，训练时间减少48.6%，显存减少50.7%，所有采样预算下FID均有提升。

Conclusion: CoFRe提供了一套实用的训练到推理框架，能以更低的训练成本实现更强的低预算掩码生成性能。

Abstract: Masked Generative Models (MGMs) enable parallel decoding and achieve strong performance across modalities, but require full-sequence bidirectional transformers at every step, making training costly and degrading quality under low sampling budgets. Existing work improves efficiency via better samplers or cheaper fixed-depth denoisers, but they still allocate a fixed amount of denoiser computation to each refinement step. We introduce Fixed-Point Masked Generative Models (FP-MGMs), which replace part of the denoiser with a fixed-point solver over shared attention layers to enable adaptive depth with fewer parameters. To make it more effective for masked generation, we first introduce a cross-step consistency loss, which aligns hidden representations at neighboring denoising steps and, second, three-state reuse (3SR) which warm-starts the solver using the previous solution by treating differently unchanged, still-masked, and newly revealed tokens respectively. Together, these components define our complete training-to-inference framework for fixed-point masked generation, \emph{CoFRe}. We also show that pre-trained MGMs can be converted into FP-MGMs with short fine-tuning, avoiding full retraining. Across modalities, CoFRe improves the quality and cost trade-off. On OpenWebText, CoFRe reduces parameters by 38.8\%, training time by 11.5\%, and VRAM by 16.9\%, while improving generative perplexity from 830.8 to 101.8 at a budget of $96$ transformer-block forward passes, compared to MDLM. In ImageNette, CoFRe reduces training time by 48.6\% and VRAM by 50.7\%, while improving FID in all sample budgets tested. Overall, CoFRe offers a practical framework for cheaper training and stronger low-budget masked generation.

</details>


### [112] [Multivariate Distributional Reinforcement Learning Using Sliced Divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31222)
*Baptiste Debes,Tinne Tuytelaars*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出切片分布强化学习(SDRL)，通过将高维收益分布投影到一维空间，利用可计算的一维散度来度量多变量收益分布，并证明了均匀切片和最大切片变体下的贝尔曼收缩性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布强化学习(DRL)在多变量场景下扩展困难：常见度量无法自然推广到高维或失去计算可追踪性，且多变量情况下的通用矩阵折扣缺乏收缩性结果。

Method: 通过投影将可处理的一维散度（如Wasserstein、Cramér、MMD）提升到多变量收益分布；提出均匀切片（共享标量折扣）和最大切片（通用稠密折扣矩阵）两种变体，并分析哪些变体适用于标准单样本贝尔曼更新。

Result: 证明了均匀切片在共享标量折扣下的贝尔曼收缩性，以及最大切片在通用稠密折扣矩阵下的收缩性；在玩具链问题、网格世界图像环境和部分Atari游戏上进行了评估。

Conclusion: SDRL为多变量分布强化学习提供了一种通用且理论上有保证的框架，将一维散度的计算效率与多变量收益分布的表达能力结合起来。

Abstract: Distributional reinforcement learning (DRL) models the full return distribution rather than expectations, but extending it to multivariate settings remains challenging. Many common metrics do not naturally generalize beyond one dimension or lose computational tractability, and the multivariate case introduces additional difficulties such as general matrix discounting, for which no contraction results are available. We introduce Sliced Distributional Reinforcement Learning (SDRL), which lifts tractable one-dimensional divergences to multivariate return distributions via projections. We prove Bellman contraction for uniform slicing under shared scalar discounting, and introduce a maximum-slicing variant with contraction under general dense discount matrices. SDRL supports a broad class of base divergences; we analyze Wasserstein, Cramér, and Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD), and characterize which SDRL variants suit the standard single-sample Bellman update used in distributional RL. We evaluate SDRL on a toy chain problem and a gridworld image-based environment as well as a subset of Atari games.

</details>


### [113] [What changes after deployment? A survey on On-device Learning in TinyML](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31226)
*Massimo Pavan,Luca Pezzarossa,Fabrizio Pittorino,Manuel Roveri,Xenofon Fafoutis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了约70篇TinyML设备上学习（ODL）的研究，从分布变化类型这一统一原则出发，分析了不同分布变化如何影响应用场景、硬件选择及解决方案结构，并指出了方法基准与现实部署之间的持续差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微控制器级设备上的机器学习模型（TinyML）在部署后面临分布变化导致的模型退化问题，现有文献未能系统描述分布变化的类型及其对解决方案的影响。

Method: 以“分布变化机制”为统一原则，对约70篇设备上学习（ODL）相关工作进行了系统综述，分析不同类型分布变化对可寻址应用、硬件及解决方案结构的影响。

Result: 识别出不同的分布变化类型需要不同的解决方案；指出了方法论基准与现实部署场景之间存在的持续性差距。

Conclusion: 分布变化类型是ODL方案设计的关键维度，当前研究在基准测试与真实部署之间仍有鸿沟，未来需更注重实际分布变化场景的建模与评估。

Abstract: Machine learning models on microcontroller-class devices (TinyML) face a fundamental challenge: post-deployment distribution change undermines static models. On-device learning (ODL) addresses this by running the learning process directly on the device. The existing literature has not characterized how distribution change occurs or how different change types require different solutions. Approximately 70 ODL works are surveyed under one principle: the distribution change regime. The survey analyzes how different types of distribution change influence the applications addressable on-device, the hardware employed, and the structure of the solutions. A persistent gap between methodological benchmarks and real-world deployment scenarios is also identified.

</details>


### [114] [Spectral Reach: Understanding Neural Scaling as Progress into the Spectral Tail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31244)
*Konstantin Nikolaou,Jonas Scheunemann,Sven Krippendorf,Samuel Tovey,Christian Holm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"谱位置"（spectral position）这一可扩展度量，用于量化经验神经正切核（eNTK）特征值对损失下降的贡献。实验发现，训练过程中谱位置持续下降，学习从主导特征模态向谱尾转移；更大模型具有更强的"谱可达性"（spectral reach），能持续学习弱小谱信号；特征学习（feature learning）通过自适应放大梯度幅度，是维持谱可达性的关键机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经缩放定律已广泛指导大模型开发，但其背后的机制尚不明确，缺乏可扩展的分析工具来理解缩放定律的成因。

Method: 提出"谱位置"度量，基于经验神经正切核（eNTK）的特征值分析，衡量当前训练阶段哪些特征模态主导损失下降；通过缩放实验观察谱位置变化与模型大小的关系。

Result: 训练中谱位置递减，学习从主导模态转向谱尾；大模型比小模型具有更强谱可达性，能触及并利用更弱的谱信号；特征学习自适应放大梯度，维持谱尾学习。

Conclusion: 大模型之所以性能更优，是因为其能持续学习小模型无法捕捉的弱谱信号；特征学习是维持谱可达性的关键，为架构和优化器设计提供了具体改进方向。

Abstract: Neural scaling laws describe predictable power-law relationships between model size, dataset size, compute, and performance. While these laws guide the development of modern foundation models, the mechanisms underpinning them remain poorly understood, in part due to the absence of scalable analysis tools. To close this gap, we introduce "spectral position": a scalable measure of which eigenvalues of the empirical neural tangent kernel (eNTK) currently drive loss reduction. Applying this measure to scaling experiments, we find that spectral position decreases throughout training: learning shifts from dominant eigenmodes into the spectral tail. Larger models reach further into the tail than smaller models, revealing a size-dependent capacity we call "spectral reach". This suggests why larger models achieve lower losses: they sustain learning on weak spectral signals inaccessible to smaller models. We further identify feature learning as a key enabler of spectral reach. It adaptively amplifies gradient magnitudes as learning advances, sustaining progress where frozen representations stall. This points to concrete interventions through architecture and optimizer design.

</details>


### [115] [Learning Cardiac Latent Representations in Vectorcardiogram Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31249)
*Bosong Huang,Panzhen Zhao,Zengxiang Li,Patricia Lee,Wei Jin,Alan Wee-Chung Liew,Ming Jin,Shirui Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LVCG，首个在向量心电（VCG）空间进行自监督表示学习的框架，通过学习物理上更本质的潜在心脏电活动表征，减少冗余、提升泛化能力，在心电任务上优于传统ECG空间方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准十二导联ECG是同一心脏电活动在不同空间方向的投影，在ECG信号空间进行表示学习存在大量冗余，易导致伪相关和过拟合。为解决该问题，受Frank向量心电（VCG）模型启发，提出直接在物理上更本质的VCG空间中学习统一的心脏电活动潜在表征。

Method: 提出LVCG框架，首个在VCG空间中进行通用自监督表示学习的方法。通过学习视图不变（view-invariant）的潜在VCG表征，而非特定导联的伪影，从而减少冗余、提升泛化能力。

Result: LVCG在多个任务上全面优于ECG空间基线方法，尤其在领域偏移（domain shift）场景下表现出更强的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 在VCG这一物理基础更扎实的潜在空间中进行表示学习，可以有效减少ECG信号空间的冗余，提升模型在心脏任务上的表现和泛化能力。

Abstract: Electrocardiography (ECG) is a cornerstone of cardiac assessment, making the learning of informative ECG representations fundamental to tasks ranging from disease diagnosis to clinical report generation. However, existing methods operate almost exclusively in the observable ECG signal space. In practice, the standard twelve-lead ECG represents multiple projections of the same underlying cardiac electrical activity from different spatial orientations. Therefore, representation learning in the ECG space inevitably introduces substantial redundancy, which may lead to spurious correlations and increased risk of overfitting. To address this and motivated by the Frank vectorcardiogram (VCG) model, we propose learning a unified latent representation of cardiac electrical activity directly in the VCG space. We introduce LVCG, the first general self-supervised representation learning framework designed to operate in this physically grounded latent space. By learning view-invariant latent VCG representations rather than lead-specific artifacts, VCG minimizes redundancy and improves generalization. LVCG generally outperforms ECG-space baselines across tasks, demonstrating enhanced robustness and generalization, especially in domain shift settings.

</details>


### [116] [Algorithmic Recourse of In-Context Learning for Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31272)
*Wenshuo Dong,Jiaming Zhang,Shaopneg Fu,Hongbin Lin,Di Wang,Lijie Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次研究上下文学习（ICL）下表格数据的算法反事实解释，提出自适应子空间反事实解释框架（ASR-ICL），实现高效、可操作的稀疏反事实生成，并通过理论分析和实验验证了其收敛性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险决策场景（如信贷审批）中，预测模型需要提供可操作的反事实解释（recourse）。尽管大语言模型通过上下文学习（ICL）已能处理表格预测任务，但针对ICL场景下的算法反事实解释仍处于空白状态。

Method: 1) 理论分析：证明ICL下反事实解是有定义且有界的，并刻画了随上下文规模增加向经典解收敛的行为。2) 提出ASR-ICL框架：一种零阶反事实方法，通过自适应子空间搜索高效生成可操作且稀疏的反事实解释，并自然扩展至多分类表格任务。

Result: 在多个真实数据集和模型上的实验表明，ASR-ICL能以更少的查询次数达到与现有方法相当的反事实质量，并实证验证了理论预测的收敛行为。

Conclusion: 本文填补了ICL场景下表格数据算法反事实解释的研究空白，为高风险决策中基于LLM的表格预测模型提供了一种高效、理论支撑的可解释性工具。

Abstract: As predictive models are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings such as credit approval, there is a growing need for post-hoc methods that provide recourse to affected individuals. Many such models operate on tabular data, where features correspond to real-world attributes. Recently, in-context learning (ICL) has enabled large language models to perform tabular prediction by conditioning on labeled examples at inference time, without explicit training. However, algorithmic recourse for tabular decision-making under ICL remains largely unexplored. In this work, we present the first study of algorithmic recourse for tabular data under ICL. We carry out a theoretical analysis, showing that recourse remains well-defined and bounded, and we characterize how recourse converges toward classical solutions as the context size increases. In practice, we propose a novel zeroth-order recourse framework, Adaptive Subspace Recourse for In-Context Learning (ASR-ICL), that efficiently generates actionable and sparse recourse for black-box ICL models. The proposed framework naturally extends to multi-class tabular tasks. Experiments across multiple real-world datasets and models demonstrate that ASR-ICL achieves recourse quality comparable to existing methods with fewer queries and empirically confirm the predicted convergence behavior, supporting our theoretical analysis.

</details>


### [117] [Learning Parametric Nitrogen Fertilizer Response Curves Using Neuro Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31276)
*Giorgio Morales,John Sheppard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号回归方法，无需预定义函数形式即可学习氮响应曲线，通过Transformer和多集符号骨架预测策略发现不同管理区域间的共享函数结构，在合成数据和真实冬小麦数据上均表现出更优的拟合性能和区域特异性函数发现能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准农业中准确建模作物对氮肥的响应关乎经济效益与环境可持续性，现有方法要么依赖预定义的参数化形式，要么使用不透明的机器学习模型，限制了从数据中解释或发现特定地点的函数关系的能力。

Method: 提出神经符号回归方法，集成基于Transformer的多集符号骨架预测策略，通过构建多样化的输入子集并强制执行跨子集一致性，发现跨子域/管理区的共享符号骨架，随后使用遗传算法拟合观测数据。

Result: 在合成一维问题上，该方法在数据稀缺条件下仍能恢复正确的表达式；在真实冬小麦数据上，发现的表达式不仅比传统模型（二次平台和指数函数）拟合误差更低，还能捕获不同空间区域多样化的函数行为。

Conclusion: 神经符号回归能够发现特定地点的农学函数关系，为精准农业中的知情决策提供支持。

Abstract: Accurately modeling crop response to Nitrogen (N) fertilization is a fundamental challenge in precision agriculture, as it impacts both economic returns and environmental sustainability. Existing approaches either rely on predefined parametric forms or opaque machine learning models, limiting their ability to interpret or discover site-specific functional relationships from data. In this work, we propose a neuro symbolic regression (SR) approach to learn parametric N-response curves without assuming a predefined functional form. Our approach integrates a transformer-based Multi-Set Symbolic Skeleton Prediction strategy, enabling the discovery of shared functional structures across multiple subdomains or management zones (MZs). By constructing diverse input subsets and enforcing consistency across them, the method recovers robust symbolic skeletons that are subsequently fitted to observed data using a genetic algorithm. This framework was first evaluated on synthetic one-dimensional problems to assess its robustness under varying levels of epistemic uncertainty. The results demonstrate the ability of the proposed SR approach to recover correct expressions even in data-scarce regimes. In this work, we present the results of applying our method to real-world winter wheat data, learning distinct parametric N-response curves for different MZs within a field. The results show that the discovered expressions not only achieve lower fitting errors than traditional models such as quadratic-plateau and exponential functions, but also capture diverse functional behaviors across spatial regions. This demonstrates the potential that neuro SR has to enable the discovery of site-specific agronomic relationships and support informed decision-making in precision agriculture.

</details>


### [118] [The Terminal Representation in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31289)
*Amir Esterhuysen,Anders Jonsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的强化学习表示——终端表示（TR），它通过奖励加权轨迹编码状态，无需特征分解即可实现与默认表示（DR）类似的功能，且计算开销更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后继表示（SR）和默认表示（DR）虽然有效，但DR需要特征分解才能用于下游任务，且特征分解假设对称转移动力学，限制了其适用性和计算效率。本文旨在提出一种无需特征分解、计算效率更高的替代表示。

Method: 定义了终端表示（TR），推导了其理论基础，包括数学推导、两种学习算法的收敛性证明、零样本组合性以及不同奖励公式间的等价关系。证明了TR嵌入在DR的最大特征向量中，无需特征分解即可捕获相同知识。

Result: TR在学习、存储和使用上计算开销更低，无需特征分解即可直接用于选项发现、奖励塑形、迁移学习和探索等下游任务。

Conclusion: TR是DR的一个可行替代方案，在保持类似功能的同时降低了计算复杂度，为强化学习中的表示学习提供了新方向。

Abstract: Representation learning is a powerful tool for spatio-temporal abstraction within reinforcement learning (RL). Two well established approaches are through the successor representation (SR) and the default representation (DR). The SR encodes states by the future trajectories they induce, capturing information flow decoupled from reward. The DR builds on this by weighting trajectories with reward, integrating credit-assignment structure into the representation. Eigenvectors of both representations have been used to support a range of downstream tasks -- including option discovery, reward shaping, transfer learning, and exploration. We introduce a structurally distinct formulation: the terminal representation (TR). The TR encodes reward-weighted trajectories similarly to the DR, but can be learned as a lower-dimensionality object, and can be used directly for the mentioned applications without eigenvector computations. Eigendecomposition also imposes the assumption of symmetric transition dynamics, which the TR can bypass. In this work we develop the theoretical foundations of the TR: its derivation, convergence of two learning algorithms, its use for zero-shot compositionality, and equivalences between alternative reward formulations. We further show the TR is embedded in the top DR eigenvector, allowing it to capture the same underlying knowledge without eigendecomposition. Additionally, we provide empirical evidence of the TR as a viable alternative to existing representations in subsidiary applications, while requiring less computational overhead to learn, store, and use.

</details>


### [119] [Interpretability Without Tradeoffs: Disentangling Polysemanticity At Equal Predictive Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31304)
*Doğukan Bağcı,Bernt Schiele,Simone Schaub-Meyer,Jonas Fischer,Robin Hesse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ELUDe是一种无需训练、无损的 disentanglement 方法，能够在保持模型输出完全不变的前提下，将 DNN 的潜在表示分解为可解释的单义特征子单元，从而提升模型可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNN 中单个神经元往往编码多个不相关的概念（多义性），这阻碍了模型决策过程的理解。现有的稀疏自编码器等方法虽能分离混合信号，但通常需要改变模型结构，导致下游性能下降。

Method: ELUDe 通过重新组织层间信息流动的方式，将概念特定的贡献重新路由到清晰的、可检查的子单元中，同时通过构造性设计保证原始计算完全不变。该方法无需显式训练、无需标签，可直接应用于预训练模型。

Result: 在 DINOv2 和监督式 ViT-B/16 等多个视觉模型上，ELUDe 提升了可解释性，保持下游精度不变，运行高效，并支持模型表示操控等实际应用。

Conclusion: ELUDe 实现了几乎无需权衡的可解释性：在不损失性能的前提下，提供更清晰、可扩展且可操作的低成本模型洞察。

Abstract: Deep neural networks (DNNs) are widely used, but interpreting what they actually learn remains difficult. A major obstacle is that individual neurons often encode multiple unrelated concepts, obscuring the decision process of the network. While prior work, such as sparse autoencoders, can separate these mixed signals into more meaningful, "monosemantic" features, this typically requires altering the model in ways that can degrade downstream performance. To overcome this, we introduce ELUDe (explicit, lossless, unsupervised disentanglement), a method for improving the interpretability of DNNs while preserving their functional equivalence. ELUDe breaks latent representations into clear, inspectable sub-units that behave like interpretable features, while guaranteeing that the model's outputs remain exactly the same. It requires no explicit training, no labels, and can be applied to pretrained models. ELUDe works by reorganizing how information flows between layers, re-routing concept-specific contributions while preserving the original computation by construction. Across several vision models, including DINOv2 and supervised ViT-B/16, ELUDe improves interpretability, keeps downstream accuracy unchanged, runs efficiently, and supports practical uses such as steering model representations. In short, ELUDe offers interpretability (almost) without a tradeoff: clearer, scalable, and actionable model insights with no loss in performance.

</details>


### [120] [Non-Asymptotic Convergence of Stochastic Iterative Algorithms: A Lyapunov Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31309)
*Zaiwei Chen,Siva Theja Maguluri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于Lyapunov的随机迭代算法（随机逼近）有限时间分析方法，展示了广义Moreau包络作为通用Lyapunov函数的统一框架，涵盖随机梯度下降、线性SA及强化学习中的Q学习和时序差分学习，并扩展到马尔可夫噪声、半范数压缩算子、耗散算子及高概率界等场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为随机逼近算法的有限时间分析提供统一、自洽的理论框架，解决不同范数下Lyapunov函数选择问题，并将其系统应用于强化学习等场景。

Method: 利用广义Moreau包络作为通用Lyapunov函数，对算子压缩性和噪声结构进行分析，给出均方收敛保证；并扩展至马尔可夫噪声、半范数压缩算子、耗散算子及高概率界。

Result: 证明了广义Moreau包络框架可统一处理不同范数下的随机逼近收敛分析，并成功应用于SGD、线性SA、Q学习和TD学习等算法。

Conclusion: 该综述为随机逼近的有限时间分析提供了系统框架，同时指出了若干开放问题。

Abstract: We survey Lyapunov-based techniques for the finite-time analysis of stochastic iterative algorithms, also known as stochastic approximation (SA) algorithms, for solving fixed-point equations $\bar{F}(x)=x$, where the operator $\bar{F}(\cdot)$ can only be accessed through a noisy oracle. We first focus on the standard setting in which $\bar{F}(\cdot)$ is contractive with respect to some norm and the noise is i.i.d., and explain how generalized Moreau envelopes serve as universal Lyapunov functions, regardless of the underlying norm. We then show how this framework yields mean-square convergence guarantees and applies to stochastic gradient descent, linear SA, and value-based reinforcement learning algorithms such as Q-learning and temporal-difference learning. Finally, we discuss extensions to Markovian noise, seminorm-contractive operators, dissipative operators, and high-probability bounds, and conclude with open problems. The goal is to present a unified and self-contained roadmap for the finite-time analysis of SA and its applications, especially in reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [121] [Forgetting Has Neighbors: Localized Collateral Forgetting in Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31317)
*Polina Dolgova,Sebastian U. Stich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现机器遗忘方法存在局部化附带遗忘问题：即使整体指标良好，遗忘模型在靠近遗忘集的样本上预测与重训练模型差异更大。原因在于替代目标与局部预测结构不一致。提出局部教师蒸馏（Local Teacher Distillation）方法，用遗忘集邻近样本训练的教师模型生成软标签替代随机目标，有效缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘评估使用聚合指标（如准确率、遗忘分数）可能隐藏局部失败模式，需要细粒度的样本级评估来揭示这些缺陷。

Method: 提出局部教师蒸馏（Local Teacher Distillation）：在遗忘集邻近的保留样本上训练一个小型教师模型，用其生成的软标签替代梯度上升/随机标签方法中的随机目标，使替代目标与局部预测结构一致。

Result: 在CIFAR-100部分类别删除任务上，该方法使遗忘模型预测更接近重训练模型（尤其靠近遗忘集区域），同时保持有竞争力的聚合遗忘指标。

Conclusion: 证明了机器遗忘中局部化附带遗忘的存在及其机制（替代目标与局部预测结构不一致），并提出了有效的缓解策略。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove the influence of selected training examples without full retraining. Standard evaluations often summarize unlearning quality with aggregate metrics, such as accuracy- and forgetting-based scores, which can hide localized failures. We study this failure mode at the example level by comparing the predictions of an unlearned model to those of the model retrained after deletion. We show that this pointwise discrepancy can be highly non-uniform: for gradient-ascent and random-labeling methods, with and without retain-set fine-tuning, it grows with geometric proximity to the forget set. We call this phenomenon localized collateral forgetting. Our analysis identifies a mechanism behind the effect: surrogate targets used during unlearning can be inconsistent with the local prediction structure induced by retraining, and this inconsistency propagates through shared representations to nearby examples. Motivated by this mechanism, we propose Local Teacher Distillation, a simple mitigation strategy that replaces random targets with soft labels from a small teacher trained only on retained neighbors of the forget set. On CIFAR-100 partial-class deletion, this local teacher brings the unlearned model substantially closer to retraining, especially near the forget set, while maintaining competitive aggregate unlearning metrics.

</details>


### [122] [Inconsistency-Aware Minimization: Improving Generalization with Unlabeled Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31324)
*Hee-Sung Kim,Hyeonseong Kim,Sungyoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从信息几何视角推导的、无需标签即可计算的泛化度量——局部不一致性（local inconsistency），并基于此设计了不一致性感知最小化（IAM）方法，在监督、半监督和自监督学习中均能提升泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习中估计泛化差距和开发提升泛化的优化方法对理论和实践都很重要，利用无标签数据来实现这些目标在实际场景中具有显著优势。

Method: 从信息几何视角出发，提出局部不一致性度量，将其与Fisher信息矩阵和损失Hessian矩阵建立理论联系；进一步提出不一致性感知最小化（IAM），将局部不一致性融入训练目标。

Result: 经验上，局部不一致性与泛化差距相关；在标准监督学习中IAM达到与Sharpness-Aware Minimization等现有方法相当的性能；在半监督和自监督学习中IAM同样有效。

Conclusion: 局部不一致性是一种无需标签即可计算的泛化度量，基于此的IAM方法能有效提升多种学习范式下的泛化性能。

Abstract: Estimating the generalization gap and developing optimization methods that improve generalization are crucial for deep learning models, for both theoretical understanding and practical applications. Leveraging unlabeled data for these purposes offers significant advantages in real-world scenarios. This paper introduces a novel generalization measure, local inconsistency, derived from an information-geometric perspective on the parameter space of neural networks. A key feature of local inconsistency is that it can be computed without explicit labels. We establish theoretical underpinnings by connecting local inconsistency to the Fisher information matrix and the loss Hessian. Empirically, we demonstrate that local inconsistency correlates with the generalization gap. Based on these findings, we propose Inconsistency-Aware Minimization (IAM), which incorporates local inconsistency into the training objective. We demonstrate that in standard supervised learning settings, IAM enhances generalization, achieving performance comparable to that of existing methods such as Sharpness-Aware Minimization. Furthermore, IAM exhibits efficacy in semi- and self-supervised learning scenarios, where the local inconsistency is computed from unlabeled data.

</details>


### [123] [dashi: A Python library for Dataset Shift Characterization to Support Trustworthy AI Development and Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31360)
*David Fernández-Narro,Pablo Ferri,Ángel Sánchez-García,Juan M. García-Gómez,Carlos Sáez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 dashi，一个用于数据集偏移（dataset shift）探索、量化和表征的开源Python库。dashi 提供无监督方法（基于信息几何与非参数统计流形）和有监督方法（量化模型性能退化），支持时间维度和多源维度下的数据偏移分析，并在妊娠期糖尿病、COVID-19和紧急医疗调度三个健康AI案例中验证了其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据集偏移（包括时间偏移和多源偏移）会严重降低AI模型性能并影响数据质量，尤其在健康AI领域可能危及患者安全与基本权利。尽管协变量偏移、先验偏移和概念偏移的理论基础已较成熟，但缺乏易用且全面的软件工具来进行实际分析。

Method: dashi 提供双重分析路径：(1) 无监督方法：基于信息几何和非参数统计流形进行数据变异性表征，包括信息几何时间图（IG Temporal plots）以及全局概率偏差（Global Probabilistic Deviation）和源概率异常度（Source Probabilistic Outlyingness）等多源变异性度量；(2) 有监督方法：量化并表征模型性能退化。两种方法均支持用户自定义的时间批次和领域/源批次。

Result: 在三个健康AI案例研究（妊娠期糖尿病、COVID-19、紧急医疗调度）上验证了dashi的效用，展示了其通过交互式可视化分析和变异性度量来支持数据一致性和AI性能评估的能力。

Conclusion: dashi 通过提供数据集偏移的探索、量化和表征工具，支持AI生命周期各阶段的可信赖性，有助于构建稳健、安全的机器学习流程。

Abstract: The Artificial Intelligence (AI) life cycle requires a thorough understanding of the underlying data dynamics for robust, safe and cost-effective AI development and use. Dataset shifts are defined as changes between train and test data distributions. Whether occurring over time (temporal) or across different sites (multi-source), they can severely degrade model performance and compromise data quality. This is particularly important in health AI, where the safety and fundamental rights of patients can be severely affected by uncontrolled shifts both at training and operational stages. While the theoretical foundations of covariate, prior, and concept shifts are well established, there is a lack of accessible and comprehensive software tools to perform their analysis. We introduce dashi, an open-source Python library designed for the exploration, quantification, and characterization of dataset shifts. dashi provides a dual approach: an unsupervised approach that leverages information geometry and non-parametric statistical manifolds to data variability characterization and analysis (e.g., Information Geometric Temporal plots and Multi-Source Variability metrics like Global Probabilistic Deviation and Source Probabilistic Outlyingness), and a supervised approach that quantifies and characterizes model performance degradation. Both unsupervised and supervised approaches work across user-defined temporal and domain/source batches. We demonstrate the utility of dashi on three simulated and real-world health AI case studies on gestational diabetes mellitus, COVID-19 and emergency medical dispatch. By providing interactive visual analytics and variability metrics, dashi supports trustworthiness of AI life cycle stages enabling robust and safe machine learning pipelines through the assessment of data coherence and AI performance.

</details>


### [124] [Trading Complexity for Expressivity Through Structured Generalized Linear Token Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31367)
*Erwan Fagnou,Paul Caillon,Blaise Delattre,Alexandre Allauzen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，将token混合层的关键特征（直接影响与循环传播）分离，设计新的循环模式以在运行时间与表达能力之间实现可证明的权衡，并在合成任务和语言建模上验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有token混合层（如注意力机制、状态空间模型）在解码速度、内存需求和缓存大小之间存在必要权衡，缺乏统一的理论框架来理解和设计新的权衡方案。

Method: 提出一个统一框架，分离两个关键特征：(i) 单步生成中输入对输出的直接影响；(ii) 通过过去输出的循环信息传播。该框架不仅涵盖注意力和状态空间模型，还通过允许每个状态依赖多个过去状态（而非仅前驱）泛化循环方程。在此结构下设计新的循环模式，理论证明其复杂度并分析表达能力与运行时间的权衡。

Result: 在合成任务和语言建模上进行了实证验证，新设计的循环模式在理论上实现了期望的复杂度，并在表达能力与运行时间之间提供了有原则的权衡。

Conclusion: 该统一框架为跨模型家族的高效且富有表达力的token混合器的理解和设计提供了统一工具集。

Abstract: Token mixing layers play a key role in how language models can learn and generate long-range dependencies. Their efficiency relies on the necessary trade-off between decoding speed and the memory requirements, along with the cache size. Considering causal generation, this paper explores new trade-offs thanks to a unified framework which separates two crucial features: (i) the direct influence of inputs on outputs in one generation step; (ii) the recurrent propagation of information through past outputs. This framework encompasses major architectures such as attention and state-space models, but also generalizes the recurrence equations by allowing each state to depend on multiple past states rather than only the immediate predecessor. By introducing structure, we design new recurrence patterns that provably achieve the desired complexity, while providing theoretical insights on their expressivity -- trading runtime for expressivity in a principled way. Empirical validation is performed on synthetic tasks, along with language modeling. Together, these results provide a unified toolkit for the understanding and design of efficient and expressive token mixers across model families.

</details>


### [125] [A Unifying View of Variational Generative Wasserstein Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31369)
*Paul Caucheteux,Clément Bonet,Anna Korba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出生成Wasserstein流（GWF）统一框架，证明多种现有生成模型可视为参数化JKO方案实例，将框架扩展到IPM和MMD²，推导新算法并实验验证JKO正则化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型虽可统一为最小化分布散度，但依赖不同算法与几何原理，缺乏统一理论框架来理解它们之间的关系。

Method: 基于Wasserstein梯度流的JKO格式，建立参数化生成模型的统一理论框架，将f-散度、IPM、MMD²等不同目标函数纳入该框架，分析参数化Wasserstein流。

Result: 证明多种现有方法可推导为参数化JKO方案实例，建立若干近期提出算法间的等价关系，导出基于JKO的新生成算法，阐明与GANs的联系。

Conclusion: JKO正则化对不同目标函数的生成模型有显著影响，参数化Wasserstein流为生成建模提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Many modern generative models can be viewed as minimizing divergences between probability distributions, yet they rely on different algorithmic and geometric principles. Wasserstein gradient flows provide a continuous-time formulation for optimizing over distributions, and can be approximated through their implicit discretization via the Jordan-Kinderlehrer-Otto (JKO) scheme. In this work, we present a unified theoretical framework for generative modeling based on Wasserstein gradient flows, which we refer to as Generative Wasserstein Flows (GWF). We show that a broad class of existing methods can be derived as instances of parametric JKO schemes for $f$-divergence objectives, and we establish equivalences between several recently proposed algorithms. We extend this framework beyond f-divergence to Integral Probability Metrics and squared Maximum Mean Discrepancy, deriving new JKO-based generative algorithms, and clarifying their connections with GANs. We study empirically the impact of the JKO regularization for a wide set of objectives. Finally, we analyze parametric Wasserstein flows, where the dynamics are restricted to distributions induced by parametrized maps.

</details>


### [126] [Softsign: Smooth Sign in Your Optimizer For Better Parameter Heterogeneity Handling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31371)
*Dmitrii Feoktistov,Timofey Belinsky,Andrey Veprikov,Amir Zainullin,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SoftSignum和SoftMuon，通过平滑松弛符号优化中的硬符号映射为温度控制的软符号变换，实现从符号更新到幅值敏感更新的参数化过渡，并辅以自适应分位数温度调度，在多种深度学习任务（含LLM预训练）中优于传统符号优化器和AdamW。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于符号的优化器虽具有强性能和低内存占用，但其固定幅值更新机制与梯度幅值解耦，且未能考虑参数异质性，导致终端收敛时出现振荡而非收敛。

Method: 1) 提出SoftSignum，用温度控制的软符号变换替代硬符号映射，实现参数级别的从符号更新到SGD类幅值敏感更新的平滑过渡；2) 设计自适应分位数温度调度策略；3) 将相同原理扩展到矩阵值优化器得到SoftMuon；4) 基于强凸正则化子和Fenchel共轭建立广义几何松弛框架，证明其非凸随机收敛性。

Result: 在多种深度学习任务（包括LLM预训练）中，SoftSignum和SoftMuon一致优于基于硬符号的对应优化器以及标准AdamW。

Conclusion: 平滑松弛符号优化器能够有效解决硬符号优化器终端收敛振荡问题，同时保留其低内存优势，是替代传统优化器的有效方案。

Abstract: Sign-based and LMO-inspired optimizers have recently attracted substantial attention in deep learning due to their strong performance and low memory footprint. However, their fixed-magnitude updates can hurt terminal convergence: they decouple update mechanisms from gradient magnitudes and fail to account for parameter heterogeneity, often leading to oscillation rather than convergence. We propose SoftSignum, a smooth relaxation of sign-based optimization that replaces the hard sign map with a temperature-controlled soft-sign transformation, enabling a parameter-wise transition from sign-like updates to magnitude-sensitive SGD-like steps. We complement it with an adaptive quantile-based temperature schedule and extend the same principle to matrix-valued optimizers, obtaining SoftMuon. We also develop a generalized geometry-relaxation framework based on strongly convex regularizers and Fenchel conjugates, proving convergence in stochastic non-convex setting. Experiments on diverse deep learning tasks, including LLM pretraining, show that SoftSignum and SoftMuon consistently improve over their hard sign-based counterparts and standard AdamW.

</details>


### [127] [Constrained Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning with Max-Min Criterion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31388)
*Giseung Park,Hyunyoung Nam,Woohyeon Byeon,Amir Leshem,Youngchul Sung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将最大最小（max-min）准则与显式约束满足相结合的MORL框架，通过理论分析、收敛性证明以及在表格环境、建筑热控制、多目标运动控制和温室气体排放感知交通管理等多个领域的实验验证，展示了该方法在公平性和约束满足之间的有效平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的最大最小MORL方法在促进公平性方面有效，但在需要纳入约束条件时适用性受限，因此需要一种既能保证公平性又能显式满足约束的MORL框架。

Method: 提出一个集成max-min准则与显式约束满足的MORL框架，建立理论基础，通过收敛性分析和表格环境实验进行验证，并在建筑热控制、多目标运动控制、温室气体排放感知交通管理等实际场景中测试。

Result: 所提方法在多个领域中有效平衡了公平性和约束满足，并通过收敛性分析验证了算法的稳定性。

Conclusion: 该框架成功扩展了max-min MORL的适用性，使其能够在约束条件下实现公平的多目标决策，具有广泛的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning (MORL) extends standard RL by optimizing policies with respect to multiple, often conflicting, objectives. While max-min MORL has emerged as an effective approach for promoting fairness, its applicability remains limited, particularly when constraints must be incorporated. In this paper, we propose a MORL framework that integrates the max-min criterion with explicit constraint satisfaction. We establish a theoretical foundation for the proposed framework and validate the resulting algorithm through convergence analysis and experiments in tabular settings. We further demonstrate the practical relevance of our approach in simulated building thermal control, multi-objective locomotion control, and greenhouse-gas-emission-aware traffic management. Across these domains, our method effectively balances fairness and constraint satisfaction in multi-objective decision-making.

</details>


### [128] [Fixed Universal Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31423)
*Jingwen Liu,Alexandr Andoni,Daniel Hsu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通用Transformer（Universal Transformers），即固定参数的Transformer可以通过适当的输入嵌入模拟给定类别中的任何Transformer，类似于通用图灵机。研究表明，当嵌入维度足够大时，可以通过显式稀疏构造实现通用性，且随机初始化的Transformer几乎必然具有通用性。实验在括号平衡和多跳推理任务上验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Transformer的表达能力是否主要源于输入表示而非学习到的权重，以及是否存在固定的Transformer架构可以模拟其他任意Transformer。

Method: 提出通用Transformer概念，通过输入嵌入编码目标模型的描述，固定内部参数。提供了显式稀疏构造方法，并从理论上证明了随机初始化Transformer几乎必然具有通用性。

Result: 当嵌入维度足够大时，可以通过稀疏构造实现通用性；随机初始化的Transformer几乎必然（almost surely）是通用的；实验验证了括号平衡和多跳推理任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: Transformer的大量表达能力可能驻留在输入表示中而非学习到的权重中，这为理解Transformer的泛化能力提供了新视角。

Abstract: We introduce \emph{universal transformers}: fixed transformers that can simulate any transformer in a given class via a suitable input embedding. Analogous to a universal Turing machine, the input embedding encodes a description of the target model while all internal parameters remain fixed. We provide explicit sparse constructions achieving universality when the embedding dimension is sufficiently large, and further show that universality is generic: randomly initialized transformers are universal almost surely, which aligns with recent empirical results of Zhong and Andreas (2024). We empirically validate our theory on the algorithmic tasks of parenthesis balancing and multi-hop reasoning. Our results suggest that much of a transformer's expressive power may reside in its input representation rather than its learned weights.

</details>


### [129] [DG-CoLearn: An Efficient Collaborative Learning Framework for Dynamic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31427)
*Ashley Hoi-Ting Au,Zikun Zhang,Ligang He,Qiang Ni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DG-CoLearn，一种面向协作动态图学习的客户端透明框架，通过增量快照处理和服务端中介的嵌入交换机制，在保护跨客户端结构隐私的同时，实现高达33.8倍训练加速和27.4倍通信开销降低，并在节点分类和链接预测任务上持续提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态图学习方法因重复全快照重训练导致计算开销大，且不适用于数据分区的协作场景；跨分区边不可避免，但直接共享图结构会违反隐私约束。

Method: 提出DG-CoLearn框架，基于增量图快照处理，将计算聚焦于时态更新的图区域；通过时序建模保留历史信息；引入服务端中介的嵌入交换机制，在不暴露原始跨客户端结构信息的前提下实现准确的多跳消息传递。

Result: 训练时间加速最高33.8倍，通信开销降低最高27.4倍；节点分类F1提升最高13.36%，链接预测MAP提升最高8.27%。

Conclusion: DG-CoLearn有效桥接了协作动态图学习中的效率、可扩展性和客户端间结构隐私保护问题，验证了增量设计在全图处理流程中的一致性优势。

Abstract: Dynamic graph learning (DGL) is essential for modelling evolving graph data, but existing methods suffer from significant computational overhead due to repeated full-snapshot retraining and are not well-suited for collaborative settings with partitioned data. In realistic graph systems, cross-partition edges are unavoidable, but direct sharing of graph structure between clients may violate privacy constraints. We propose DG-CoLearn, a client-oblivious collaborative dynamic graph learning framework built on incremental graph snapshot processing, which focuses computation on graph regions affected by temporal updates while preserving historical information through temporal modelling. This incremental design is consistently applied across the entire graph processing pipeline, including a server-mediated embedding exchange mechanism to enable accurate multi-hop message passing without exposing raw cross-client structural information. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DG-CoLearn achieves up to 33.8$\times$ speedup in training time and 27.4$\times$ reduction in communication overhead, while consistently improving predictive performance on both node classification (up to 13.36% F1 improvement) and link prediction (up to 8.27% MAP improvement) tasks. These results highlight the effectiveness of DG-CoLearn in bridging efficiency, scalability, and client-to-client structural privacy in collaborative dynamic graph learning.

</details>


### [130] [Flow map learning in nonlinear vector autoregressive models: influence of the feature-library structure on the training error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31438)
*Markus Gross*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了非线性向量自回归（NVAR）/下一代储层计算（NG-RC）模型在时间序列预测中的可识别性问题，揭示了训练误差随时间分辨率的（预）渐近标度律，并指出当特征库与真实数据生成过程不匹配时，小训练误差与弱泛化能力并存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测通常需要学习非线性和时间延迟依赖关系，而NVAR/NG-RC模型是一类重要的预测模型。然而，这类模型的可识别性问题——即训练误差与泛化能力之间的关系——尚未被充分理解，尤其是在不同特征库和时间分辨率下的行为。

Method: 通过理论分析，考虑马尔可夫非线性动力系统的可识别性问题，推导训练误差作为时间分辨率函数的（预）渐近标度律；针对多项式向量场驱动的动力系统，使用单项式和傅里叶特征库验证机制；数值实验在多个混沌动力系统上验证理论预测。

Result: 训练误差的标度律取决于特征库能否精确表示流映射的早期Lie级数系数；延迟项可减少最优一步训练误差，但仅当特征库提供足够非线性时才改善长程预测；模型类与真实数据生成过程不匹配时，小训练误差与弱泛化共存。

Conclusion: NVAR/NG-RC模型的训练误差与泛化能力之间存在复杂关系：降低一步训练误差不一定保证长程预测性能提升，特征库的充分性比单纯增加延迟项更重要。

Abstract: Time series forecasting often requires learning nonlinear and time-delayed dependencies. A paradigmatic class of forecasting models are nonlinear vector autoregressive processes (NVAR), also known as next-generation reservoir computers (NG-RCs). These models approximate the Koopman operator on the space spanned by their explicit feature library. We consider the identifiability problem for learning Markovian nonlinear dynamical systems and show that the training error as a function of time resolution follows characteristic (pre-)asymptotic scaling laws. These laws depend on whether the feature library can represent the early Lie-series coefficients of the flow map (propagator) exactly or merely approximately. For dynamical systems governed by polynomial vector fields, we demonstrate the mechanism for NVAR/NG-RC models with monomial and Fourier feature libraries. We determine the dependence of the training error on the temporal resolution, the involved nonlinear degree, and the number of delay terms. While delay terms reduce the optimal one-step training error, they improve long-horizon forecasts only when the library provides sufficient nonlinearity. Thus, small training error coexists with weak generalization as the model class is mismatched to the true data-generating process. Numerical experiments on various chaotic dynamical systems confirm the theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [131] [DRIFT: Decoupled Rollouts and Importance-Weighted Fine-Tuning for Efficient Multi-Turn Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31455)
*Jian Mu,Tianyi Lin,Chengwei Qin,Zhongxiang Dai,Yao Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DRIFT提出了一种解耦的离线策略优化方法，通过从固定参考策略采样交互轨迹并计算基于回报的重要性权重，再使用加权监督微调来优化策略，在保持训练高效性的同时达到了与在线多轮强化学习相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线强化学习虽能有效处理多轮交互动态，但每次更新都需要生成完整校正轨迹，成本过高；而离线监督微调虽高效，却面临分布偏移和行为崩溃问题。因此需要一种兼具两者优势的方法。

Method: DRIFT将KL正则化强化学习目标重新表述为重要性加权监督学习问题，通过从固定参考策略离线采样交互轨迹、推导基于回报的重要性权重，然后对所得数据集执行加权SFT来优化策略，实现了rollout与优化的解耦。

Result: 实验表明DRIFT在性能上达到或超过了多轮强化学习基线，同时保持了标准监督微调的训练效率和简洁性。

Conclusion: DRIFT成功解决了在线RL的高成本与离线SFT的分布偏移之间的权衡困境，为多轮交互场景下的LLM优化提供了一种高效且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in multi-turn interactive settings where users or environments can iteratively provide lightweight feedback. Unfortunately, optimizing such behavior presents a sharp dilemma in practice: online reinforcement learning is able to effectively address multi-turn dynamics but is prohibitively expensive due to the cost of generating full correction trajectories at every update, whereas offline supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is efficient but suffers from distribution shift and behavioral collapse. To this end, we novelly propose DRIFT (Decoupled Rollouts and Importance-Weighted Fine-Tuning), a framework that operationalizes the theoretical insight that the KL-regularized RL objective is equivalent to importance-weighted supervised learning. DRIFT decouples rollout from optimization by sampling offline interaction trajectories from a fixed reference policy, deriving return-based importance weights, and optimizing the policy via weighted SFT on the resulting dataset. Empirically, we demonstrate that DRIFT matches or exceeds the performance of multi-turn reinforcement learning baselines while maintaining the training efficiency and simplicity of standard supervised fine-tuning. Code is available at https://github.com/2020-qqtcg/DRIFT.

</details>


### [132] [PithTrain: A Compact and Agent-Native MoE Training System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31463)
*Ruihang Lai,Hao Kang,Haozhan Tang,Akaash R. Parthasarathy,Zichun Yu,Junru Shao,Todd C. Mowry,Chenyan Xiong,Tianqi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MoE训练框架的Agent任务效率(ATE)指标，并构建了面向AI编码Agent的原生框架PithTrain和评估基准ATE-Bench。PithTrain在保持与生产框架相当吞吐量的同时，显著提升了Agent任务效率（Agent轮次减少62%，活跃GPU时间减少64%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE训练框架经过多年工程优化，但难以适应新架构和系统优化。AI编码Agent虽然可自动化部分开发工作，但现有框架对Agent不友好，存在隐形成本，而当前评估仅关注吞吐量，忽略了这一维度。

Method: 提出Agent任务效率(ATE)概念，基于四项Agent原生设计原则构建了紧凑型Agent原生MoE训练框架PithTrain，并设计了覆盖真实训练框架任务的ATE-Bench评估基准。

Result: PithTrain在吞吐量上与生产框架持平；在ATE-Bench上，Agent任务效率显著提升：Agent轮次减少62%，活跃GPU时间减少64%。

Conclusion: MoE训练框架设计应考虑Agent任务效率这一新维度，通过Agent原生设计原则构建的框架可以在不牺牲吞吐性能的前提下大幅提升Agent的开发效率。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) has become the dominant architecture for frontier language models. To meet this demand, production frameworks have built optimized MoE training stacks over years of engineering effort. Yet evolving these stacks for new architectures and system optimizations remains expensive. With the rise of AI coding agents, they could automate parts of training-framework development and accelerate this evolution. But applying them to these existing frameworks carries hidden costs, invisible to today's throughput-only evaluations. We name this missing dimension agent-task efficiency (ATE): the cost of using coding agents to understand, operate, and extend a framework. Grounded in four agent-native design principles, we build PithTrain, a compact, agent-native MoE training framework. We further introduce ATE-Bench, covering real-world training-framework tasks. Our evaluation shows PithTrain matches the throughput of production frameworks, and on ATE-Bench, PithTrain enables higher agent-task efficiency, with up to 62% fewer Agent Turns and 64% less Active GPU Time.

</details>


### [133] [Balanced LoRA: Removing Parameter Invariance to Accelerate Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31484)
*Valérie Castin,Kimia Nadjahi,Pierre Ablin,Gabriel Peyré*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoRA存在过参数化问题，导致不同低秩因子对的条件数差异显著，影响收敛速度。本文提出BaLoRA，将迭代投影到平衡流形上以改善损失景观的条件数，实现更快收敛和更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA虽然被广泛使用，但其过参数化特性导致不同低秩分解对的条件数差异很大，这直接影响收敛速度。作者希望通过改善条件数来提升LoRA的训练效率。

Method: 提出BaLoRA，在LoRA优化过程中将低秩因子投影到平衡流形上（该流形上矩阵的条件数得到优化），同时保持最终的适应矩阵不变。投影计算轻量，可无缝集成到现有微调流程中。

Result: BaLoRA相比标准LoRA收敛更快，在多种微调任务上取得更优性能。理论和实验均证实了不同低秩对的条件数差异及其对收敛速度的影响。

Conclusion: 通过平衡流形投影改善条件数是提升LoRA训练效率的有效手段，BaLoRA是一种简单高效的改进方案。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is the most widely adopted method for fine-tuning large language models. Notably, LoRA is inherently overparameterized: multiple pairs of low-rank factors can yield the same adapted weight matrix. We show--both theoretically and empirically--that these pairs exhibit significantly different condition numbers. As a result, converging to different loss minimizers directly impacts the convergence rate of LoRA. Building on this observation, we introduce Balanced Low-Rank Adaptation (BaLoRA), a variant of LoRA that projects iterates onto a balanced manifold. This manifold improves the conditioning of the loss landscape while preserving the adapted matrix. The projection step is computationally lightweight and integrates seamlessly into existing fine-tuning pipelines. Empirically, BaLoRA converges faster than standard LoRA and achieves superior performance across a range of fine-tuning tasks.

</details>


### [134] [Assign and Add: A Mechanistic Study of Compositional Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31497)
*Brady Exoo,Alberto Bietti,John Sous*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过变量赋值和模加法的简单控制实验，研究了Transformer中组合泛化的机制。发现小型Transformer能泛化到未见过的变量与数字组合，同一MLP模块可同时处理直接输入和间接变量赋值。训练动力学揭示了三个阶段：学习模加法、学习变量赋值结构、泛化到困难序列。提供了理论框架解释组合性如何从训练动力学中涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Transformer中组合泛化的具体机制，特别是模型如何组合已学技能以执行训练中未见过的复杂任务。

Method: 在变量赋值和模加法的简单控制环境中，将训练数据划分为不相交集合，通过机制分析、训练动力学经验分析和理论框架，研究小型Transformer的组合泛化能力。

Result: 发现同一"模加法"MLP模块可用于直接输入和间接变量赋值；训练动力学呈现三阶段：模加法学习→变量赋值结构学习→困难序列泛化精炼。

Conclusion: 组合泛化可以是Transformer内部机制组合性的自然结果。

Abstract: Large language models are able to compose skills in order to perform complex tasks, many of which might not have been seen during training. The details of how exactly this composition occurs remain elusive. In this paper, we study a mechanism for compositional generalization in transformers by considering a simple controlled setting involving variable assignment and modular addition. By partitioning our training data into disjoint sets, we observe that small transformers are able to generalize to previously unseen combinations of variables and numbers. Our mechanistic analysis shows that the same ``modular addition'' MLP module is used whether the inputs are given directly or indirectly through a separate variable assignment mechanism. We also analyze the training dynamics from an empirical lens, which reveals three phases of learning: first, modular addition is learned, then the structure required for variable assignment, and finally a refinement phase where the model generalizes to some hard sequences not seen in training. Finally, we provide a theoretical framework to explain how compositionality emerges from training dynamics. These results suggest that compositional generalization can be a natural consequence of the compositionality of internal mechanisms in~transformers.

</details>


### [135] [On Efficient Scaling of GNNs via IO-Aware Layers Implementations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31500)
*Daria Fomina,Daniil Krasylnikov,Alexey Boykov,Andrey Dolgovyazov,Vyacheslav Zhdanovskiy,Fedor Velikonivtsev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以I/O和算术强度为中心，将GNN层分为三类核函数族（SpMM卷积、规约聚合、注意力层），分别开发了减少数据移动、改善局部性的GPU内核。融合注意力核在Graph Transformer上最高提速3.9倍（中位1.6倍），Tensor Core变体最高7.3倍；GATv2最高提速8.5倍（中位2.0倍），峰值内存降低最高76倍（中位6倍）；规约核最高提速10倍（中位2.6倍）；SpMM层在多数评估中优于基线。同时发现图重排序的效果依赖于内核映射方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN框架（如DGL、PyTorch Geometric）存在稀疏、不规则的显存访问瓶颈，复杂层会物化边级中间结果，增加显存流量并限制大规模图的扩展性。

Method: 将GNN层分为三类核函数族：SpMM基卷积、规约基聚合、注意力层（GATv2/Graph Transformer）。针对每类开发优化的GPU内核，包括融合注意力核、度数感知规约核、缓存优化的cuSPARSE核。同时研究图重排序对不同核映射的影响。

Result: 融合注意力核：Graph Transformer最高3.9×加速（中位1.6×），Tensor Core变体最高7.3×；GATv2最高8.5×加速（中位2.0×），峰值内存降低最高76×（中位6×）。度数感知规约核最高10×加速（中位2.6×）。缓存优化的cuSPARSE在SpMM层多数评估中优于基线。

Conclusion: 通过I/O和算术强度感知的优化，不同类型GNN层可显著加速；图重排序效果依赖于具体的核函数映射方式。方法作为即插即用替代方案开源。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are bottlenecked by sparse, irregular memory access. Popular frameworks such as DGL and PyTorch Geometric support general message passing, but complex layers often materialize edge-wise intermediates, increasing memory traffic and limiting scalability on large graphs. We take an I/O- and arithmetic-intensity--centric view and show that widely used layers fall into three kernel families: SpMM-based convolutions, reduction-based aggregations, and attention-based layers (GATv2/Graph Transformer). For each family, we develop GPU kernels that reduce data movement, improve locality, and remain robust across realistic graphs. We also study graph reordering and find that its impact depends on the kernel mapping: it benefits neighbor-parallel (gather-dominated) kernels more consistently than feature-parallel designs. Empirically, our fused attention kernels reach up to $\textbf{3.9}\times$ speedup for Graph Transformer (median $\textbf{1.6}\times$), with Tensor Core (block-sparse) variants up to $\textbf{7.3}\times$ on locally dense graphs; for GATv2 we reach up to $\textbf{8.5}\times$ speedup (median $\textbf{2.0}\times$) while reducing peak memory by up to $\textbf{76}\times$ (median $\textbf{6}\times$). Our degree-aware reduction kernels achieve up to $\textbf{10}\times$ speedup (median $\textbf{2.6}\times$). For SpMM-based layers, properly cached cuSPARSE achieves up to $\textbf{8}\times$ speedup over DGL and outperforms evaluated custom baselines in the majority of evaluations. We release our implementations as drop-in replacements to support reproducible, hardware-aware GNN acceleration.

</details>


### [136] [When Are Multimodal Predictions Biologically Supported? A Diagnostic Evaluation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31504)
*Dylan Steiner,Gustavo Arango-Argoty,Gerald Sun,Etai Jacob*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DECAT是一个模型无关的事后评估框架，用于区分多模态医学表示中的共享生物学、单模态特异信号和虚假关联。在合成数据和TCGA真实数据上验证发现，CLIP等纠缠模型虽能完美检测共享生物学，但大部分情况下会错误声明共享生物学存在，且该错误率随混杂强度增加而上升。DECAT能检测AUROC无法发现的混杂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态肿瘤学模型能产生准确预测，但高准确性无法揭示模型学到的是跨模态共享的生物学、单一模态独有的生物学，还是反映混杂因素的虚假关联。现有方法缺乏区分这些诊断场景的能力。

Method: DECAT提出基于五种零假设参考指标和规则化决策程序的事后评估框架，将多模态表示分类为四种诊断场景。该方法不依赖具体混杂标签，在表示层面操作，证据不足时返回不确定结果。

Result: 在超过2,500个训练表示的合成数据和8,979名TCGA患者的真实数据上验证。纠缠模型（如CLIP）共享生物学检测近乎完美，但在真实基础模型嵌入中多数情况下虚假声称共享生物学存在；该错误率随混杂强度增大而上升。DECAT能在无需混杂标签的情况下检测AUROC不可见的混杂。

Conclusion: DECAT提供了一种无需混杂标签即可检测多模态表示中虚假关联的方法，揭示了现有纠缠模型在共享生物学诊断中的局限性，为多模态医学模型的可靠评估提供了新工具。

Abstract: Multimodal models in oncology can produce accurate predictions, but accurate prediction does not reveal whether the model has learned biology that is shared across modalities, biology confined to one modality, or spurious correlations that reflect confounders rather than genuine biology. We introduce DECAT, a model-agnostic post-hoc evaluation framework that classifies multimodal representations into four diagnostic scenarios for a given task and modality, using five null-referenced metrics and a rule-based decision procedure. The framework operates on learned representations, requires no knowledge of which specific confounder is present, and returns indeterminate when the evidence is insufficient. We validate DECAT on synthetic data across four multimodal model classes (over 2,500 trained representations) and on real data from 8,979 TCGA patients, evaluating both multimodal embeddings and five pretrained pathology foundation models. Entangled models (e.g., CLIP) achieve near-perfect shared biology detection but falsely claim shared biology in the majority of cases where it is absent on real foundation model embeddings. This false claim rate increases with confound strength so that larger cohorts and stronger representations produce more confident but still incorrect diagnoses. Applied to both multimodal TCGA embeddings and five pathology foundation models without paired RNA, DECAT detects confounding invisible to AUROC without requiring the confounder labels, as confirmed by post-hoc stratification.

</details>


### [137] [Skill Reuse as Compression in Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31509)
*Zhikun Xu,Yu Feng,Jacob Dineen,Taiwei Shi,Jieyu Zhao,Ben Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ReuseRL方法，基于最小描述长度(MDL)原则，通过从成功轨迹中提取可复用的技能字典，并在强化学习目标中加入分割代价惩罚，使大语言模型智能体学到更通用、可压缩的行为模式，在多个基准上提升了分布内外的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RL训练的大语言模型智能体容易学到脆弱、任务特定的捷径，泛化能力差。作者假设当成功轨迹在结构上可压缩、能分解为一小组可复用的抽象模式时，智能体泛化能力更强。

Method: ReuseRL方法：1) 从成功轨迹中提取共享的技能字典；2) 在RL目标中加入分割代价，显式惩罚编码效率低的 idiosyncratic 行为；3) 基于MDL原则，用PAC-Bayes泛化界证明了压缩惩罚的有效性。

Result: 在ALFWorld、TextWorld-Cooking和Countdown-Stepwise三个基准上，ReuseRL在分布内和分布外测试中均优于vanilla GRPO和强round-length基线方法。

Conclusion: 压缩成功轨迹的结构（用MDL原则惩罚低效编码）可以显著提升LLM智能体的泛化能力，这为将压缩与泛化理论联系提供了实证和理论支持。

Abstract: Large language model agents trained with reinforcement learning (RL) often learn brittle, task-specific shortcuts. We hypothesize that agents generalize better when their successful trajectories are structurally compressible, decomposed into a small set of reusable abstract patterns. To formalize this, we introduce ReuseRL, which grounds agentic RL in the Minimum Description Length (MDL) principle. ReuseRL extracts a shared skill dictionary from successful trajectories and augments the RL objective with a segmentation cost, explicitly penalizing idiosyncratic behaviors that encode poorly. We prove a PAC-Bayes generalization bound for this compression penalty. Across ALFWorld, TextWorld-Cooking, and Countdown-Stepwise, ReuseRL improves in- and out-of-distribution success over vanilla GRPO and strong round-length baselines.

</details>


### [138] [On the Relationship Between Activation Outliers and Feature Death in Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31518)
*Elana Simon,Etowah Adams,James Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 稀疏自编码器（SAEs）中特征死亡问题源于激活空间中的维度级离群值，这些离群值导致某些特征在初始化时获得永久性负预激活而永不激活。通过均值中心化预处理可消除该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释不同模型间SAE特征死亡率差异巨大的原因（从GPT-2的近零到AlphaFold3的70%以上），并解决因特征死亡造成的字典容量浪费和叠加问题重现。

Method: 定义了离群严重度指标 γ = ‖μ‖/‖σ‖；在454个模型-层组合（涵盖语言、视觉、蛋白质、基因组模型）上验证了γ与初始死亡率的相关性（Spearman ρ=0.89/0.82）；分析了死特征复活机制，发现均值中心化可消除离群导致的死亡。

Result: γ可高精度预测初始特征死亡率；死特征恢复需要SAE偏置学习激活均值，在高γ下极其缓慢；均值中心化在所有测试模型上完全消除离群诱发的特征死亡。

Conclusion: 特征死亡的根本机制是维度级激活离群值导致的初始化偏差，均值中心化是一种原理性的必要预处理步骤。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) decompose neural network activations into interpretable features, but many learned features never activate, a problem called feature death that wastes dictionary capacity and can reintroduce superposition. Death rates vary dramatically between models: near-zero on GPT-2, over 70% on AlphaFold3 with identical configurations. We find that dimension-level activation outliers (dimensions whose mean magnitude is large relative to per-token variation) cause this by shifting pre-activations at initialization based on each feature's alignment with the activation mean. Features anti-aligned with the mean receive permanently negative pre-activations and never fire. We formalize outlier severity as $γ= \|μ\|/\|σ\|$; it predicts initial death rates (Spearman $ρ= 0.89$ for dead-by-TopK, $0.82$ for dead-by-ReLU) across 454 model-layer combinations spanning language, vision, protein, and genomic models. Dead features can revive during training, but recovery requires the SAE bias to learn the activation mean, a process that is prohibitively slow at high $γ$. Mean-centering (subtracting the activation mean) sidesteps this and eliminates outlier-induced death across all tested models, confirming the mechanism and providing a principled basis for when and why this preprocessing step is necessary.

</details>


### [139] [Chem-PerturBridge: a harmonized compendium of small molecule perturbation transcriptomic effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31522)
*Artur Szałata,Olga Novitskaia,Maiia Shulman,Matthew Mella,Altynbek Zhubanchaliyev,Fabian J. Theis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Chem-PerturBridge是一个整合了37k+化合物、136种细胞环境和125万转录组样本的多数据集资源，通过标准化标识符和元数据实现跨数据集分析。研究发现不同数据集间同化合物条件的logFC排序一致性较弱，但方向一致性较强。该资源作为预训练数据可提升化合物表示学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有小分子建模所需的转录组资源分散于不同技术平台、元数据规范、对照设置、剂量条件和预处理流程中，缺乏统一的跨数据集资源来支持大规模扰动模型训练和跨数据集诊断评估。

Method: 构建Chem-PerturBridge：整合37k+化合物、136种细胞环境和125万转录组样本，涵盖8种检测类型，标准化标识符和元数据，计算复本感知的条件级效应。基于该资源进行跨数据集匹配条件一致性评估和数据集内复本一致性评估。在化合物留出(OP3)分割和11个数据集上的分子留出评估中测试预训练效果。

Result: 不同数据集间同化合物条件的细粒度logFC排序和幅度一致性普遍较弱，常低于同环境不同化合物的基线；但logFC方向一致性显著更稳定，通常超过基线。Chem-PerturBridge预训练嵌入在OP3分割上优于L1000-only嵌入、Morgan指纹和OP3基线；在11个数据集上的分子留出评估中，基于该资源训练的模型优于或持平未使用该资源的模型。

Conclusion: Chem-PerturBridge既支持跨数据集签名一致性的诊断评估，也支持异构扰动转录组数据的模型重用，为大规模化合物建模提供了统一的基础资源。

Abstract: Large perturbation models require training data encompassing chemical, cellular, and assay diversity. Current transcriptomic resources for small-molecule modeling, however, are fragmented across technologies, metadata conventions, controls, doses, and preprocessing pipelines. We introduce Chem-PerturBridge, a harmonized multi-dataset resource comprising over 37k compounds, 136 cellular contexts, and 1.25M transcriptomic samples across eight assay types, with standardized identifiers, metadata, and replicate-aware condition-level effects. We use the resource to evaluate matched-condition agreement across datasets and replicate agreement within datasets. Matched same-compound conditions generally show weak agreement in fine-grained logFC rankings and magnitudes across most dataset pairs, often falling below same-context different-compound baselines. In contrast, logFC direction agreement is substantially more stable and usually exceeds these baselines. We further evaluate Chem-PerturBridge as a pretraining resource for compound representation learning. Under a compound-held-out OP3 evaluation split, embeddings pretrained on Chem-PerturBridge improve over L1000-only embeddings, Morgan fingerprints, and the descriptor-free OP3 baseline across metrics. An extensive molecule-holdout evaluation across 11 datasets further shows that models trained on Chem-PerturBridge outperform or match those that are not. Chem-PerturBridge therefore supports both diagnostic evaluation of cross-dataset signature agreement and model-oriented reuse of heterogeneous perturbation transcriptomic data.

</details>


### [140] [The Dynamic-Probabilistic Consistency Gap in Chaotic Surrogate Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31547)
*Andre Herz,Matthijs Pals,Daniel Durstewitz,Georgia Koppe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了动力系统重构（DSR）中存在动态-概率一致性差距（DPC gap）：追求有限时域概率目标会破坏动力学特性或使预测不确定性与局部切线动力学脱钩。作者提出KAFFEE框架，基于可微扩展卡尔曼滤波训练，通过评估局部预测残差（创新）的似然并利用学习到的局部Jacobian矩阵传输协方差，有效缓解了核心坍缩、噪声掩蔽和盲不确定性三种失效模式。在随机超混沌Lorenz-96系统上，KAFFEE相比开环目标改善了动力学不变量重建，并在13个混沌系统的概率适应性DSR基础模型中实现了上下文贝叶斯滤波，同时保持了零样本动力学性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力系统重构中，现有的有限时域概率目标（如开环高斯 rollout 目标）会导致模型在混沌系统中产生两种问题：一是动力学退化（如核心坍缩），二是预测不确定性与系统实际的局部切线动力学（由Jacobian矩阵刻画）脱钩。这种动态-概率一致性差距（DPC gap）会严重影响不确定性估计的可靠性，尤其是在混沌系统中，不确定性的正确演化对模型可信度至关重要。

Method: 提出KAFFEE（Kalman-Aware Framework For Ergodic Emulation）框架，其核心是可微扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）训练方法：在每个时间步，利用当前估计状态和学习的Jacobian矩阵预测下一个状态的先验分布，然后通过观测值计算创新（innovation）及其似然作为训练损失；同时通过EKF的协方差更新规则，将不确定性通过Jacobian矩阵在时间上向前传播。这种方法使得模型的协方差演化与局部切线动力学（Jacobian）自然耦合，避免了开环目标中的优化捷径。

Result: 在随机超混沌Lorenz-96系统上，KAFFEE减少了核心坍缩、噪声掩蔽和盲不确定性三种失效模式，改进了动力学不变量（如Lyapunov指数）的重建，同时保持了有竞争力的预测分数。在13个混沌系统上对DSR基础模型进行概率适应时，KAFFEE实现了上下文贝叶斯滤波，同时基本保持了零样本的动力学性能。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统识别了DSR中的DPC gap及其三种机制，并证明基于EKF的闭环训练范式可以有效弥合这一差距。KAFFEE为将概率建模与动力学保真度统一起来提供了一个通用框架，对混沌系统的可靠不确定性估计和基础模型适应性部署具有重要意义。

Abstract: Dynamical systems reconstruction (DSR) aims to learn surrogate models that capture the dynamics underlying time-series data. Reliably deploying these surrogates requires uncertainty estimates consistent with the learned dynamics. We expose a dynamic-probabilistic consistency (DPC) gap: the pursuit of finite-horizon probabilistic objectives can degrade dynamics or decouple predictive uncertainty from the local tangent dynamics it ought to reflect. We isolate three mechanisms behind this gap: core collapse, noise masking, and blind uncertainty. Specifically, we show that open-loop Gaussian rollout objectives can penalize Jacobian-generated covariance growth in chaotic systems, encouraging optimization shortcuts that weaken physical expansion or decouple uncertainty from it. To mitigate this gap, we propose KAFFEE (Kalman-Aware Framework For Ergodic Emulation), a differentiable extended Kalman filter-based training framework that evaluates likelihood on local predictive residuals (innovations) while transporting covariance through learned local Jacobians. On stochastic hyperchaotic Lorenz-96, KAFFEE reduces the identified failure modes, improves reconstruction of dynamical invariants relative to open-loop objectives, and maintains competitive predictive scores. We further show that the DPC gap appears when probabilistically adapting a DSR foundation model across 13 chaotic systems, where KAFFEE enables in-context Bayesian filtering while largely preserving zero-shot dynamics.

</details>


### [141] [Positional versus Symbolic Attention Heads: Learning Dynamics, RoPE Geometry, and Length Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31558)
*Felipe Urrutia,Juan José Alegría,Cinthia Sanchez Macias,Jorge Salas,Cristian B. Calderon,Cristobal Rojas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过在两种结构等价的多跳推理任务（数字位置推理和字母符号推理）上训练GPT-J，研究了注意力头的学习动态。研究发现成功学习伴随着纯注意力头的涌现（即头表现为纯位置型或纯符号型），并揭示了两种机制在长序列外推能力上的定量差异：符号机制更可靠地外推到更长序列，而位置机制面临更严格的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Transformer解决结构化任务的机制及其在新场景中的行为，对于安全部署至关重要。已有研究缺乏对注意力头在结构化任务中学习动态的深入理解，尤其是在多跳推理任务中位置机制与符号机制的区分。

Method: 在两种结构等价的多跳推理任务（数字位置推理任务和字母符号推理任务）上训练解码器-only Transformer（GPT-J），使用分类注意力头行为为位置型或符号型的度量指标，分析学习动态，并结合理论构造展示单层RoPE注意力如何通过几何可解释的QKV操作实现这些功能。

Result: 成功学习伴随着纯注意力头（位置型或符号型）的涌现；数字任务需要位置型和符号型两种头，而字母任务仅需符号型头；符号机制在长序列外推上更可靠，位置机制面临更严格限制，该差异通过新提出的"差异度"（discrepancy）概念形式化。

Conclusion: 位置机制和符号机制在Transformer中扮演不同的计算角色，对序列长度具有不同的鲁棒性。符号机制能更可靠地外推到长序列，而位置机制存在固有的局限性。这些发现有助于预测模型在新场景中的行为，对安全部署有重要意义。

Abstract: Transformer-based language models are widespread in today's society. As such, understanding the mechanisms by which they solve structured tasks and predicting how they may behave in novel scenarios is of great importance for safe deployment. We study the learning dynamics of attention heads in a controlled setting by training a decoder-only Transformer (GPT-J) on two structurally equivalent multi-hop reasoning tasks: a number task requiring positional reasoning and a letter task requiring symbolic reasoning. Using a recently introduced metric that classifies attention-head behavior as positional or symbolic for a given prompt, we show that successful learning is associated with the emergence of pure heads, i.e., heads that express themselves as either positional or symbolic. Despite the tasks' structural equivalence, they impose different mechanistic demands: the number task requires both positional and symbolic heads, whereas the letter task requires only symbolic heads. We then identify the computational roles of these heads, characterize the basic functions they implement, and give theoretical constructions showing how single-layer RoPE-based attention can realize these functions through geometrically interpretable query, key, and value operations. This analysis yields a quantitative separation between positional and symbolic mechanisms in their robustness to longer sequences, formalized through a novel notion of discrepancy. We empirically validate the resulting predictions in both controlled and real-world models, showing that symbolic mechanisms extrapolate more reliably to longer sequences while positional mechanisms face sharper limitations.

</details>


### [142] [Functional Attention: From Pairwise Affinities to Functional Correspondences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31559)
*Jiefang Xiao,Maolin Gao,Simon Weber,Guandao Yang,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Functional Attention，一种将注意力机制重新解释为函数空间中对应对应关系的算子学习方法，通过结构化线性算子替代softmax亲和度，实现紧凑、可泛化、分辨率不变的全局依赖建模，在PDE求解、3D分割和回归任务中达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于Transformer的算子学习方法通常将连续场视为离散token并通过token级注意力处理，忽略了全局函数结构，缺乏对无限维函数空间的真正理解。

Method: 受几何函数映射启发，将注意力重新解释为自适应基之间的函数对应关系，用结构化线性算子替代softmax亲和度，从而获得紧凑、可泛化且分辨率不变的表示。

Result: 在PDE求解、3D分割和回归等多种算子学习任务中达到SOTA性能，且对不同离散化方式具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Functional Attention通过函数空间视角重新定义注意力机制，有效捕获全局依赖，为算子学习提供了一种更结构化的方案。

Abstract: Learning mappings between infinite-dimensional function spaces, or operator learning, is essential for many machine learning applications. Although transformer-based operators are popular, they often rely on token-wise attention. These methods treat continuous fields as discrete tokens and usually ignore the global functional structure. We introduce \emph{Functional Attention}, which reinterprets attention as a functional correspondence between adaptive bases. Inspired by geometric functional maps, our method replaces softmax affinities with structured linear operators. This yields a compact, generalizable, resolution-invariant representation that explicitly captures global dependencies. Experiments demonstrate that \emph{Functional Attention} can match state-of-the-art performance in many operator learning tasks, including solving PDEs, 3D segmentation, and regression, while remaining robust to varying discretizations. Project page is available at https://github.com/xjffff/FUNCATTN.

</details>


### [143] [Effective Biological Representation Learning by Masking Gene Expression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31562)
*Kian Kenyon-Dean,Alina Selega,Ihab Bendidi,Jordan M. Sorokin,Luca Bertinetto,David Errington,Hayley Donnella,Oren Kraus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TxFM模型，通过掩码自编码方法对RNA-seq数据进行自监督表征学习，在精心构建的DiverseRNA-1.4M数据集上训练，证明归纳式自监督学习在转录组学表征中优于线性基线模型，甚至超越规模大100倍以上的图谱级模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有转录组基础模型在技术噪声和批次效应下表现不如线性基线，引发深层表征学习相比原始转录本计数是否具有优势的疑问，因此需要探索自监督学习的有效性。

Method: 提出TxFM模型，采用面向多样化RNA-seq计数数据的掩码自编码方法，通过消融研究识别关键的架构配置，并构建DiverseRNA-1.4M公开训练语料。

Result: TxFM在精心策划的DiverseRNA-1.4M数据集上训练后，生成的基因表征质量超越了在规模大100倍以上的图谱级语料上训练的基础模型。

Conclusion: 归纳式自监督学习是转录组学表征的可行建模方法，关键在于模型架构与训练数据策划的精细结合。

Abstract: RNA sequencing produces rich and diverse datasets of gene expression, offering compelling insights into cellular state and function that have many applications in drug discovery. Modeling such data is challenging due to inherent technical noise and experimental batch effects, as evidenced by many existing transcriptomic foundation models (FMs) underperforming relative to linear baselines. Such results raise the question of whether deep representation learning provides a distinct advantage over the direct use of raw transcript counts. Our work explores this by developing a new self-supervised model, TxFM, with a focus on inductive representation learning evaluations. TxFM employs a masked autoencoding approach tailored to diverse RNA-seq count data, and our ablation study empirically identifies crucial architecture configurations required for strong transfer performance. Additionally, we curate a public training corpus, DiverseRNA-1.4M, and find that TxFM trained on this curated dataset yields high-fidelity gene representations that outperform FMs trained on atlas-scale corpora over 100x larger. Overall, our results indicate that inductive self-supervised learning is a viable modeling approach for transcriptomics representation, provided a careful synthesis of model architecture and training data curation.

</details>


### [144] [Giving Sensors a Voice: Multimodal JEPA for Semantic Time-Series Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31580)
*Utsav Dutta,Gerardo Pastrana,Sina Khoshfetrat Pakazad,Henrik Ohlsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CHARM模型，利用通道级文本描述和Transformer编码器，通过JEPA目标和新损失函数学习多变量时间序列的通用表示，在异常检测、分类和预测任务中仅用线性探针即取得强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用多变量时间序列的表示学习尚未被充分探索，现有Transformer在语言和视觉领域成功，但缺乏针对异构多变量时间序列的通用表征方法。

Method: CHARM包含三个关键设计：(1) 通道级文本描述集成到与通道顺序等变的Transformer编码器中；(2) 采用联合嵌入预测架构(JEPA)进行训练；(3) 设计新损失函数促进信息丰富且时间稳定的嵌入，潜空间预测增强对传感器噪声的鲁棒性，描述感知门控提供可解释性。

Result: 在异常检测、分类、短期和长期预测任务中，仅用线性探针即可获得强性能。性能主要来自JEPA目标和条件架构，文本描述作为通道标识符辅助跨数据集泛化。

Conclusion: CHARM成功将文本描述与Transformer结合，为多变量时间序列提供了通用表征学习方法，验证了JEPA目标和通道级条件架构的有效性，且文本描述在跨数据集泛化中起关键标识作用。

Abstract: Transformer-based architectures have advanced sequence modeling in language and vision, yet general-purpose representation learning for heterogeneous multivariate time series remains underexplored. We introduce CHARM (Channel-Aware Representation Model), which incorporates channel-level textual descriptions into a Transformer encoder equivariant to channel order. CHARM is trained with a Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) and a novel loss promoting informative, temporally stable embeddings; latent-space prediction encourages robustness to sensor noise while description-aware gating provides interpretability through learned inter-channel relationships. Across anomaly detection, classification, and short- and long-term forecasting, the learned embeddings achieve strong performance using only a linear probe. Performance is driven primarily by the JEPA objective and conditioning architecture, with text descriptions serving as channel identifiers for cross-dataset generalization.

</details>


### [145] [A Tight Theory of Error Feedback Algorithms in Distributed Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31594)
*Daniel Berg Thomsen,Adrien Taylor,Aymeric Dieuleveut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对分布式学习中的梯度压缩误差反馈算法EF和EF21，通过识别最优步长选择和构建最优Lyapunov函数，提供了紧的收敛性分析，结果与智能体数量无关，并在单智能体情形下恢复已知的最优保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式学习中通信开销是主要瓶颈，梯度压缩虽能减少通信但会降低收敛性能，误差反馈机制可缓解此问题，但已有多种变体且其相对性能尚不明确。

Method: 通过识别两类误差反馈算法（EF和EF21）的最优步长，并为其各自构建优化的Lyapunov函数进行收敛分析。

Result: 给出了两种算法紧的收敛性保证，结果独立于智能体数量，并在单智能体场景下达到已知最优界。

Conclusion: 误差反馈算法EF和EF21在最优步长和恰当Lyapunov函数下能实现紧的收敛性，且其性能不随智能体数量退化。

Abstract: Communication costs are a major bottleneck in distributed learning and first-order optimization. A common approach to alleviate this issue is to compress the gradient information exchanged between agents. However, such compression typically degrades the convergence guarantees of gradient-based methods. Error feedback mechanisms provide a simple and computationally cheap remedy for this issue, but numerous variants have been proposed, and their relative performance remains poorly understood. This paper provides tight convergence analyses for two of the main error-feedback algorithms from the literature, the classic Error Feedback method (EF) and Error Feedback 21 (EF21), by identifying optimal step-size choices and constructing optimal Lyapunov functions tailored to each method. The results hold independently of the number of agents and recover the known best guarantees possible in the single-agent regime.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [146] [Physics-informed Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning under Hybrid Contact Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30503)
*Vittorio Giammarino,Anastasios Manganaris,Ahmed H. Qureshi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对接触丰富操作任务中物理信息目标条件强化学习（Pi-GCRL）方法失效的问题，提出了接触感知和分层公式，通过选择性应用物理信息归纳偏置来改善性能，为Pi-GCRL在接触丰富操作中的扩展提供了原则性步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Pi-GCRL方法在导航和无物体目标到达领域表现良好，但在接触丰富操作任务中，接触交互导致的混合动力学、模式依赖可控性及非光滑价值景观使得这些方法性能下降，需要提出新的解决方案。

Method: 通过分析接触丰富操作中混合动力学、模式依赖可控性和非光滑价值景观等结构特性对现有Pi-GCRL方法的影响，提出接触感知（contact-aware）和分层（hierarchical）公式，在操作问题中选择性应用物理信息归纳偏置。

Result: 所提出的接触感知和分层公式有效克服了现有Pi-GCRL方法在接触丰富操作中性能下降的问题，为该方法在此类任务中的推广提供了可行方案。

Conclusion: 本文为实现Pi-GCRL在接触丰富操作领域的扩展迈出了原则性的一步，表明了在复杂接触动力学场景下选择性应用物理信息归纳偏置的重要性。

Abstract: Learning to reach arbitrary goals from sparse feedback requires agents to infer a rich notion of reachability across state--goal pairs. Goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL) tackles this challenge by learning policies that generalize across goals, but this generalization becomes increasingly difficult as the underlying dynamics become high-dimensional, hybrid, or contact-dependent. To address this issue, physics-informed GCRL (Pi-GCRL) introduces optimal-control-inspired inductive biases into goal-conditioned value learning. While Pi-GCRL methods have proven effective in navigation and object-free goal-reaching domains, their reliability in contact-rich tasks remains unclear, where contact interactions induce hybrid dynamics, mode-dependent controllability, and nonsmooth value landscapes. In this work, we show that these structural properties can cause existing Pi-GCRL methods to degrade when applied naively to contact-rich manipulation. Motivated by this analysis, we introduce contact-aware and hierarchical formulations that apply physics-informed inductive biases selectively across the manipulation problem. Our results provide a principled step toward extending Pi-GCRL to contact-rich manipulation.

</details>


### [147] [Learning-Based Navigation for Indoor Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30468)
*Tri-Tin Nguyen,Tien-Dat Nguyen,Gia-Uy Le,Vinh Nguyen,Vinh-Hao Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合监督学习的全局规划器（从A*专家轨迹学习）和基于学习改进的DWA局部规划器（先行为克隆再PPO优化）的室内移动机器人导航框架，在仿真和真实环境中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统导航方法在全局和局部规划中分别存在路径次优和参数敏感等问题，本文旨在通过端到端的学习方法整合全局与局部规划，提升室内移动机器人在障碍物环境中的导航安全性和可靠性。

Method: 全局规划器：基于成本感知的A*专家轨迹进行监督学习训练；局部规划器：构建于DWA动作格上的离散候选选择策略，先通过行为克隆进行初始训练，再使用PPO算法在可行性感知掩码下进行强化学习微调。

Result: 在仿真和真实室内环境中，该方法能够生成可行的全局路径和可靠的局部运动指令，实现存在障碍物条件下的安全目标导向导航。

Conclusion: 将学习型全局规划与强化学习微调的局部控制相结合，是解决室内移动机器人导航问题的有效途径。

Abstract: This paper presents a learning-based navigation framework for indoor mobile robots. The proposed method combines a supervised neural global planner, trained from cost-aware A* expert trajectories, with the proposed Learning-Based DWA local planner, which is formulated as discrete candidate selection over the Dynamic Window Approach (DWA) action lattice. For local planning, the policy is first trained by behavior cloning and then refined by Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) under feasibility-aware masking. The framework is implemented and evaluated in both simulated and real-world indoor environments. Experimental results show that the proposed method generates feasible global routes and reliable local motion commands for safe goal-directed navigation in the presence of obstacles. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of integrating learning-based global planning with reinforcement-learning-refined local control for indoor mobile robot navigation. The source code will be released at https://ntdathp.github.io/rl_robot_web/.

</details>
