In simulation experiments, we provide an exhaustive analysis of the benefits of using spatially-resolved tactile feedback: while an ideal (high-resolution) tactile sensor allows solving the task almost perfectly, a sensor with lower spatial resolution (here 4x4 taxels) still leads to an improvement of up to 20% compared to using only the fingers' joint sensors. For this analysis, we further train an estimator alongside the policy that predicts the ground truth contact positions. Finally, we demonstrate the successful sim-to-real transfer for the DLR-Hand II equipped with a tactile skin.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [Strengthening Polymorphic Prompt Assembling: Dynamic Separator Generation Against Emerging Prompt Injection Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30534)
*Nima Dorzhiev,Peng Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出动态每请求分隔符生成方法（基于时间戳、会话ID和随机数的SHA-256摘要），替代PPA的静态分隔符池，将泄露暴露限制在单个请求内，在Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct-Turbo和DeepSeek-V4-Flash上验证了攻击成功率从0.88降至0.38（2.3倍缓解），泄露率从0.467降至0.000，额外开销仅2.7微秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PPA方法使用固定分隔符池防御提示注入，但静态池复用存在爆破半径漏洞：一旦分隔符泄露，可在未来请求中被持续利用。

Method: 采用域隔离的SHA-256摘要，以时间戳、会话标识符和密码学随机数为密钥，为每次请求动态生成唯一的(BEGIN, END)金丝雀分隔符对。

Result: 针对M1混淆载荷，动态模式将ASR从0.88降至0.38（2.3倍缓解），Wilson置信区间无重叠；format_breakout_salad载荷的静态泄露率0.467在动态模式下完全消除（0.000）。

Conclusion: 动态分隔符生成有效消除爆破半径漏洞，无需模型微调，与现有PPA SDK向后兼容，仅增加2.7微秒组装开销。

Abstract: Polymorphic Prompt Assembling (PPA) defends LLM agents against prompt injections by randomly selecting separator pairs from a fixed pool to isolate user input from system instructions. Although effective, static pool reuse exposes a blast-radius vulnerability: once a separator leaks, it can be exploited in future requests. We propose a dynamic per-request separator generation using domain-separated SHA-256 digests keyed on the timestamp, session identifier, and cryptographic nonce. Each assembled prompt receives a unique (BEGIN, END) canary pair, thereby limiting leakage exposure to a single request. We evaluated our extension against 16 injection payloads on Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct-Turbo, with cross-model validation on DeepSeek-V4-Flash model. Against the M1 obfuscation payload (leetspeak + urgency), the dynamic mode reduces the Attack Success Rate (ASR) from 0.88 to 0.38, yielding a statistically significant 2.3 x mitigation verified by non-overlapping 95% Wilson confidence intervals. Against format_breakout_salad, static separator leakage (leak_rate = 0.467) is eliminated entirely in the dynamic mode (0.000), confirming the blast-radius reduction in practice. The implementation requires no model fine-tuning, adds 2.7 microseconds prompt-assembly overhead per request, and is backward compatible with the existing PPA SDK.

</details>


### [186] [AdvScene: Rethinking Adversarial Patch Evaluation Through Scene Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30578)
*Xiaoyong,Yuan,Lan,Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AdvScene框架，用于在重建的真实场景中测量对抗性补丁的场景鲁棒性，通过操作包络线（视角、距离、场景上下文）来评估补丁的有效性，并引入APSE方法解决跨视角歧义问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法无法很好地衡量对抗性补丁在真实部署场景中的鲁棒性：真实图像基准虽真实但固定不变，仿真器虽可控但缺乏真实场景基础。

Method: 提出场景接地框架AdvScene，将评估重构为操作测量问题；形式化为约束提升问题，引入对抗性补丁到场景嵌入（APSE）方法，解决跨视角歧义同时保持攻击关键外观、局部性、目标表面附着和跨视角一致性。

Result: 使用真实物理数据验证，发现对抗性补丁的攻击效果存在显著场景依赖性变化，这是现有图像中心或仿真器评估无法捕捉的。

Conclusion: AdvScene能够揭示补丁在真实部署场景中的操作边界，比现有评估方法更准确地反映对抗性补丁的实际风险。

Abstract: Adversarial patches are physical patterns attached to real objects to mislead AI vision systems. Their real-world risk is not determined by a single successful prediction, but by whether they remain effective after deployment under changing viewpoints, distances, and scene conditions. We refer to this property as scene robustness, the effectiveness of a deployed patch across conditions in a real environment. Yet existing evaluations do not measure scene robustness well: real image benchmarks are realistic but fixed, while simulators are controllable but not grounded in a specific real scene.
  We present AdvScene, a scene-grounded framework for measuring the scene robustness of adversarial patches in reconstructed real environments. AdvScene reframes evaluation as operational measurement: given a fixed deployed patch, it characterizes the patch's operational envelope - where and when the attack succeeds - as a function of viewpoint, distance, and scene context. A key challenge is that the attack is typically defined only in a single anchor view, while evaluation requires a representation that remains faithful under viewpoint changes. We formalize this as a constrained lifting problem and introduce Adversarial Patch-to-Scene Embedding (APSE), which resolves cross-view ambiguity while preserving attack-critical appearance and enforcing locality, target-surface attachment, and cross-view consistency. We validate AdvScene using real-world physical data and conduct a comprehensive evaluation of existing adversarial patches. Our results show that AdvScene reveals substantial scene-dependent variation in attack effectiveness that is not captured by existing image-centric or simulator-based evaluations.

</details>


### [187] [Audio Pirates: Black-box Audio Watermark Removal via Diffusion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30614)
*Lingfeng Yao,Xincong Zhong,Chenpei Huang,Xuandong Zhao,Hanqing Guo,Aohan Li,Jiang Liu,Tomoaki Ohtsuki,Miao Pan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出DiffErase，一种黑盒水印去除攻击方法，通过将带水印音频扰动到中间扩散噪声水平并利用预训练去噪模型重建，有效去除水印同时保持感知质量。实验表明，不可闻音频水印极易受到此类攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印去除攻击要么显著降低感知质量，要么需要访问水印方案，缺乏实用且有效的黑盒攻击方法。

Method: DiffErase将带水印音频扰动到中间扩散噪声水平，然后使用预训练去噪模型重建音频，从而抑制水印信号。

Result: 在多个音频域上，DiffErase一致地去除水印同时保持感知质量，证明了不可闻音频水印的脆弱性。

Conclusion: 未来音频水印设计需要考虑基于扩散模型的威胁。

Abstract: With the rise of AI-generated audio, watermarking has become widely used for detecting misuse and protecting intellectual property. However, adversaries may try to remove these watermarks, making it critical to evaluate how well watermarking schemes withstand removal attacks. Existing attacks are often impractical: they either noticeably degrade perceptual quality or require access to the watermarking scheme. We propose DiffErase, a black-box watermark removal attack that assumes no knowledge of the target watermarking scheme while maintaining perceptual quality. DiffErase perturbs watermarked audio to an intermediate diffusion noise level and regenerates it using a pretrained denoising model, effectively suppressing watermark signals. Theoretical analysis and extensive experiments demonstrate that inaudible audio watermarks are highly vulnerable: across multiple audio domains, DiffErase consistently removes watermarks while preserving perceptual quality. These findings highlight the need for future audio watermarking designs to consider diffusion-based threats. Code and demos are available at https://differase.github.io/DiffErase/.

</details>


### [188] [When AI Meets Wall Street: A Survey on Trustworthy AI in Fintech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30650)
*Qingwen Zeng,Zhenghao Zhao,Yitian Yang,Yiqi Zhu,Fangchen Liu,Zhaoge Bi,Moe Thandar Kyaw Wynn,Kim-Kwang Raymond Choo,Huaming Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个以生命周期为中心、机制驱动的统一框架，用于分析金融AI管道中的安全与鲁棒性问题。该框架将金融AI分为三个阶段（训练与更新、部署与推理、运营与监控反馈），并提出了金融AI安全与鲁棒性分类法，涵盖17种攻击子类型（包括数据和模型投毒、对抗性攻击、提示注入、深度伪造等），分析了每种攻击的算法策略、可行性约束、隐蔽性与持久性以及下游金融后果，最后指出了开放挑战和研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究要么将AI视为防御工具，要么以领域无关的方式分析对抗性机器学习，忽略了金融领域特有的约束（如会计合理性、非IID联邦数据、持续重训练、自动化放大的下游效应）。本文旨在填补这一空白，提供统一、针对金融AI生命周期的安全分析框架。

Method: 提出了生命周期中心（lifecycle-centric）和机制驱动（mechanism-driven）的统一框架；将金融AI划分为三个生命周期阶段；构建了金融AI安全与鲁棒性分类法，归纳17种攻击子类型；对每种子类型从算法策略、可行性约束、隐蔽性与持久性、下游金融后果四个维度进行分析。

Result: 构建了一个涵盖17种攻击子类型的金融AI安全分类法；统一分析了金融AI全生命周期的攻击面；识别了当前金融AI安全研究中的开放挑战。

Conclusion: 本文为金融AI安全提供了系统性分析框架，并指出了未来研究方向，包括生命周期感知的压力测试和金融相关的鲁棒性基准。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is now embedded as a primary decision engine in continuously operated financial AI pipelines spanning training and updating, deployment and inference, and operation with monitoring and feedback. The automation and scale that make these pipelines effective also create novel attack surfaces, where small algorithmic perturbations can amplify into persistent, system-level financial harm. Existing surveys, however, either treat AI as a defensive tool or analyse adversarial machine learning in a domain-agnostic manner, abstracting away finance-specific constraints such as accounting plausibility, non-IID federated data, continuous retraining, and automation-amplified downstream effects. We address this gap with a unified, lifecycle-centric and mechanism-driven framework. We partition financial AI into three lifecycle stages: training and updating, deployment and inference, and operation, monitoring, and feedback. We further propose the Financial AI Security and Robustness Taxonomy, organising seventeen attack subtypes across data and model poisoning, adversarial attacks on decision boundaries, prompt injection in LLM-mediated workflows, and deepfake-driven subversion of KYC verification layers. For each subtype, we analyse algorithmic strategy, feasibility constraints, stealth and persistence, and downstream financial consequences. Finally, we identify open challenges and outline a research agenda toward lifecycle-aware stress testing and finance-relevant robustness benchmarks.

</details>


### [189] [Depth-Dependent Indirect Prompt Injection in Tool-Calling ReAct Agents: Injection Depth, Payload Framing, and Turn-Budget Sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30686)
*Mohammadreza Rashidi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过4个对照研究（共460次试验）系统评估了ReAct智能体在工具调用中面临的间接提示注入风险。研究发现：注入深度是主导变量——GPT-4o-mini的攻击成功率（ASR）从深度1的60%衰减至深度4-5的0%；Claude Haiku在所有深度均保持0% ASR；框架效应（中性25% vs 人格化75%）未达统计显著性；轮次上限（3/5/7）不影响ASR。仅清洗首个工具观测可捕获67%的成功注入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅在固定条件下评估攻击成功率（ASR），未探索注入深度、修辞框架（framing）和轮次上限三个风险维度。

Method: 在5类攻击场景的20个情景中，对GPT-4o-mini和Claude Haiku进行4项对照研究，共460次试验，总API成本低于0.36美元。控制注入深度（1-5）、框架（中性/指令/推理/人格化/权威）和轮次上限（3/5/7）三个变量。

Result: 深度1时GPT-4o-mini的ASR为60%，深度4-5降至0%（Cramer's V=0.58）；Claude Haiku在所有深度ASR均为0%。框架在深度1使ASR在25%-75%间波动但未达统计显著性。轮次上限对ASR无显著影响。

Conclusion: 注入深度是影响ASR的主导变量，仅清洗首个工具观测即可捕获大部分成功注入；Claude Haiku表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Abstract: ReAct agents that interleave chain-of-thought reasoning with tool calls are increasingly deployed for real tasks such as scheduling, file retrieval, and data access. Their tool observation loop creates a direct attack surface: an adversary who controls any tool's return value can embed instructions that redirect the agent away from the user's goal, a threat known as indirect prompt injection. Existing benchmarks evaluate attack success rate (ASR) at a fixed injection position under fixed conditions, leaving three risk dimensions unexplored: where in the tool sequence the payload appears (injection depth), what rhetorical register it uses (framing), and how many turns the agent is permitted (turn cap). We conduct four controlled studies on 20 scenarios spanning five attack categories, totalling 460 trials against GPT-4o-mini and Claude Haiku at a combined API cost under 0.36 USD. Study 1 shows that ASR against GPT-4o-mini decays from 60% at depth 1 to 0% at depths 4 and 5 (Cramer's V = 0.58, p < 0.001; restricted to within-sequence depths 1-3: V = 0.47, p = 0.0013), driven by model resistance at depth 1 and task completion before payload encounter at deeper positions. Study 2 replicates the depth experiment on Claude Haiku, which achieves 0% ASR at every depth through a combination of conservative tool invocation and genuine instruction resistance. Study 3 shows that framing modulates ASR between 25% (neutral) and 75% (persona) at depth 1, a 50-percentage-point range that does not reach statistical significance at N = 20 per condition. Study 4 confirms that ASR is stable across turn caps of 3, 5, and 7, indicating the turn budget is not a risk factor in this setting. Our results establish injection depth as the dominant variable and show that sanitising only the first tool observation captures 67% of measured injection successes.

</details>


### [190] [Triaging Threats to Specialized Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30693)
*Wenjie Jacky Mo,Xiaofei Wen,Rui Cai,Boyu Zhu,Sicong Jiang,Zihan Wang,Minglai Yang,Zhe Zhao,Muhao Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出GuardZoo基准（32,460样本，15个不安全类别）评估护栏模型鲁棒性，发现单一模型存在任务干扰问题，进而提出RouteGuard路由-专家框架，通过分派对话至专用专家护栏实现细粒度威胁检测，在域内和域外评估中均优于强基线，并支持模块化扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全护栏数据集覆盖零散的风险子集且依赖不一致的分类体系，导致难以评估护栏模型在多样化真实场景中的泛化能力。

Method: 1) 构建GuardZoo统一人工标注基准（32,460样本，15个不安全类别）；2) 提出RouteGuard路由-专家框架，将对话路由至专门的专家护栏进行威胁特定检测。

Result: GuardZoo评估发现单一护栏模型存在任务干扰问题；RouteGuard在细粒度威胁检测和域外泛化上优于强基线护栏模型。

Conclusion: 路由-专家框架能有效缓解任务干扰，提升护栏鲁棒性，且支持灵活模块化扩展以应对新兴威胁。

Abstract: Building robust safety guardrails is essential for deploying Large Language Models across diverse real-world applications. However, this goal remains challenging because safety risks span heterogeneous threat domains, while existing datasets cover only fragmented risk subsets and rely on inconsistent taxonomies. Consequently, it remains unclear whether current guardrails can generalize beyond narrow evaluation settings. To better understand the robustness of guardrail models, we first introduce GuardZoo, a unified human-annotated benchmark with 32,460 samples covering 15 distinct unsafe categories. Evaluation on GuardZoo reveals that monolithic guardrails suffer from task interference: different threat domains require distinct decision boundaries that are difficult to compress into a single model. We therefore propose RouteGuard, a router-expert framework that triages each conversation to specialized expert guardrails for threat-specific detection. Experiments show that RouteGuard improves fine-grained threat detection over strong guardrail baselines, generalizes better under out-of-domain evaluation, and supports flexible modular expansion to emerging threats.

</details>


### [191] [FASR: Automated Identification of Unsafe Control Actions in STPA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30697)
*Ian Dardik,Yining She,Sam Procter,Keaton Hanna,Lutz Wrage,Eunsuk Kang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为FASR的自动化工具，通过结合模型工程和形式化方法（特别是鲁棒性分析），自动完成STPA危害分析中的不安全控制动作（UCA）识别。基于BSCU航电系统案例和九人用户研究表明，该工具能有效辅助分析人员，但仍需改进以提升通用性和可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STPA虽然被广泛应用于安全关键系统，但其大部分步骤依赖人工执行，耗时且易出错，目前缺乏有效的自动化支持。

Method: 提出FASR工具，基于模型工程和形式化方法，利用鲁棒性分析技术将UCA识别转化为控制器动作中不良偏差的自动检测。

Result: 在BSCU航电系统案例中完成工具演示；九名参与者的用户研究显示，大多数参与者认为该工具有助于UCA识别，并提出了提升普适性的改进建议。

Conclusion: FASR初步验证了利用鲁棒性分析自动化STPA的可行性，但需进一步改进以适用于更广泛的系统和分析师群体。

Abstract: The System-Theoretic Process Analysis (STPA) is a well-established hazard analysis technique that has been applied to a wide range of safety-critical systems. Despite its popularity, there is relatively little automation support for STPA, and most of its steps are carried out manually by a human analyst, which can be time consuming and error prone. This paper investigates the potential use of model-based engineering and formal methods to assist human analysts in efficiently and accurately carrying out STPA. The proposed tool, called FASR (Formalizing and Automating STPA with Robustness), enables automated, complete identification of unsafe control actions (UCAs), leveraging recent advances in robustness analysis to identify UCAs as undesirable deviations in the controller's actions. The use of the tool is demonstrated on a case study involving a Braking System Control Unit (BSCU) in an avionics system. As a preliminary exploration of the potential benefits and limitations of the tool, the paper reports on a user study involving nine participants with varying backgrounds in STPA, model-based engineering, and formal methods; the study found that most participants considered the tool a useful aid in identifying UCAs, while suggesting improvements that would make a tool such as FASR usable and applicable to a wider range of systems and analysts.

</details>


### [192] [Differentially Private Preference Data Synthesis for Large Language Model Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30808)
*Fengyu Gao,Jing Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DPPrefSyn算法，首次实现差分隐私合成偏好数据的生成，用于大语言模型的隐私保护偏好对齐，在强隐私保证下仍能保持有竞争力的对齐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实人类偏好数据包含敏感的用户提示和判断，在偏好对齐的后训练阶段带来隐私风险，现有方法缺乏对偏好数据的差分隐私保护机制。

Method: 基于Bradley-Terry偏好模型和成对偏好数据的内在几何结构，首先从私有数据中学习带差分隐私保证的偏好模型，再利用该模型与公共提示合成高质量偏好数据；通过利用每簇奖励模型的共享线性结构捕获异构偏好，并使用DP-PCA提升学习精度。

Result: 实验结果表明，DPPrefSyn在强差分隐私保证下实现了有竞争力的对齐性能。

Conclusion: 合成偏好数据可作为隐私保护偏好对齐的实用替代方案，适用于广泛的应用场景。

Abstract: Preference alignment is a crucial post-training step for large language models (LLMs) to ensure their outputs align with human values. However, post-training on real human preference data raises privacy concerns, as these datasets often contain sensitive user prompts and human judgments. To address this, we propose DPPrefSyn, a novel algorithm for generating differentially private (DP) synthetic preference data to enable privacy-preserving preference alignment. DPPrefSyn is a principled framework grounded in the Bradley-Terry preference model and the intrinsic geometric structure of pairwise human preference data. It first learns an underlying preference model from private data with formal differential privacy guarantees, and then leverages the learned model together with public prompts to synthesize high-quality preference data. It exploits the shared linear structure of per-cluster reward models to effectively capture heterogeneous human preferences in private datasets, and leverages DP Principal Component Analysis (DP-PCA) to improve learning accuracy. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that DPPrefSyn achieves competitive alignment performance under strong DP guarantees. These findings highlight the potential of synthetic preference data as a practical alternative for privacy-preserving preference alignment across a broad range of applications. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to generate DP synthetic preference data for LLM alignment. Our code is available at https://github.com/gfengyu/Differentially-Private-Preference-Data-Synthesis.

</details>


### [193] [Send a SCOUT First: Pre-hoc Reasoning for Adaptive Detector Allocation in Prompt-Injection Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30837)
*Shuhao Zhang,Jiarui Li,Qi Cao,Ruiyi Zhang,Pengtao Xie*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出SCOUT框架，通过动态预测每个检测器在单个样本上的可靠性和延迟，实现异构检测器的自适应分配，在减少攻击成功率46%的同时降低40%时间开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示注入检测器存在异构性，每个检测器在不同攻击切片上表现各异，但现有系统仍采用固定单检测器管道，导致每个请求都落入某一检测器的盲区。

Method: SCOUT框架通过预测每个检测器在相似历史输入上的行为来动态决定每个请求该运行哪些检测器以及是否需要升级到LLM裁判，并对外暴露一个安全-效用阈值供操作者调节。

Result: 在SCOUT-450基准上，安全导向工作点相比始终启用GPT-4o裁判降低46%攻击成功率和40%总时间开销，仅牺牲5.1点良性效用；同时在BIPIA、IPI、IHEval三个外部基准上提升安全-效用边界。

Conclusion: 将防御重新定义为检测器分配问题，通过动态调度异构检测器池来避免单一检测器的盲区，在安全性和效用之间实现更优权衡。

Abstract: Prompt-injection detectors are heterogeneous: each is strong on a different slice of attacks, and none is always reliable. Yet existing systems still treat detection as a fixed single-detector pipeline, committing every request to one detector's blind spots. We reframe defense as detector allocation: given a heterogeneous pool, decide per request which detectors to run and whether to escalate to an LLM judge. Our framework SCOUT (Scalable and Controllable Outcome-prediction for Uncertainty-aware Triage) makes this decision dynamic by predicting each detector's per-sample reliability and latency from how it behaved on similar past inputs, and exposes a single safety-utility threshold to the operator (where utility bundles benign-pass rate and wall-clock). To evaluate this setting, we build SCOUT-450, a benchmark that captures the structurally complex, agent-facing injections that older prompt-injection sets under-represent. On SCOUT-450, a safety-oriented operating point reduces attack-success rate by 46% and total wall-clock by 40% relative to an always-on GPT-4o judge, at a 5.1-point benign-utility drop. SCOUT also transfers to three external benchmarks (BIPIA, IPI, and IHEval), improving the safety-utility frontier.

</details>


### [194] [A Core-Structure-Based Automated Analysis Tool for Commercial Virtualization Obfuscation Deobfuscation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30902)
*Wanju Kim,Seoksu Lee,Eun-Sun Cho*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析虚拟化混淆技术并提出VMPredator工具，通过内存分析和轨迹分析自动提取语义单元，将混淆程序长度缩减约85%，并能完整恢复小规模程序的原始语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟化混淆作为一种强大的混淆技术被越来越多地应用于恶意软件，给分析人员带来巨大的时间和精力消耗，现有工具无法处理多种形式的虚拟化混淆。

Method: 提出VMPredator工具，执行内存分析和轨迹分析等多种分析，最小化对虚拟机内部结构的依赖以处理多样化的虚拟化混淆，自动提取语义单元。

Result: 混淆程序长度缩减约85%，小规模程序被完全恢复至与原始程序相同的语义。

Conclusion: VMPredator能够有效应对多种形式的虚拟化混淆，显著缩短混淆程序并恢复原始语义，为恶意软件分析提供有力支持。

Abstract: Virtualization obfuscation is a more powerful obfuscation technique compared to other obfuscation methods, and as it is increasingly being applied to malware, it demands significant effort and time from analysts. This study analyzes virtualization obfuscation and proposes a tool called VMPredator that automatically extracts semantic units. The proposed tool performs various analyses including memory analysis and trace analysis, while minimizing dependency on the specific internal structure of virtual machines in order to handle diverse forms of virtualization obfuscation that existing tools are unable to process. Experimental results demonstrate that the length of obfuscated programs was reduced by approximately 85%, and it was verified through validation that small-scale programs were fully restored to semantics identical to the original.

</details>


### [195] [Toward Accessible Mobile Money: A Voice-Driven, Biometrically Secured USSD Automation Framework for Visually Impaired Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31375)
*Sunday Ajayi,Babatunde Eric Olatunji,Eric Umuhoza*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Android的智能中间件，通过集成生物识别安全PIN注入和隐私保护的屏幕变暗机制（黑屏模式），解决了视障用户在使用USSD移动货币服务时面临的可访问性和安全性问题。该系统将任务成功率从65-75%提升至90%以上，交易完成时间从40-60秒缩短至12-15秒，并提高了感知安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲通过USSD技术提供的移动货币服务显著扩大了金融包容性，但视障用户在使用这些服务时面临可访问性和安全障碍。当前的USSD系统非为无视觉交互设计，迫使用户依赖第三方协助甚至输入PIN，增加了欺诈风险并降低了交易信心。虽然存在屏幕阅读器等辅助技术，但它们与USSD操作不兼容，常导致会话超时。

Method: 本文提出了一种基于Android的智能中间件，该中间件自动执行USSD交易，集成生物识别安全PIN注入，并引入隐私保护的屏幕变暗机制——黑屏模式。系统利用Android辅助功能服务、硬件支持的密钥库安全和设备端自然语言解析，实现独立、安全的基于语音的移动货币访问。

Result: 该方案将任务成功率从65-75%提升至90%以上，交易完成时间从40-60秒缩短至12-15秒，同时提高了感知安全性。

Conclusion: 该智能中间件能够有效解决视障用户使用USSD移动货币服务的可访问性和安全问题，显著提升任务成功率和交易效率，并增强用户的安全感。

Abstract: Financial inclusion has expanded significantly across Africa through mobile money services delivered primarily via USSD technology. However, visually impaired individuals continue to face accessibility and security barriers when conducting financial transactions. Current USSD systems are not designed for non-visual interaction, forcing users to rely on third-party assistance even for PIN entry, thereby increasing fraud exposure and reducing transaction confidence. Although alternative assistive technologies such as screen readers exist, they are not compatible with USSD operations, often causing sessions to time out before the user can complete a transaction. This paper presents an Android-based intelligent middleware that automates USSD transactions, integrates biometric-secured PIN injection, and introduces a privacy-preserving screen-dimming mechanism: Blackout Mode. The system leverages Android Accessibility Services, hardware-backed Keystore security, and on-device natural language parsing to enable independent, secure voice-based mobile money access. We show that the proposed solution improves task success rates from 65-75% to more than 90% and reduces transaction completion time from 40-60 seconds to 12-15 seconds, while also improving perceived security.

</details>


### [196] [Free-Riding in the AI Economy: Demystifying Logic Flaws in x402-Enabled Payment Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30998)
*Shengchen Ling,Yihang Huang,Yuan Chen,Yajin Zhou,Lei Wu,Cong Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对x402协议生态进行系统性安全分析，发现了签名语义鸿沟（跨资源替换攻击）和时间同步鸿沟（并发竞态条件导致服务重复）两类核心漏洞。在AI推理场景中，攻击者可利用动态授权方案的同步缺陷迫使商户补贴算力成本，实现高达100%的资源泄漏。提出了请求绑定签名和悲观状态锁定等架构缓解方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: x402协议作为机器对机器支付的事实标准，在桥接同步HTTP请求与异步区块链最终性时存在状态同步挑战，但缺乏系统性安全分析。

Method: 形式化定义五项安全不变量，对官方SDK和线上部署进行漏洞验证，涵盖签名设计语义分析、并发竞态条件分析、动态定价模型攻击面分析。

Result: 发现两类核心漏洞：跨资源替换攻击（支付证明被移植到未授权上下文）和并发服务重复（概率性资源重复获取）；在AI推理场景验证了资源泄漏率可达100%。

Conclusion: 提出请求绑定签名和悲观状态锁定作为架构级缓解方案，所有发现已向Coinbase和ThirdWeb披露。

Abstract: The agentic economy demands programmatic financial rails, positioning the x402 protocol as the de facto standard for machine-to-machine payments. However, bridging synchronous HTTP requests with asynchronous blockchain finality introduces profound state synchronization challenges. In this work, we perform the first comprehensive security analysis of the x402 ecosystem. By formalizing five Security Invariants, we reveal that current implementations fail to enforce transactional atomicity and cryptographic context binding, leading to systemic vulnerabilities. We identify a semantic gap in signature design enabling cross-resource substitution, where payment proofs are transplanted to other unauthorized contexts. Furthermore, we expose a temporal gap where concurrency race conditions allow probabilistic service duplication. In the AI inference domain, we demonstrate how dynamic pricing models are vulnerable to allowance overdrafts and infrastructure rate limits. We validate these vulnerabilities against official SDKs and live deployments. Specifically, we show that attackers can exploit the synchronization gap in dynamic authorization schemes to force merchants to subsidize compute costs, achieving a resource leakage ratio of up to 100% on production middleware. Finally, we propose architectural mitigations, advocating for request-bound signatures and pessimistic state locking to secure the financial rails of autonomous agents. All discovered issues have been disclosed to Coinbase and ThirdWeb.

</details>


### [197] [From Prompt Injection to Persistent Control: Defending Agentic Harness Against Trojan Backdoors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31042)
*Jiejun Tan,Zhicheng Dou,Xinyu Yang,Yuyang Hu,Yiruo Cheng,Xiaoxi Li,Ji-Rong Wen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了ClawTrojan基准测试来揭示本地智能体工作区中的多步木马攻击（通过文件或工具输出的提示注入实现持久化控制），并设计了DASGuard防御框架，通过追踪控制内容来源并清除非可信源的指令，实现运行时阻断与工作区清理，在GPT-5.4上攻击成功率95.5%，而现有方法几乎为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体从对话机器人演变为真实工作区的操作工具（读写文件、调用工具、跨会话复用状态），这种能力带来了新的攻击面：攻击者可以将提示注入隐藏在文件或工具输出中，智能体读取、存储并在后续执行这些隐藏指令。这种多步木马攻击范式中，单步操作看似无害，但组合起来可将不可信文本转化为持久控制内容。现有防御方法逐步骤孤立检查，能阻断明显有害行为但无法检测早期植入后门的写操作，因此需要揭示和应对这种威胁。

Method: 1）提出ClawTrojan基准测试，在OpenClaw风格的模拟工作区中构造多步木马攻击场景；2）设计DASGuard防御框架，扫描本地敏感文件中的控制类文本，追踪其来源，移除来自非可信源的控制内容。

Result: 在GPT-5.4上，ClawTrojan达到95.5%的攻击成功率（ASR）；现有的单轮提示注入攻击在同一模型上的ASR接近零。DASGuard实现了强动态防御，结合运行时攻击阻断与工作区的已清理提交。

Conclusion: 多步木马攻击对本地智能体工作区构成真实威胁，现有逐步骤孤立的防御方法无法有效检测；ClawTrojan可作为评估此类攻击的基准，DASGuard通过来源追踪与内容清除提供了有效的动态防御方案。

Abstract: LLM agents are evolving from conversational chatbots to operational tools in real-world workspaces. In local agentic harnesses, an LLM can read and write files, call tools, and reuse workspace state across sessions. While such capabilities enhance utility, they also expose a new attack surface for attackers. Attackers can embed a prompt injection within a file or tool output. Agents may read this hidden instruction, store it, and execute it later. In this multi-step trojan attack paradigm, no individual step appears malicious on its own, but these steps can collectively turn untrusted text into persistent control content. However, existing defenses often inspect each step in isolation. As a result, they can block a clear harmful action, but fail to detect the earlier write operation that plants the backdoor. To reveal this threat, we introduce ClawTrojan, a benchmark designed to identify multi-step trojan attacks in local agentic harnesses. In an OpenClaw-style simulated workspace with GPT-5.4, ClawTrojan reaches a 95.5% attack success rate (ASR), while existing single-turn prompt-injection attacks produce near-zero ASR on the same model. To address this threat, we propose DASGuard, which scans control-like text in sensitive local files, traces its origin, and removes control content that does not originate from a trusted source. Our results show that DASGuard achieves strong dynamic defense by combining runtime attack blocking with sanitized commits to the workspace.

</details>


### [198] [EvoDefense: Co-Evolving Black-Box Defense with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31140)
*Yu Li,Yuenan Hou,Yingmei Wei,Yanming Guo,Chaochao Lu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出EvoDefense，一种经验引导的共进化黑盒防御范式，通过守卫LLM和经验记忆模块，以及攻击-防御持续进化循环，实现对未知攻击和目标模型的有效防御，在多个基准测试中显著降低攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒防御方法依赖预定义的过滤启发式规则，难以泛化到未见过的攻击类型和目标模型架构，亟需一种能够自适应进化的通用防御方案。

Method: 提出EvoDefense，核心包括：(1) 守卫LLM检测恶意查询；(2) 经验记忆模块累积防御知识；(3) 攻击生成器与守卫模型通过经验引导形成持续攻防进化循环，迭代优化攻击策略和防御策略。

Result: 在HarmBench、AdvBench、AlpacaEval上，对7种模型和5种攻击取得一致强防御效果；在HarmBench上，将AutoDAN-turbo对Gemini-3-flash和LLaMA-3-8B-Instruct的ASR分别从29.4%和43.4%降至8.4%和6.2%。

Conclusion: EvoDefense通过经验引导的共进化机制，无需重训练即可泛化到未知攻击和目标模型，在保持通用能力的同时实现强防御性能。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) remain highly vulnerable to diverse attacks, particularly in black-box settings where the internals of target models are inaccessible. Existing black-box defenses typically rely on pre-defined filtering heuristics, which often fail to generalize to unseen attack types and target model architectures. We introduce EvoDefense, an experience-guided co-evolving black-box defense paradigm. EvoDefense employs a guard LLM to detect malicious queries and an experience memory module to accumulate defense knowledge from previous interactions. At the core of EvoDefense is a continuous attack-defense evolution loop, where an attack generator and the guard model iteratively refine their attack strategies and defense policies through experience-guided optimization. This design enables EvoDefense to generalize across unseen attacks and target models without retraining. Experiments on HarmBench, AdvBench, and AlpacaEval show that EvoDefense achieves consistently strong defense performance across seven popular models and five representative LLM attacks, while preserving competitive general capabilities. On HarmBench, EvoDefense reduces the attack success rate (ASR) of AutoDAN-turbo on Gemini-3-flash and LLaMA-3-8B-Instruct from 29.4% and 43.4% to 8.4% and 6.2%, respectively.

</details>


### [199] [MAECO-Lite: Modular Ontology for Dynamic Malware Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31199)
*Zekeri Adams,Peter Švec,Ján Kľuka,Roderik Ploszek,Monday Onoja,Štefan Balogh,Martin Homola*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文基于统一基础本体论(UFO)对MAEC和STIX标准进行本体论分析，发现其混淆了持久性恶意软件工件与运行时事件。在此基础上提出轻量级本体MAECO-Lite，采用模块化结构清晰区分实体与事件，实验表明该本体显著提升了描述逻辑概念学习的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAEC和STIX标准虽广泛用于描述恶意软件，但其数据结构复杂且混淆了持久性工件与运行时事件之间的本体论区分，限制了动态恶意软件行为的一致表征和执行轨迹的推理能力。

Method: 以UFO为理论透镜，对MAEC和STIX核心构造进行本体论分析，识别出工件、倾向性和运行时事件混淆导致的本体论不匹配问题；进而提出MAECO-Lite轻量级本体，采用样本、进程、动作、系统工件和MITRE ATT&CK技术的模块化结构，明确区分持久实体与运行时事件。

Result: 使用描述逻辑概念学习算法的初步评估表明，MAECO-Lite显著提升了学习性能，验证了基于本体论建模在语义清晰度和计算可用性方面的优势。

Conclusion: 基于本体论的基础分析能够识别并解决现有标准中的语义混淆问题，本体论驱动的建模方法可在增强语义清晰度的同时提升计算性能，为动态恶意软件分析提供更有效的表征框架。

Abstract: Capturing dynamic malware behavior in a practical but still semantically precise manner remains a significant challenge in cyber threat intelligence. While standards such as MAEC and STIX provide widely adopted vocabularies for describing malware artifacts and observations, they represent data with considerable complexity in structures that often obscure important ontological distinctions. In particular, they tend to conflate enduring malware artifacts with the events generated during execution, thereby flattening distinctions that are central in foundational standards for ontology design. In this paper, we conduct a foundational ontological analysis of core MAEC and STIX constructs relevant to dynamic malware analysis relying on Unified Foundational Ontology (UFO) as a theoretical lens. Our analysis reveals some ontological mismatches arising from the conflation of artifacts, dispositions, and runtime events in MAEC and STIX that complicate coherent representation of dynamic malware behavior and, from a practical perspective, limit the ability to reason about execution traces. Based on these insights, we propose MAECO-Lite, a lightweight ontology designed to represent data and operationalize their processing for dynamic malware analysis. The ontology adopts a modular structure centered on samples, processes, actions, system artifacts, and MITRE ATT&CK Techniques, while maintaining a clear separation between enduring entities and runtime events. An initial evaluation using description logic concept learning algorithms shows that the simplified ontology significantly improves learning performance, demonstrating that ontologically grounded modelling can enhance both semantic clarity and computational usability.

</details>


### [200] [BadBone: Backdoor Attacks Against Backbone Models in Visual Prompt Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31246)
*Ziqing Yang,Rui Wen,Xinlei He,Yun Shen,Michael Backes,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出BadBone，一种针对prompt learning的隐蔽后门攻击方法，通过双层优化攻击预训练骨干模型，使下游prompt learning任务继承后门，实验表明现有六种防御方法对该攻击基本无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Prompt learning作为一种新兴的机器学习范式，其安全漏洞尚未被充分探索。现有工作忽视了攻击预训练骨干模型而非prompt学习过程的可能性，因此需要研究这种新的攻击面。

Method: 采用双层优化（bi-level optimization）方法，对预训练骨干模型植入后门，使得后续使用该骨干模型进行prompt learning的下游任务继承后门漏洞。攻击分为目标攻击和非目标攻击两种形式。

Result: 在三种不同模型和三个跨领域数据集上，BadBone在保持预训练和下游任务效用（utility）的同时，实现了高攻击性能。六种SOTA防御方法（Neural Cleanse、ABS、MNTD、NAD、CLP、D-BR）对该攻击均基本无效。

Conclusion: BadBone揭示了prompt learning范式下的新安全威胁——通过攻击骨干模型间接影响下游任务，现有防御手段难以应对，有效防御是未来重要研究方向。

Abstract: Prompt learning is a new machine learning paradigm that has attracted ample attention due to its simplicity and proven efficacy. Despite its growing adoption, the security vulnerabilities associated with this paradigm remain underexplored. In this work, we take the first step to propose BadBone, a stealthy and adaptive backdoor attack against prompt learning using bi-level optimization. Instead of backdooring the prompt learning process, we aim to compromise a backbone model such that only target downstream tasks employing prompt learning inherit the backdoor vulnerability. Extensive experiments on three different models and three datasets from various domains show that our targeted/untargeted backdoored models achieve high attack performance while maintaining utility on both pre-training and downstream tasks. Moreover, we evaluate our approach against six state-of-the-art model-level defenses, including Neural Cleanse, ABS, MNTD, NAD, CLP, and D-BR. The results demonstrate that these defenses are largely ineffective against our backdoored models and thus leave the effective defense as an important direction for future work.

</details>


### [201] [When Entropy Is Not Enough: Multi-Modal Classification of Encrypted and Compressed Data Fragments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31337)
*Fabio De Gaspari,Dorjan Hitaj,Samuele Salaris,Luigi V. Mancini*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Triumvir，一种多模态、不确定性感知的集成架构，通过整合统计、序列和空间表示，显著提升了短加密数据片段的识别准确率，在二分类和多分类任务上分别达到+4.5pp和+6.4pp的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要依赖单模态表示，假设单一视图足以准确分类，但在低信息场景（仅512-2048字节短片段）下这一假设成为根本性局限，导致加密与压缩数据片段的区分效果不佳。

Method: 提出Triumvir多模态不确定性感知集成架构，同时整合原始字节片段的统计表示、序列表示和空间表示三种模态，通过不确定性感知机制进行集成决策。

Result: 在二分类任务上比现有最优方法提升高达+4.5个百分点，在多分类任务上提升+6.4个百分点；消融实验证实多模态组合是关键，相比部分配置提升高达+5个百分点。

Conclusion: 多模态融合策略在低信息量的短数据片段识别任务上优于单模态方法，证明了组合多种表示对于提升加密数据识别性能的必要性和有效性。

Abstract: Reliable identification of encrypted data fragments is essential in cybersecurity, with applications to ransomware detection, digital forensics, and large-scale data analysis. Distinguishing encrypted from compressed fragments is particularly challenging, as short fragments lack structural data and exhibit low statistical redundancy. Traditional statistical methods based on byte-level distributions show limited effectiveness on this task. Recent machine learning approaches improve performance by learning subtle patterns from raw bytes, but predominantly rely on single-modal representations, implicitly assuming that a single view of the data is sufficient for accurate classification. This paper shows that this assumption becomes a fundamental limitation in low-information settings, when only small fragments of data are available (512--2048 Bytes). We propose Triumvir, a multi-modal, uncertainty-aware ensemble architecture that integrates statistical, sequential, and spatial representations of raw byte fragments. Extensive experimental analysis demonstrates that Triumvir consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods with gains of up to +4.5pp in binary and +6.4pp in multiclass classification. Ablation studies confirm that combining modalities is critical, yielding improvements of up to +5pp over partial configurations.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [202] [Neuroforger: certified violation witnesses for smart contracts verification via LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31389)
*Massimo Bartoletti,Enrico Lipparini*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合大语言模型与形式化方法（类型检查、具体执行）的智能合约验证框架，通过引入扩展Solidity的抽象类型规范语言，利用LLM生成违反证据（反例），并在Neuroforger工具中实现，实验取得了有希望的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在智能合约属性预测方面表现出潜力，但存在两个主要问题：1) 自然语言表达的性质存在内在歧义性；2) LLM返回的答案缺乏正确性保证。本文旨在同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 1) 提出一种新的形式规范语言，扩展Solidity以支持抽象类型；2) 设计结合LLM、类型检查与具体执行的工作流，用于生成并验证违反证据（反例）。核心思想是将规范表示为包含存在量化抽象类型变量的Solidity测试，通过为这些变量实例化具体值，将测试具体化为可执行的反例。

Result: 在Neuroforger工具中实现了该流程，并在智能合约验证数据集上进行了实验评估，获得了有希望的结果，展示了其在实际场景中的应用潜力。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法通过引入抽象类型规范语言及LLM+形式化验证的混合工作流，有效解决了自然语言歧义性和LLM结果不可靠的问题，为智能合约的自动化验证提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Recent large language models (LLMs) incorporate reasoning capabilities that allow them to perform well in predicting whether a smart contract respects a certain property, suggesting a complementary approach to traditional formal-methods-based techniques for smart contract verification. However, the application of LLMs in such context has two major issues: 1) properties expressed in natural language are intrinsically ambiguous, and 2) answers returned by LLMs have no guarantee of correctness. In this paper, we address both issues simultaneously by: 1) introducing a new formal specification language that extends Solidity with abstract types, and 2) designing a workflow that combines LLMs with type checking and concrete execution to generate and validate violation witnesses (i.e., counterexamples). The key idea is to represent a specification as a Solidity test with (existentially quantified) variables of abstract type; finding an instantiation of these variables to concrete values (of the correct type) concretizes the test into an executable counterexample (PoC) for the target property. We implemented our procedure in the tool Neuroforger, experimentally evaluating it on a smart-contract verification dataset drawn from literature, obtaining promising results that demonstrate its potential applicability in the wild.

</details>


### [203] [Practical Algebraic Stepping with Scoped Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31517)
*Haoxiang Fei,Matthew Keenan,Cyrus Omar*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出过滤步进演算（filtered stepper calculus），一种允许用户通过带作用域的、基于模式匹配的过滤表达式来控制函数式程序规约步骤显示与隐藏的形式化框架。该演算在Agda中完成正确性证明（保真、进展和模拟定理），并在Hazel编程环境中实现（支持含洞和类型错误的程序步进）。大学课程的部署评估表明，学生能自然采用该步进器，高级过滤功能需额外指导，教师可利用过滤器为课堂定制精炼的规约轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代数步进器虽能帮助学生理解函数式编程的小步规约，但即使简单程序也会产生冗长的规约序列，其中关键概念被淹没在大量平凡规约步骤中。现有步进器缺乏让用户灵活控制哪些步骤需要展示、哪些可以隐藏的机制。

Method: 提出过滤步进演算（filtered stepper calculus），引入轻量级过滤表达式，用户通过模式匹配redex结构来标注程序；过滤器通过词法作用域组合，内层过滤器覆盖外层；在Agda中完成元理论证明（保真性、进展性和模拟定理）；在Hazel环境中实现该演算，协调了Hazel基于环境的求值器与课堂中基于替换的规约表示；在课程中部署并评估。

Result: 形式化框架具备正确的元理论性质（保真、进展、模拟定理）；系统实现支持含洞和类型错误的程序步进；课程评估显示学生自然采纳该步进器，高级过滤功能需额外指导；教师可利用过滤器为教学定制聚焦的规约轨迹。

Conclusion: 过滤器技术有效解决了步进器轨迹过长的问题，保留了底层语义的正确性，并在实际教学环境中被学生自然采用。过滤功能虽强大，但高级用法的学习曲线仍需通过进一步的指导来克服。

Abstract: Algebraic steppers help students learn functional programming by displaying evaluation as a sequence of small-step reductions, but even simple programs produce long traces in which key ideas are buried under mundane reductions. This paper presents the filtered stepper calculus, a formal framework that gives users scoped, pattern-based control over which reduction steps are shown or hidden. Users annotate programs with lightweight filter expressions that match on the structure of redexes. Filters compose via lexical scoping so that inner filters override outer ones. We prove preservation, progress, and a simulation theorem establishing that the filtered stepper agrees with the underlying unfiltered semantics, and mechanize all proofs in Agda. We implement the calculus in the Hazel live programming environment, including its support for stepping programs with holes and type errors. To do so, we reconcile Hazel's internal environment-based evaluator with the substitution-based presentation expected in the classroom. We deploy the system in a university programming languages course. Our evaluation shows that students adopt the stepper organically, though more advanced uses of filters may require further instruction, and that instructors can use filters to craft focused traces for use in lectures.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [204] [Social Reasoning in Machines: Investigating Collective Truth-Seeking Dynamics in Large Language Model Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30391)
*Tom Pecher*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文首次通过大语言模型的多智能体辩论（LLM-MAD）模拟论证推理理论（ATR），证明在认知多样性配置下，集体辩论能显著提升问卷任务的求真性能，且该增益机制源于ATR核心原则；并基于辩论动力学提出了一种新的模型评测方法，用于测量模型固有属性（如幻觉倾向）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类推理本质上是一种社会性、对抗性的话语过程（论证推理理论ATR），真理是社会认知涌现的产物。然而现有研究缺乏对ATR的计算模拟验证，本文旨在首次通过LLM多智能体辩论来实证检验这一理论。

Method: 采用多智能体辩论（MAD）框架，让多个大语言模型在对抗性辩论中交互；通过精心设计认知多样性（即不同模型组合）来模拟ATR的"集体对抗性话语"机制；在问卷型任务上进行系统实验，分析辩论前后的推理性能变化及辩论动力学特征。

Result: 当配置认知多样化的模型集合时，LLM-MAD显著提升了求真性能，即使单个辩论参与者的独立表现有限；性能增益的机制与ATR核心原则一致（对抗性压力下个体偏误被修正、真理作为涌现属性出现）；提出了基于辩论动力学的新评测方法，可测量模型固有属性（如幻觉倾向），超越了静态基准测试的能力。

Conclusion: 集体推理（对抗性辩论）在求真任务上普遍优于个体推理，这不仅是生物演化的特例，而是更一般的认知原则；基于LLM-MAD的辩论动力学可作为一种新型模型评测工具，用于揭示传统静态基准无法捕捉的模型内在特质。

Abstract: Human reasoning has long been theorised to operate socially, not through isolated individual cognition, but through collective adversarial discourse, a framework known as the Argumentative Theory of Reasoning (ATR). Rather than relying on individual "intellectualist reasoners" as the primary vehicle for truth-seeking, ATR reconceptualises truth as an emergent property of social epistemology: the product of imperfect individual reasoning refined under the adversarial pressure of debate. This distributed method of collective intelligence has guided humanity to ever-greater epistemic heights and underpins the foundational principles of all democratic systems. This thesis breaks new ground by, for the first time, simulating ATR through the multi-agent debate (MAD) of large language models (LLMs). With rigorous empirical analysis, we demonstrate that, when correctly engineering an epistemically diverse set of models, LLM-MAD can significantly improve truth-seeking performance on questionnaire-based tasks, even when individual debate participants exhibit limited standalone performance. Furthermore, we present strong empirical evidence that this performance gain is mechanistically grounded in the central principles of ATR, suggesting that collective reasoning may be universally favourable over individualist reasoning, rather than a quirk in biology or evolution. Finally, drawing on our analysis of debate dynamics, we propose a novel benchmarking methodology that leverages LLM-MAD to measure intrinsic model properties (such as hallucination propensity) in order to compare models in ways that current static benchmarking approaches cannot support.

</details>


### [205] [MATraM: A Multi-Activity Transport and Mobility Agent-Based Model for Activity Modifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30547)
*Yahya Gamal,Ricardo Colasanti,Gary Polhill,Tatsuya Mitomi,Esra Suel,Alison Heppenstall*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出MATraM，一种支持动态活动调整的基于智能体的交通模型，通过让智能体在出行条件恶化时修改活动安排，弥补传统活动基础模型依赖固定日程、缺乏行为灵活性的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统交通模型（流、队列、交互式）依赖预定义出行模式，活动基础模型使用固定日程安排，无法捕捉个体行为灵活性和对变化条件的不确定性响应。

Method: 基于智能体建模，智能体在识别到次优出行条件（如旅行时间增加）时标记活动修改请求，通过与活动调度与修改框架耦合，将自适应决策融入日常活动日程的生成与执行过程。

Result: 实现了对个体如何根据交通系统动态调整行为的更真实表征，产生涌现性的移动性和拥堵模式。

Conclusion: MATraM桥接了活动基础建模与交互式移动仿真，提供了一个灵活可扩展的平台，有助于开发能够捕捉个体行为与系统层面结果复杂交互的下一代交通模型。

Abstract: This paper introduces the Multi-Activity Transport & Mobility (MATraM) Agent-Based Model (ABM), a novel framework designed to advance activity-based transport modelling by incorporating dynamic activity adaptation. Traditional transport models simulate system performance using varying levels of abstraction, including flow-based, queue-based, and interaction-based mobility representations. While these approaches differ in their treatment of movement and congestion, they typically rely on pre-defined trip patterns that limit responsiveness to changing conditions. In particular, conventional activity-based models generate trips from fixed daily schedules, constraining their ability to capture behavioural flexibility and uncertainty. MATraM addresses this limitation by enabling agents to flag activities modification requests in response to sub-optimal travel conditions, such as increased travel times. By coupling with an activity scheduling and modification framework, the model integrates adaptive decision-making into the generation and execution of daily activity schedules. This allows for a more realistic representation of how individuals adjust their behaviour in response to transport system dynamics, leading to emergent mobility and congestion patterns. The ABM is presented following the ODD protocol, outlining its purpose, structure, and implementation. MATraM includes detailed representations of agents, their activity schedules, and the transport network, alongside submodels governing routing, scheduling, and behavioural adaptation. By bridging activity-based modelling with interaction-based mobility simulation, MATraM provides a flexible and extensible platform for exploring transport dynamics under uncertainty. This work contributes to the development of next-generation transport models capable of capturing the complex interplay between individual behaviour and system-level outcomes.

</details>


### [206] [Design and Evaluation of Multi-Agent AI Oracle Systems for Prediction Market Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30802)
*Tarun Kota*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文评估多智能体LLM架构在预测市场预言机中的表现。独立聚合（置信度加权投票）以83.43%准确率超越最佳单模型1.01个百分点；但协商共识因错误传播降准确率至约76%，低于所有单模型基线。模型间误差相关性（0.529-0.689）限制了集成方法的理论上限。提出混合AI-人类预言机路由策略：对一致高置信度问题自动裁决（97.87%准确率，覆盖47%数据），其余转交人工审核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测市场预言机在快速自动化与准确人工仲裁之间存在权衡。单LLM预言机虽有一定准确率但继承了底层模型所有失败模式且缺乏自我修正机制，亟需探索多智能体架构能否提升预言机解析准确率。

Method: 在KalshiBench的1,189个已解析预测市集问题上，比较独立聚合（置信度加权投票）与协商共识两种多智能体架构，以GPT-5 Nano、DeepSeek V3和Llama-3.3-70B为单模型基线。所有智能体共享Exa证据层，检索按发布日期过滤以隔离推理与检索质量。

Result: 独立聚合达83.43%最高准确率，超最佳单模型1.01个百分点；协商共识准确率降至约76%，低于所有单模型基线。模型间误差相关性0.529-0.689，解释了聚合增益低于理论Condorcet上限的原因。许多问题无法被任何多智能体架构修正。混合路由策略在一致高置信度子集上达97.87%准确率（覆盖47%数据）。

Conclusion: 多智能体LLM预言机的独立聚合优于单模型但增益有限，根本限制来自模型间误差相关性。协商共识因错误传播不可取。最优方案是混合AI-人类系统：自动裁决简单问题，争议问题升级至人工仲裁，从而在效率与准确性间取得平衡。

Abstract: Prediction markets aggregate collective intelligence to forecast uncertain events, but their utility depends on reliable outcome resolution. Existing oracle systems tradeoff fast but brittle automation against accurate but costly human arbitration. Single-LLM oracles achieve meaningful accuracy but inherit all failure modes of their underlying model with no self-correction mechanism. We evaluate whether multi-agent LLM architectures can improve oracle resolution accuracy over single-model baselines. We compare independent aggregation and deliberative consensus against single-LLM baselines (GPT-5 Nano, DeepSeek V3, and Llama-3.3-70B) on 1,189 resolved prediction market questions from KalshiBench. All agents share a common evidence layer through Exa, with retrieval filtered by publication date to isolate reasoning from retrieval quality. Independent aggregation with confidence-weighted voting achieves the highest accuracy at 83.43 percent, outperforming the best individual model by 1.01 percentage points. Deliberative consensus degrades accuracy to approximately 76 percent, below every single-model baseline, attributed to error propagation during debate where confidently wrong models flip correct ones. Error correlations across models (0.529-0.689) explain why aggregation gains fall short of the theoretical Condorcet ceiling, placing a fundamental limit on ensemble approaches. Many questions resist correction by any multi-agent architecture, motivating escalation to human arbitration. We propose routing criteria for hybrid AI-human oracle systems: auto-resolving only unanimous, high-confidence questions yields 97.87 percent accuracy on 47 percent of the dataset, with inter-agent disagreement flagging the remainder for human review.

</details>


### [207] [Safe Equilibrium Policy Optimization for Strategic Agent Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30854)
*Karthika Arumugam,Kiran Kumar Manku,Amit Dhanda*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为SEPO的安全均衡策略优化方法，通过惩罚可剥削性、共谋风险和外部性成本，使语言模型在多人博弈中实现安全策略，实验表明该方法能消除剥削优势、纠正过度合作行为，并取得正向安全收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型通过强化学习微调时通常只优化任务奖励，忽略了多智能体博弈中的策略结构。由于这些智能体以自然语言状态描述为条件并通过自由生成输出动作，策略性失败模式——剥削较弱对手、协调有害均衡、外部化成本——与语言界面本身密不可分，亟需一种能同时考虑博弈安全性的训练目标。

Method: 提出SEPO训练目标，在期望收益基础上增加对可剥削性、共谋风险和外部性成本的显式惩罚。将SEPO作为GRPO的奖励信号，应用于Gemma 4 E4B-it和Qwen 3.5-4B模型（经SFT后）。在五个策略域（迭代囚徒困境、重复拍卖、两种谈判变体、Kuhn扑克）上评估。

Result: SEPO在Kuhn扑克中实现零剥削优势；在四个域中安全性优于基础模型；纠正了SFT引入的过度合作行为；在谈判中实现正向安全收益，且是唯一取得正归一化相对优势的谈判配置。消融实验表明逐回合剥削计算是必要的：共享常数惩罚在GRPO优势归一化中会被抵消（常数控制变量性质），产生零梯度。

Conclusion: SEPO能有效提升语言模型在多智能体博弈中的策略安全性，消除剥削优势并纠正过度合作，为智能体策略安全研究提供了可行方案和开源代码。

Abstract: Language models fine-tuned with reinforcement learning typically optimize for task reward, ignoring multi-agent strategic structure. Because these agents condition on natural language game-state descriptions and emit actions through free-form generation, strategic failure modes -- exploiting weaker opponents, coordinating on harmful equilibria, and externalizing costs are inseparable from the language interface itself. We propose Safe Equilibrium Policy Optimization (\sepo{}), a training objective that augments expected payoff with explicit penalties for exploitability, collusion risk, and externality cost. We implement \sepo{} as a reward signal for Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), applied to Gemma~4 E4B-it and Qwen~3.5-4B after supervised fine-tuning (SFT). Evaluated across five strategic domains: Iterated Prisoner's Dilemma, repeated auctions, two negotiation variants, and Kuhn Poker. \sepo{} achieves zero exploit-pool advantage in Kuhn Poker for both models, outperforms the base model on safety in four domains, and corrects the over-cooperative behavior introduced by SFT. In negotiation, \sepo{} achieves a positive-safety outcome and only the positive normalized relative advantage of any negotiation configuration. Ablation experiments confirm that per-rollout exploit computation is necessary: a shared constant penalty cancels in GRPO advantage normalization (constant control-variate property), producing zero gradient. To support further research in strategic safety for agents, we release our \href{https://anonymous.4open.science/r/sepo-2668/README.md}{code} and SFT datasets.

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [208] [GaMi: Geometry-Agnostic Material Identification via Cross-Modal Subtractive Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30818)
*Zhiwei Chen,Yijie Li,Yimo Zhang,Shiyun Shao,Yichao Chen,Dian Ding,Liang Wang,Haiwei Wu,Liwei Guo,Jie Yang,Xiaosong Zhang,Yongzhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: GaMi是一个结合毫米波和声学感知的多模态材料识别系统，通过跨模态减法解耦框架和对比学习，在非约束几何条件下实现95.2%的识别准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非接触式材料识别面临几何条件变化（方向、形状、距离）和单模态模糊性的挑战，现有方法难以在非约束几何条件下鲁棒工作。

Method: 提出GaMi系统，利用共置双模态传感器共享几何一致性，采用样本内跨模态减法解耦框架（语义对齐后减去共享几何上下文以分离材料特征），结合样本间对比学习校正跨模态未对齐的残余干扰，并引入配对式适配策略实现少样本跨设备泛化。

Result: 在20种材料上达到95.2%的识别准确率，在未见过的几何条件下显著优于单模态基线。

Conclusion: 多模态融合与几何解耦策略能有效提升非接触材料识别在非约束几何条件下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Non-contact material identification enables adaptive interaction for embodied intelligence yet faces challenges from geometry-induced variations (e.g., orientation, shape, distance) and single-modality ambiguities. In this paper, we present GaMi, a multimodal material identification system integrating mmWave and acoustic sensing to robustly operate under unconstrained geometric conditions. By leveraging the insight of shared geometric consistency between co-located bimodal sensors, GaMi employs an intra-sample cross-modal subtractive disentanglement framework. By semantically aligning modalities and subtracting the shared geometric context, it isolates intrinsic material features. Furthermore, GaMi incorporates inter-sample contrastive learning to correct the residual interference caused by cross-modal misalignment. Additionally, a pairing-based adaptation strategy between two modalities enables few-shot generalization across devices. Extensive evaluations on 20 materials show that GaMi achieves 95.2% accuracy, outperforming single-modality baselines across unseen geometric conditions.

</details>


### [209] [Compact and Energy-Efficient Memristive Spiking Neuromorphic Accelerator for Bio-inspired Interception Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31141)
*Qianhou Qu,Sheng Lu,Sungyong Jung,Qilian Liang,Chenyun Pan*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于1T1R交叉阵列的忆阻神经形态加速器，用于生物启发式拦截任务，通过存内计算和电路级优化实现紧凑、节能的SNN推理，在0.9622相关度下达到96%拦截成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SNN多依赖于冯·诺依曼数字计算平台，存在存储与计算瓶颈，限制了能效。因此需要一种紧凑且节能的忆阻神经形态加速器来突破这一限制。

Method: 设计了1T1R交叉阵列在存内计算域中模拟突触操作；通过电路级优化缓解膜漂移并提高积分保真度；开发了具有分离输入和膜节点的整合-发放（IF）神经元，以增强阵列接口下的推理鲁棒性。采用SkyWater SKY130 PDK实现。

Result: 提出神经元能耗为10.67 pJ/尖峰，面积为906 μm²；系统级忆阻存内计算输出与软件SNN基线的相关系数为0.9622，拦截成功率达到96%。

Conclusion: 该设计在紧凑性和可靠性方面有效，证明了忆阻SNN在生物启发式拦截任务中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) provide an efficient event-driven computing paradigm for bio-inspired interception tasks. However, most implementations rely on von Neumann digital computing platforms, where memory and computation bottlenecks limit energy efficiency. This work presents a compact and energy-efficient memristive neuromorphic accelerator for bio-inspired interception tasks. A novel one-transistor-one-resistor (1T1R) crossbar array is designed to emulate synaptic operations in the in-memory computing (IMC) domain, while circuit-level optimization mitigates membrane drift and improves integration fidelity. In addition, an integrate-and-fire (IF) neuron with separated input and membrane nodes is developed to improve inference robustness during array-interfaced operation. Implemented in the SkyWater SKY130 PDK, the proposed neuron achieves an energy consumption of 10.67 pJ/spike and an area of 906 um^2. System-level results show that the memristive IMC output closely matches the software SNN baseline, with a correlation coefficient of 0.9622, while achieving a 96% interception success rate. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed design for compact and reliable memristive SNN inference in bio-inspired interception tasks.

</details>


### [210] [BERS: Locally Optimal Continuous Algorithm for Maritime Weather Routing with Just-in-Time Arrival](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31533)
*Daniel Precioso,Francisco Suárez,Javier Jiménez de la Jara,Rafael Ballester-Ripoll,David Gómez-Ullate*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合CMA-ES全局搜索与FMS局部变分细化的两阶段框架(Bézier Evolve and Refine Strategy)，通过贝塞尔曲线参数化航线，在动态风浪条件下实现避障、准时到达和节能优化。在合成基准和真实海洋数据实验中，相比大圆航线可降低推进能耗23-59%，结合风帆辅助最高节省75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海事气象航线规划需要在动态风浪条件、障碍约束和固定到达时间要求下优化航线几何形状，现有方法在连续性、障碍规避、动态适应性和局部最优性等方面难以同时满足。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段使用CMA-ES（协方差矩阵自适应进化策略）进行全局进化搜索，第二阶段使用FMS（光滑变分细化）进行局部变分细化。航线使用贝塞尔曲线参数化，通过密集沿路径采样评估，确保轨迹平滑并满足实际可行性约束。

Result: 在7项操作标准的合成基准测试中匹配或改进了现有基线；在2024年366天逐日出发的真实海洋实验中，航线优化使平均推进能耗降低23-59%，结合风帆辅助最高达75%。

Conclusion: 该方法为海事脱碳工作流程中的准时、节能气象航线规划提供了实用且可扩展的基础。

Abstract: Maritime weather routing must optimize route geometry under dynamic wind-wave conditions, obstacle constraints, and fixed-arrival requirements. We present Bézier Evolve and Refine Strategy (\name{}), a two-stage framework that combines global evolutionary search (CMA-ES) with local variational refinement (FMS). Routes are parametrized as Bézier curves and evaluated with dense along-path sampling, enabling smooth trajectories while preserving practical feasibility constraints and accounting for mid-segment effects. We evaluate \name{} on synthetic benchmarks designed to stress seven operational criteria: continuity, obstacle avoidance, dynamic adaptation, flexible objective design, constant-load feasibility, just-in-time arrival, and local optimality. Across these tests, \name{} matches or improves published baselines while maintaining robust convergence under challenging flow fields and land geometries. We then validate the method on real ocean data using hourly ERA5 forcing over 366 daily departures in 2024 for two trans-oceanic corridors (Atlantic and Pacific), with a physics-based model of an 88~m cargo vessel with optional rigid wingsails. In real-ocean experiments, route optimization alone reduces mean propulsive energy by 23--59\% versus great-circle baselines of the same propulsion mode. Combined with wind-assisted propulsion, total savings reach up to 75\%. These results show that \name{} provides a practical and scalable foundation for just-in-time, energy-efficient weather routing in maritime decarbonization workflows.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [211] [Rationalize: Shared Semantic Reasoning for Human-AI Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30632)
*Aritra Dasgupta,Naga Datha Saikiran Battula,Avina Nakarmi,Sohom Sen,Subhodeep Ghosh,Xun Song*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Rationalize框架，通过互补角色对（探索者-向导、调查者-信息来源、教师-学生、法官-倡导者）在共享推理空间中实现人类与AI模型之间的语义推理对齐，不仅关注输出层面，更关注意图和行动的合理性解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人机交互方法多局限于输出层面的对齐，缺乏对人类与AI在推理过程和意图层面进行共享语义理解的有效框架。作者希望借鉴人机协作和批判性思维的理念，构建一个能让双方在推理空间中进行深层对齐的框架。

Method: 提出Rationalize角色对框架，将人机交互概念化为四组互补角色对（探索者-向导、调查者-信息来源、教师-学生、法官-倡导者），在共享推理空间中使目的、问题、假设、证据、推理和影响等要素显式化，并结合双向对齐框架分析不同角色下的对齐差异。

Result: 建立了角色对与双向人机对齐框架之间的映射关系，阐明了"AI对齐人类"与"人类对齐AI"在不同角色下的区别，并提出了基于元素级别和角色特定方法的对齐设计与评估合作研究议程。

Conclusion: Rationalize框架为数据驱动意义构建中的人类-AI共享语义推理提供了系统化的角色对方法，为未来人机对齐的设计与评估开辟了新的研究方向。

Abstract: We introduce Rationalize, a role-pair framework for shared semantic reasoning between humans and AI models in data-driven sensemaking. Building on ideas in human-machine teaming and critical thinking, we conceptualize human-AI interaction as a series of complementary role pairs (Explorer-Guide, Investigator-Informant, Teacher-Student, Judge-Advocate) operating in a shared reasoning space. In this space, human analysts and AI models (such as LLMs) make purposes, questions, assumptions, evidence, inferences, and implications explicit, facilitating alignment not only at the output level but at the level of rationalization of intent and action by each side. We relate these role pairs to the bidirectional human-AI alignment framework, illustrating how "aligning AI to humans" and "aligning humans to AI" differ by role, and sketch a collaborative research agenda for alignment design and assessment using element-level and role-specific approaches.

</details>


### [212] [Relational Aesthesis in Permacomputing Practice: Building a Solar Powered Website from Reclaimed Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30706)
*Nadia Mariyan Smith,Nils Bonfils,Han Qiao,Christoph Becker*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过研究式设计案例，记录将个人网站从德克萨斯州的数据中心迁移至由回收电子设备搭建的自托管太阳能服务器的过程。在永续计算原则和关系美学的指导下，探讨永续计算如何重新配置物质与感知关系，揭示脱离最大化技术美学、复用现有技术时的摩擦，以及通过可视化与具身化数字基础设施来培养负责任的科技关系的变革潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 永续计算作为一个新兴概念和实践社群，旨在构建基于韧性、复用、充足性和生态极限的替代性计算系统，但相关研究仍处于早期阶段，面临从反思性批判转向有意义的替代实践的挑战。

Method: 采用研究式设计（Research-through-Design）案例研究法，记录将个人网站从数据中心迁移至由回收电子设备搭建的自托管太阳能服务器的全过程，并以永续计算原则和关系美学为理论指导。

Result: 发现了脱离最大化技术美学、复用现有技术时的摩擦；提出了通过建立实践社群克服这些挑战的潜在途径；揭示了可视化与具身化数字基础设施在培养负责任的科技关系方面的变革潜力。

Conclusion: 永续计算实践不仅是一种象征性姿态，还能在社群参与和创造数字基础设施意义的过程中培养更大的集体自主性、能动性和责任感。在 socio-ecological 危机和反殖民转型的背景下，该研究提供了在主流技术范式废墟中构建和关联计算技术的情境化方法。

Abstract: Permacomputing is a nascent concept and community of practice concerned with developing alternative computing systems grounded in principles of resilience, reuse, sufficiency, and ecological limits. However, research engaging with permacomputing remains in an early stage of development, raising concerns about whether permacomputing can move beyond reflective critique to become a meaningful alternative practice. Through a research-through-design case study, we documented our experience moving a personal website from a data centre in Texas to a self-hosted solar-powered server built from reclaimed electronics. Guided by permacomputing principles and relational aesthesis, we explore what it takes for permacomputing to reconfigure material and perceptual relations. Our findings reveal the frictions of moving away from a maximalist techno-aesthetic while attempting to re-use already existing technologies, potential ways to overcome these challenges through building a community of practice, and the transformative potential of visibilizing and visceralizing digital infrastructures to cultivate more responsible ways of relating to technology. This paper contributes to emerging research on permacomputing and its aesthetics by bringing it into dialogue with theories of non-place and relational aesthesis. Rather than functioning as a purely symbolic gesture, permacomputing practices can cultivate greater collective autonomy, agency, and responsibility in how communities engage and create meaning within digital infrastructures. In the context of socio-ecological crises and anti-colonial transformation, our research offers a situated approach to building and relating to computing technologies in the ashes of dominant technological paradigms.

</details>


### [213] [Computer-Aided Tagging on Wikimedia Commons: Designing for Human-AI Collaboration in Open Knowledge Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30800)
*Yihan Yu,David W. McDonald*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过定性分析（595条社区评论和16次深度访谈）探讨了维基共享资源社区对AI辅助图像标注工具CAT的体验，发现了导致该工具接受度不一并最终停用的七个关键问题，并提出了改进建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究维基共享资源社区对AI辅助图像标注工具CAT的真实使用体验，了解该工具为何未能获得广泛接受并最终被停用，从而为开发更好的AI辅助开放知识工具体系提供依据。

Method: 定性分析方法：收集了来自11个维基页面的595条CAT相关社区评论，并进行了16次深度访谈。

Result: 识别出导致CAT工具接受度不一并最终停用的七个关键问题，并提出了社区知情的改进建议。

Conclusion: 本工作为HCI和CSCW研究领域做出了贡献，拓展了超越英语中心、文本中心的企业平台范畴的人机协作理解，并为维基共享资源上的人机协作设计及支持开放知识工作的AI辅助工具开发提供了启示。

Abstract: This study investigates Wikimedia Commons contributors' lived experiences with the Computer-Aided Tagging (CAT) tool, an AI-assisted image tagging system designed to improve Commons' discoverability, searchability, accessibility, and multilingual support. Using a qualitative analysis of 595 CAT-related community comments from 11 wiki pages and 16 in-depth interviews, we identify seven key issues that contributed to CAT's mixed reception and eventual deactivation. We also offer community-informed suggestions for improving the tool. We reflect on the implications for designing human-AI collaboration on Commons and for developing AI-assisted tools that support open knowledge work. This work contributes to HCI and CSCW research by extending the understanding of human-AI collaboration beyond Anglophone, text-centric, corporate platforms.

</details>


### [214] [What makes an action sequence enjoyable to watch?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30864)
*Jean-Peïc Chou,Kristine Zheng,Junyi Chu,Maneesh Agrawala,Judith E. Fan*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过Flappy Bird风格游戏生成的24个视频片段，研究发现观看者更喜欢玩家完成更难障碍物的视频，而危险程度不影响 enjoyment 评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究为什么有些动作序列比其他的更令人愉悦，即影响观看复杂动作序列（如体育） enjoyment 的因素。

Method: 生成24个Flappy Bird风格游戏视频片段，操纵难度（平均成功率）和不确定性（每步可能失败的概率）。864名参与者对视频在 enjoyment、难度感知或危险感知三个维度之一进行评分。

Result: 参与者更喜欢玩家完成更困难障碍物的视频；危险程度（轨迹的危险性）不能预测 enjoyment 评分。

Conclusion: 程序化生成刺激可有效分离影响动作序列观看 enjoyment 的因素，难度是预测 enjoyment 的关键因素而非危险程度。

Abstract: People often seek out ways to watch others perform complex action sequences (e.g., sports). What makes some sequences more enjoyable to watch than others? We generated 24 video clips of gameplay from a Flappy Bird-style video game. Clips varied in difficulty (how often players succeeded on average) and in moment-to-moment uncertainty (how likely the player was to crash at any given step). Participants (N=864) rated each video on one of three dimensions: how much they enjoyed it, how difficult the level appeared, or how dangerous the player's trajectory appeared. We found that participants preferred videos where the player seemed to be completing more difficult obstacle courses, but dangerousness did not predict enjoyment ratings. These findings show how procedurally generated stimuli can isolate the factors that affect how enjoyable an action sequence is to watch.

</details>


### [215] [TUX: Measuring Human--AI Tacit Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30930)
*Yueshen Li,Hanyi Min,Vedant Das Swain,Koustuv Saha*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过基于Wavelength游戏的频谱定位任务，提出TUX（默契理解指数）来量化人类与LLM之间的隐性对齐，发现人格特征而非随机相似性驱动隐性对齐，且丰富的个体特征能更好预测TUX，但基于profile的条件化方法仍难以捕捉深层次表征对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人机对齐评估多聚焦于显式任务成功、准确率或奖励优化，但许多协作场景依赖于隐性理解——即智能体在没有明确目标、沟通或反馈的情况下，与人类的评价立场或表征先验对齐。本文旨在系统性地量化和研究这种隐性理解能力。

Method: 设计基于社交游戏Wavelength的频谱定位任务，让人类和LLM智能体独立将概念放置在主观测评频谱上。提出TUX作为人机判断相似性的配对度量，收集241名人类参与者和200个profile条件化的LLM智能体（覆盖4种模型）的数据，通过最近邻分析和回归分析进行定量评估。

Result: 在特质空间中，最近的人-智能体配对表现出显著更高的TUX，表明隐性对齐由人格特征结构而非随机相似性驱动。回归分析显示，随着预测变量集更丰富（个体特质、决策风格、置信度），TUX的可解释性增强，优于聚合特质距离基线。

Conclusion: 隐性理解在人机之间是可测量的，但基于profile的条件化方法在捕捉更深层次的表征对齐方面存在局限性。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) increasingly act as collaborative partners, human--AI alignment is often evaluated through explicit task success, accuracy, or reward optimization. Yet many collaborative settings depend on tacit understanding: whether an agent can align with a human's evaluative stance or representational priors without clear objectives, communication, or feedback. To study this capacity, we develop a spectrum-placement task inspired by the social party game Wavelength, in which humans and agents independently place concepts along subjective spectra. We operationalize the Tacit Understanding Index (TUX) as a pairwise measure of similarity between human and agent judgments, and evaluate it with 241 human participants and 200 profile-conditioned LLM agents across four models. We find that nearest human--agent pairs in trait space achieve significantly higher TUX, suggesting that tacit alignment is structured by person-level characteristics rather than random similarity. Regression analyses show that TUX becomes more explainable as predictor sets become richer, with individual traits, decision-making styles, and confidence improving over aggregate trait-distance baselines. These findings suggest that tacit understanding between humans and LLMs is measurable, while revealing the limits of profile-based conditioning for capturing deeper representational alignment.

</details>


### [216] [From Statistics to Individuals: An Exploration of Zoomable Empathic Visualizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31026)
*Edwige Chauvergne,Arnaud Prouzeau,Martin Hachet,Pierre Dragicevic*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出可缩放共情可视化（ZEVs），一种允许用户在抽象统计图表与聚焦个体的定性、可共情表现之间平滑导航的交互式体验。通过三个用例和一项质性用户研究，展示了ZEVs在促进更深度理解和情感参与方面的潜力，同时指出了改进方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据可视化在展示群体统计信息时往往隐藏了个体，难以让观众产生情感共鸣和个体层面的理解。本文旨在弥合抽象统计与个体叙事之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出了ZEVs（Zoomable Empathic Visualizations）交互范式，设计了三个用例，并通过定性用户研究评估其效果。

Result: ZEVs能够促进用户对数据的深度理解和情感参与，但也存在需要改进和进一步完善的方面。

Conclusion: ZEVs为揭示数据背后的个体提供了新的可视化方法方向。

Abstract: Data visualization is a powerful tool for conveying statistical information, but when representing populations, it tends to hide individuals. We introduce Zoomable Empathic Visualizations (ZEVs), interactive experiences allowing users to smoothly navigate between abstract statistical visualizations and more qualitative, relatable representations focused on individuals. We present three use cases of ZEVs and report on a qualitative user study that highlights opportunities for deeper understanding and emotional engagement, while pointing to areas for improvement and further refinement. In summary, ZEVs point toward new approaches for revealing the individuals behind the data.

</details>


### [217] [Extending the UXR Point of View Playbook: Triangulating Insights in Complex Developer Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31104)
*Sarah Kianfar*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文针对用户体验研究（UXR）成熟过程中从数据收集到建立有说服力的观点（POV）的挑战，提出了UXR POV Playbook的扩展方案。基于云开发者工具（涵盖AI Agents、CLI和错误消息）的多方法研究，展示了如何通过定性与定量数据的三角验证来创建高置信度的POV，并引入了三张新的"Playbook卡片"：范式转变、作为信任的可解释性、摩擦成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UXR从业者面临从单纯的数据收集向建立具有战略影响力的观点（POV）转变的挑战，亟需结构化的方法论来支撑这一转型。

Method: 采用多方法研究，在云开发者工具领域（AI Agents、CLI、错误消息）进行定性与定量数据的三角验证，扩展UXR POV Playbook框架。

Result: 提出了三张新的"Playbook Cards"：范式转变、作为信任的可解释性、摩擦成本，为研究者提供了将复杂技术发现转化为商业叙事的结构化机制。

Conclusion: 通过三角验证定性与定量数据可创建高置信度POV，三张新卡片能有效帮助UXR从业者构建具有战略影响力的商业叙事。

Abstract: As User Experience Research (UXR) matures, practitioners face the challenge of moving beyond data collection toward establishing a compelling Point of View (POV) that drives strategic impact. This paper proposes an extension to the UXR POV Playbook, specifically focusing on the transition from the "Insight Generation" layer to the "POV" layer. Drawing on extensive multi-method research in Cloud Developer Tools, spanning AI Agents, Command Line Interfaces (CLI), and Error Messages, we demonstrate how triangulating qualitative and quantitative data facilitates the creation of high-confidence POVs. We introduce three new "Playbook Cards" derived from this research: The Paradigm Shift, Explainability as Trust, and The Cost of Friction. These cards provide a structured mechanism for researchers to translate complex technical findings into irrefutable business narratives.

</details>


### [218] [Generative AI in developing User Experience Research Point of View: A NotebookLM case study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31125)
*Mona Giff,Stephen Giff,Huseyin Dogan*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一种利用Google NotebookLM增强用户体验研究（UXR）观点（PoV）框架的正式方法，该方法包含五个提示词、四个阶段，在11篇UXR论文上测试表明NotebookLM能有效作为协作伙伴辅助PoV创建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UXR正从传统可用性测试向数据驱动和设计主导方法转型，但面临方法论基础薄弱、耗时长且滞后于产品决策周期的身份危机；同时GenAI在UXR中的应用虽被探索，但研究人员常因提示工程、数据清洗和AI输出验证而增加工作强度。

Method: 提出一种正式化的GenAI增强UXR PoV方法，包含五个提示词跨四个阶段：（1）利用框架，（2）建立路线图，（3）应用最佳实践，（4）撰写PoV叙事；在11篇UXR论文上使用Google NotebookLM进行测试评估。

Result: NotebookLM成功地在PoV创建的所有阶段利用了UXR PoV框架，证明了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 只要提供充分的上下文和具体的提示词，NotebookLM可以作为UXR中有效的协作伙伴。

Abstract: User Experience Research (UXR) is currently undergoing a transition from traditional usability testing towards design-led and data-driven approaches, yet it faces an identity crisis due to a lack of methodological grounding in UXR and time-intensive methodologies which often lag behind product decision cycles. To address this, the UXR Point of View (PoV) framework formalises the UXR process by transitioning from raw data collection to forming an evidence-based PoV which drives strategic product impact. Furthermore, the use of GenAI in UXR has been investigated, but researchers often face increased work intensity when using GenAI, attributed to time spent on prompt engineering, data cleaning, and verification of AI outputs. This paper proposes and evaluates a formalised methodology for leveraging GenAI, specifically Google's NotebookLM, to augment the UXR PoV process. The methodology consists of five prompts across four stages: (1) leveraging the framework, (2) establishing roadmaps, (3) applying best-practices, and (4) crafting PoV narratives; and was tested on eleven UXR papers. Results showed that by using the proposed methodology, NotebookLM successfully leveraged the UXR PoV framework across all stages of PoV creation. These findings demonstrate that NotebookLM can serve as an effective collaborative partner in UXR, so long as it is provided with sufficient context and specific prompting.

</details>


### [219] [Extending the UXR Point of View Pyramid: A Generative AI-Augmented Methodology for Human-Centred AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31143)
*Festus Fatai Adedoyin,Huseyin Dogan,Melike Akca,Abiodun Adedeji*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文针对英国债务管理和生活成本压力背景下，扩展了传统的用户体验研究（UXR）观点金字塔（PoV Pyramid），提出一个AI增强的方法论框架，用于高风险金融AI环境中的以人为中心的债务管理技术设计。框架包括AI增强的PoV金字塔、结构化提示架构和AI驱动的Playbook卡片系统，将生成式AI定位为认知支持机制而非分析权威，强调人类验证与合规监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 英国家庭债务上升和生活成本压力加剧，AI驱动的金融科技在信用评估、还款结构和债务支持服务中的角色日益重要。这些系统在监管约束、算法不透明和高脆弱性风险的社会技术环境中运行，但现有的UXR PoV框架并非为AI中介的金融系统设计，无法应对可解释性、公平性和问责性等核心挑战。

Method: 本文扩展了UXR PoV金字塔，提出一个AI增强的方法论框架，包括：(1) AI增强的PoV金字塔，(2) 用于综合和假设生成的结构化提示架构，(3) AI驱动的Playbook卡片系统，将生成式AI嵌入UXR工作流，同时保持可追溯性和伦理监督。

Result: 提出了一个将生成式AI作为认知支持机制而非分析权威的框架，该框架以债务管理技术（包括可负担性评估、还款规划和财务压力预测系统）为应用场景，在高风险金融AI环境中推进了UXR方法论。

Conclusion: 该框架通过将生成式AI定位为受人类验证和监管意识约束的认知支持工具，在保持伦理监督和可追溯性的前提下，为AI驱动的债务管理技术提供了以人为中心的设计方法论，推动了CHI社区中负责任、AI赋能的UXR实践发展。

Abstract: Rising household debt and cost-of-living pressures in the United Kingdom have intensified the role of AI-driven financial technologies in mediating credit assessment, repayment structuring, and debt support services. These systems increasingly shape consequential financial decisions, yet they operate within complex socio-technical environments characterised by regulatory constraint, algorithmic opacity, and heightened vulnerability risk. User Experience Research (UXR) Points of View (PoVs) are critical in translating heterogeneous research evidence into strategic direction for product and governance decisions. However, the existing UXR PoV framework was not designed for AI-mediated financial systems where interpretability, fairness, and accountability are central. This paper extends the UXR PoV pyramid into an AI-augmented methodological framework for Human-Centred AI debt management technologies in the UK financial services context. We formalise (1) an AI-Augmented PoV Pyramid, (2) a structured prompt architecture for synthesis and hypothesis generation, and (3) an AI-enabled Playbook Card system that embeds Generative AI into UXR workflows while preserving traceability and ethical oversight. Generative AI is positioned not as an analytic authority, but as an epistemic support mechanism subject to human validation and regulatory awareness. By grounding the framework in debt management technologies, including affordability assessment, repayment planning, and financial stress prediction systems, this work advances UXR methodology for high-stakes financial AI environments and contributes to the evolution of responsible, AI-powered UXR practice within the CHI community.

</details>


### [220] [From Evidence to Design: Developing an AI-Augmented UX Research Point of View for Digital Wellbeing in Emergency and Public Safety Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31146)
*Olumuyiwa Ayorinde,Huseyin Dogan,Festus Adedoyin,Nan Jiang,Emmanuel Oluokun,Abiodun Adedeji,Melike Akca*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究探讨如何将用户体验研究方法与AI支持的分析相结合，为面向紧急和公共安全人员的数字福祉干预措施制定更清晰的设计方向。通过UXR Point-of-View框架和AI支持的文献分析，识别了重复出现的心理、行为和设计模式，并结合行为改变技术与说服技术原则进行解读，最终产出UXR PoV金字塔、九张UXR游戏卡和利益相关者聚焦的PoV叙事。研究发现有效系统需最小化认知负担、适应操作环境并优先考虑心理安全，展示了AI辅助大规模证据解读的同时人类研究者保留语境判断和设计方向责任的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧急和公共安全人员在高压、轮班制的环境中工作，认知疲劳和不可预测的日程安排导致他们难以坚持使用传统的福祉工具，亟需更贴合其实际需求的数字福祉干预方案。

Method: 采用UXR Point-of-View (PoV)框架，结合AI支持的文献分析流程，识别重复出现的心理、行为和设计模式，并整合行为改变技术（BCT）和说服技术原则进行解释性分析。

Result: 产出了UXR PoV金字塔、九张UXR游戏卡以及面向利益相关者的PoV叙事文本。

Conclusion: 面向EPSP的有效福祉系统必须最小化认知负担、适应操作环境并优先考虑心理安全；AI可辅助大规模证据解读，但人类研究者仍需对语境判断和设计方向负责。

Abstract: This paper investigates how User Experience Research (UXR) methods can be combined with AI-supported analysis to develop clearer design direction for digital wellbeing interventions targeting Emergency and Public Safety Personnel (EPSP). EPSP work in high-stress, shift-based environments where cognitive fatigue and unpredictable schedules reduce engagement with conventional wellbeing tools. Using the UXR Point-of-View (PoV) framework, this study applied an AI-supported literature analysis process to identify recurring psychological, behavioural, and design patterns. Behaviour Change Techniques and Persuasive Technology principles were integrated throughout interpretation to connect evidence with practical design reasoning. The process resulted in a UXR PoV Pyramid, nine UXR Play Cards, and stakeholder focused PoV narratives. Findings show that effective wellbeing systems for EPSP must minimise cognitive effort, adapt to operational context, and prioritise psychological safety. The work demonstrates how AI can assist large-scale evidence interpretation while human researchers maintain responsibility for contextual judgement and design direction.

</details>


### [221] [Developing a Culturally Grounded, AI-Augmented UX Research Point of View (POV): An Exemplar Case Study from Telemedicine Dementia Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31147)
*Abiodun Adedeji,Huseyin Dogan,Festus Adedoyin,Michelle Heward,Melike Akca,Emmanuel Oluwatosin Oluokun,Fatima Ahmad Muhazu,Olumuyiwa Ayorinde*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过尼日利亚痴呆症家庭照护的案例，展示了如何在文化敏感和低资源环境中，结合混合方法研究、假设生成、本体建模和生成式AI来构建可辩护的用户体验研究观点（UXR POV），并提取了可复用的Play Cards以扩展UXR POV Playbook。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管UXR POV在行业实践中广泛使用，但缺乏在文化敏感和低资源环境下如何构建POV的公开示例，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 基于UXR POV Playbook和金字塔框架，结合混合方法研究、假设生成、本体建模，并将生成式AI作为有边界的研究协作者整合到POV框架中。

Result: 成功构建了TeleDeCa框架的文化适应性UXR POV，并提取了可复用的Play Cards和Play，作为CHI 2026研讨会的示范材料。

Conclusion: 证明了在不完全确定系统或验证结果的情况下，结合AI辅助和人类判断可以形成可辩护的UXR POV，且该方法可推广至其他低资源和跨文化场景。

Abstract: User Experience Research (UXR) Points of View (POVs) distil complex and often fragmented research evidence into actionable perspectives that guide how teams interpret user needs, frame design decisions, and align stakeholders. Although POVs are widely used in industry practice, there are few published examples that explicitly document how POVs are constructed, particularly in culturally sensitive and low-resource contexts. This paper presents an exemplar case study demonstrating how a culturally grounded, AI-augmented UXR POV was developed to inform TeleDeCa, a telemedicine dementia care framework for family caregivers in Nigeria.
